1. Introduction

1. 引言


Source: p.3 S001

Original: We introduce Olmo 3, a family of state-of-the-art, fully-open language and thinking models at the 7B and 32B parameter scales with a diverse set of capabilities, including long-context reasoning, function calling, coding, instruction following, general chat, and knowledge recall. The Olmo 3 release provides complete access to its entire model flow—the full lifecycle of a language model, including every stage, checkpoint, datapoint, and dependency required to create it. This enables infinite customization through intervention at any stage of the model development process—not just the final weights.

中文: 我们提出 Olmo 3，一个在 7B 和 32B 参数量级上具备最先进水平、全开放的语言与思考模型家族，能力涵盖长上下文推理、函数调用、编码、指令遵循、通用对话和知识回忆等多元方向。Olmo 3 的发布提供了对其整个模型流程的完整访问——即一个语言模型的全部生命周期，包括创建它所必需的每一个阶段、检查点、数据点和依赖项。这使得可以在模型开发过程的任意阶段介入、实现无限的定制化——而不仅是最终的权重。


Source: p.3 S002

Original: To truly advance open-source AI research and development, we argue that releasing a state-of-the-art language model should make its entire model flow—not just its endpoint—transparent and accessible. With the Olmo 3 release, we provide complete access to the pathways we charted throughout the model flow, from initial conception to the creation of state-of-the-art, fully-open language models.

中文: 要真正推进开源 AI 研究与开发，我们认为发布一个最先进的语言模型，应当让它的整个模型流程——而不只是终点——变得透明且可访问。借助 Olmo 3 的发布，我们提供了对我们在整个模型流程中所绘制路径的完整访问，从最初构想一直到打造出最先进、全开放的语言模型。


Source: p.3 S003

Original: Specifically, we train Olmo 3 Base as a foundation on which to build models with thinking and tool-use capabilities. From Olmo 3 Base we develop our flagship model, Olmo 3 Think, trained to perform step-by-step reasoning by generating intermediate thinking traces before producing a final answer. Olmo 3 Think 32B is the strongest fully-open thinking model, narrowing the gap to the best open-weight models of similar scale, such as the Qwen 3 32B thinking (Yang et al., 2025a) on our suite of reasoning benchmarks, while being trained on six times fewer tokens. Because of our fully-open approach, the Olmo 3 release also enables reasoning chains to be traced back to their original training data, unlocking research opportunities not possible with any other thinking model.

中文: 具体而言，我们训练 Olmo 3 Base 作为基础，在其上构建具备思考与工具使用能力的模型。我们从 Olmo 3 Base 出发，开发了旗舰模型 Olmo 3 Think，它通过在给出最终答案之前生成中间思维轨迹来进行逐步推理。Olmo 3 Think 32B 是最强的全开放思考模型，在我们的一组推理基准上缩小了与同规模最佳开放权重模型（如 Qwen 3 32B 思考模型，Yang et al., 2025a）之间的差距，同时训练所用的令牌数少了六倍。由于我们全开放的做法，Olmo 3 的发布还使得推理链能够追溯回其原始训练数据，解锁了任何其他思考模型都无法实现的研究机会。

Fig. 1. Olmo 3 模型流程：数据、代码与中间检查点

Placed near: p.3 S003
Source: p.3 C001

Original caption: The model flow encompasses training data, code and intermediate checkpoints for all stages of development. While both fully-open and open-weights models release their final checkpoints (dark teal), fully-open releases like Marin, Apertus, and Olmo provide data along their model flow, enabling the careful study of intermediate development stages (beige). Olmo 3 Think 32B is shown here along with other open models of comparable size and architecture. Olmo 3 Think is competitive with Qwen 3 32B, which does not have a released base model. Its underlying Olmo 3 Base 32B surpasses all other fully-open base models.

中文图注: 模型流程涵盖了开发所有阶段的训练数据、代码与中间检查点。虽然全开放和开放权重模型都会发布最终检查点（深青色），但 Marin、Apertus、Olmo 这类全开放发布还会在模型流程中提供数据，从而能够仔细研究中间开发阶段（米色）。图中展示了 Olmo 3 Think 32B 以及其他规模与架构相近的开放模型。Olmo 3 Think 可与 Qwen 3 32B 一较高下，而后者并未发布基座模型。其底层的 Olmo 3 Base 32B 超越了所有其他全开放基座模型。

Reading note: 左半为基座评测平均分、右半为后训练评测平均分；该图传达的核心论点是「全开放（含数据）模型」在中间阶段的可研究性，以及 Olmo 3 Base 32B 的全开放基座领先地位。


Source: p.3 S004

Original: In addition, we train Olmo 3 Instruct 7B and 32B models tuned to produce shorter, more direct responses. By avoiding intermediate “thinking” outputs, Olmo 3 Instruct effectively reduces response latency and is optimized for general chat and function calling. Olmo 3 Instruct 7B and 32B surpass other notable open-weight models of comparable size—Qwen 2.5 (Qwen et al., 2024), Gemma 3 (Gemma 3 Team, 2025), IBM Granite 3.3 (Soule and Bergmann, 2025), and Llama 3 (Grattafiori et al., 2024)—and additionally reduces the remaining performance gap to Qwen 3 (Yang et al., 2025a). Finally, we introduce Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B, a variant of Olmo 3 trained using RL directly from Olmo 3 Base. Olmo 3 RL-Zero enables researchers to study how base model data affects RL performance.

中文: 此外，我们训练了 Olmo 3 Instruct 7B 和 32B 模型，它们被调优为生成更简短、更直接的响应。通过避免中间「思考」输出，Olmo 3 Instruct 有效降低了响应延迟，并针对通用对话与函数调用进行了优化。Olmo 3 Instruct 7B 和 32B 超越了其他同规模的知名开放权重模型——Qwen 2.5（Qwen et al., 2024）、Gemma 3（Gemma 3 Team, 2025）、IBM Granite 3.3（Soule and Bergmann, 2025）和 Llama 3（Grattafiori et al., 2024）——并且进一步缩小了与 Qwen 3（Yang et al., 2025a）之间剩余的差距。最后，我们提出 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B，这是 Olmo 3 的一个变体，直接从 Olmo 3 Base 出发用 RL 训练而成。Olmo 3 RL-Zero 使研究者能够研究基座模型数据如何影响 RL 性能。


Source: p.4 S005

Original: The Olmo 3 family is the strongest collection of fully-open base models, outperforming Stanford Marin (Hall et al., 2025), Apertus (Apertus Team, 2025), and LLM360 K2-V2 (Team et al., 2025). To achieve these results, we construct new datasets for every stage of the model flow. This includes Dolma 3, our pretraining data mix encompassing carefully-sampled natural data from crawled sources, our midtraining mix of high-quality data designed to jump-start reasoning, and a large collection of science-focused PDF documents that unlock long-context support in Olmo 3. We also introduce Dolci, a post-training data suite that advances step-by-step reasoning during supervised finetuning, provides high-quality contrastive data for preference tuning, and offers challenging general and reasoning prompts for reinforcement learning.

中文: Olmo 3 家族是最强的全开放基座模型集合，超越了 Stanford Marin（Hall et al., 2025）、Apertus（Apertus Team, 2025）和 LLM360 K2-V2（Team et al., 2025）。为达成这些结果，我们为模型流程的每一个阶段都构建了新数据集。这包括 Dolma 3（我们的预训练数据混合，涵盖从爬取来源中精心采样的自然数据）、为快速启动推理而设计的高质量中期训练混合数据，以及一大批以科学为主题的 PDF 文档（它们为 Olmo 3 解锁了长上下文支持）。我们还提出 Dolci，一个后训练数据套件，它在监督微调阶段推进逐步推理，为偏好调优提供高质量对比数据，并为强化学习提供具有挑战性的通用与推理提示。


Source: p.4 S006

Original: Finally, we develop a set of new algorithmic and infrastructural advances across data processing, evaluation, pretraining, and reinforcement learning. This includes OlmoBaseEval, a benchmark suite tailored to compute-efficient base-model development, and OlmoRL, a reinforcement-learning framework incorporating efficiency optimizations tailored to our thinking models. Taken together, these training recipes are shaped by a development framework that blends distributed experimentation with centralized evaluation, enabling coordinated, capability-driven improvements throughout the model pipeline.

中文: 最后，我们在数据处理、评测、预训练和强化学习等环节开发了一系列新的算法与基础设施进展。这包括 OlmoBaseEval（一个面向算力高效基座模型开发的基准套件）和 OlmoRL（一个针对我们思考模型、融合了效率优化的强化学习框架）。综合来看，这些训练配方由一个融合了分布式实验与集中式评测的开发框架所塑造，从而在整个模型流水线中实现协调的、以能力为驱动的改进。

2. Model Flow for Olmo 3

2. Olmo 3 的模型流程


Source: p.4 S007

Original: In this section, we provide a brief overview of all components of the model flow for Olmo 3, highlighting our methodology for targeting reasoning and tool-use capabilities in ways that advance beyond OLMo 2 (OLMo et al., 2024) and other open-weight models. Subsequent sections will then provide deep dives into each of the model flow components. Olmo 3 training is divided into major stages of base model training and post-training, each further divided into sub-stages as outlined in Figure 2.

中文: 在本节中，我们简要概述 Olmo 3 模型流程的所有组成部分，突出我们针对推理与工具使用能力所采用的方法，其方式超越了 OLMo 2（OLMo et al., 2024）及其他开放权重模型。后续各节将对模型流程的每一组成部分进行深入探讨。Olmo 3 的训练分为基座模型训练与后训练两大阶段，每一阶段又进一步细分为若干子阶段，如图 2 所示。

Fig. 2. Olmo 3 模型流程示意图

Placed near: p.4 S007
Source: p.4 C002

Original caption: Depiction of model flow for Olmo 3. Development is divided into major base model training (left) and post-training (right) stages, each further divided into sub-stages with their own recipes (i.e., training data and method).

中文图注: Olmo 3 模型流程的示意。开发分为基座模型训练（左）与后训练（右）两大阶段，每一阶段又细分为具有各自配方（即训练数据与方法）的子阶段。

Reading note: 该图是全文的总览图——左为 Olmo 3 Base 的预训练→中期训练→长上下文扩展；右为 Think / Instruct / RL-Zero 三条后训练支线（SFT→DPO→RLVR）。

2.1 Base Model Training

2.1 基座模型训练


Source: p.4 S008

Original: We develop Olmo 3 Base in three stages of pretraining for up to 5.9T tokens (Section §3.4), midtraining for 100 billion tokens (Section §3.5), and the newly added long-context extension for 50B (Olmo 3 Base 7B) or 100B (Olmo 3 Base 32B) tokens (Section §3.6).

中文: 我们分三个阶段开发 Olmo 3 Base：最多 5.9T 令牌的预训练（第 §3.4 节）、1000 亿令牌的中期训练（第 §3.5 节），以及新加入的、50B（Olmo 3 Base 7B）或 100B（Olmo 3 Base 32B）令牌的长上下文扩展（第 §3.6 节）。


Source: p.4 S009

Original: Evaluation. We develop OlmoBaseEval, a collection of benchmarking suites to support decision-making during base model development (pretraining and midtraining). Our goal is to be compute-efficient by making development decisions based on models trained at a small scale. The challenge is that such models can exhibit random-chance performance on certain tasks, and have small differences in scores that are hard to distinguish from benchmark noise. To address this, we (1) aggregate scores over clusters of tasks that assess similar capabilities (Section §3.3.1); (2) develop proxy metrics for evaluating small-scale models (Section §3.3.2); and (3) improve overall signal-to-noise ratio by evaluating on more examples from noisy tasks or even removing them entirely (Section §3.3.3).

中文: 评测。我们开发了 OlmoBaseEval，一套基准测试套件，用于在基座模型开发（预训练与中期训练）过程中支持决策。我们的目标是通过基于小规模训练出的模型来做开发决策，从而实现算力高效。难点在于，这类模型在某些任务上可能表现出随机水平，且分数差异很小，难以与基准噪声区分。为此，我们（1）对评估相似能力的一簇任务聚合分数（第 §3.3.1 节）；（2）开发用于评测小规模模型的代理指标（第 §3.3.2 节）；（3）通过对噪声较大的任务评估更多样例、甚至完全移除它们，来提高整体信噪比（第 §3.3.3 节）。


Source: p.5 S010

Original: Data curriculum. We curate specialized datasets for each training stage, with latter stages focused on strengthening capabilities crucial in post-training stages, such as math, code, reasoning, instruction following, and long-context understanding:

中文: 数据课程。我们为每个训练阶段精心构建专门的数据集，靠后的阶段专注于强化那些对后训练阶段至关重要的能力，例如数学、代码、推理、指令遵循和长上下文理解：


Source: p.5 S011

Original: Pretraining — We first train Olmo 3 Base on Dolma 3 Mix (Section §3.4), our 6T-token pretraining data mix. While Dolma 3 Mix is largely comprised of the same types of data sources used in other open pretraining recipes (Soldaini et al., 2024; Bakouch et al., 2025; OLMo et al., 2024), we demonstrate three key novelties: (i) New tooling for fast and scalable global deduplication at the trillion-token scale; (ii) A novel source of academic PDFs—olmOCR science PDFs—converted to linearized plain text using olmOCR (Poznanski et al., 2025a,b); (iii) Two new methods for optimizing selection of training tokens: token-constrained mixing and quality-aware upsampling.

中文: 预训练——我们首先在 Dolma 3 Mix（第 §3.4 节）上训练 Olmo 3 Base，这是我们 6T 令牌的预训练数据混合。虽然 Dolma 3 Mix 大部分由与其他开放预训练配方相同类型的数据源组成（Soldaini et al., 2024; Bakouch et al., 2025; OLMo et al., 2024），但我们展示了三项关键创新：（i）在万亿令牌规模上实现快速、可扩展全局去重的新工具；（ii）一种新的学术 PDF 来源——olmOCR 科学 PDF——使用 olmOCR 转换为线性化纯文本（Poznanski et al., 2025a,b）；（iii）两种优化训练令牌选择的新方法：令牌约束混合（token-constrained mixing）与质量感知上采样（quality-aware upsampling）。


Source: p.5 S012

Original: Midtraining — We continue training on Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix (Section §3.5), our 100B-token data curated to boost target capabilities across code, math, and general knowledge QA domains through the introduction of: (i) a new two-part methodological framework combining 1) lightweight, distributed feedback loops on individual data sources, with 2) centralized integration tests to assess candidate mixes on base model quality and post-trainability; (ii) intentional inclusion of instruction data and thinking traces to lay groundwork for post-training.

中文: 中期训练——我们继续在 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix（第 §3.5 节）上训练，这是我们 100B 令牌的数据集，通过引入以下内容来增强代码、数学和通用知识问答领域的目标能力：（i）一个新的两部分方法学框架，将 1）针对单个数据源的轻量级分布式反馈回路，与 2）集中式集成测试（用于评估候选混合在基座模型质量与后训练能力上的表现）结合起来；（ii）有意识地纳入指令数据与思维轨迹，为后训练打下基础。


Source: p.5 S013

Original: Long-context extension — Through Dolma 3 Longmino Mix (Section §3.6), Olmo 3 supports long-context input and output, a crucial feature to unlock reasoning and tool-use capabilities. Documents in olmOCR science PDFs enable our long-context approach; with over 22.3M documents of length above 8K tokens (640B tokens total), and 4.5M documents over 32K tokens (380B tokens total), this collection is the largest openly available for long-context research. As a result, Olmo 3 is our first model with long-context capabilities, supporting up to 65K context after extension. Olmo 3 Base 32B rivals performance of Qwen 2.5 32B, Mistral Small 3.1 24B, and Gemma 3 27B on long-context benchmarks, despite a short extension stage (50B for 7B, 100B for 32B).

中文: 长上下文扩展——通过 Dolma 3 Longmino Mix（第 §3.6 节），Olmo 3 支持长上下文输入与输出，这是解锁推理与工具使用能力的关键特性。olmOCR 科学 PDF 中的文档支撑了我们的长上下文方法；拥有超过 2230 万篇长度在 8K 令牌以上的文档（合计 640B 令牌），以及 450 万篇超过 32K 令牌的文档（合计 380B 令牌），该集合是当前公开可用的最大规模长上下文研究资源。因此，Olmo 3 是我们第一个具备长上下文能力的模型，扩展后支持最高 65K 上下文。Olmo 3 Base 32B 在长上下文基准上的表现可与 Qwen 2.5 32B、Mistral Small 3.1 24B 和 Gemma 3 27B 相媲美，尽管其扩展阶段很短（7B 为 50B，32B 为 100B）。


Source: p.5 S014

Original: Open artifacts. We release all of our intermediate checkpoints as well as the final models at the end of each stage of training. For data, we release both our data mixes, which are the actual tokens used for base model training, as well as our full source data pools for each stage—9T tokens of cleaned source tokens for pretraining, and 2T and 640B tokens of specialized data for midtraining and long-context extension respectively. For pretraining, in addition to our actual training mix for Olmo 3 Base, we also release smaller sample mixes for accessible experimentation with less compute (150B for pretraining and 10B for midtraining).

中文: 开放产物。我们发布所有中间检查点，以及每个训练阶段结束时的最终模型。在数据方面，我们既发布数据混合（即基座模型训练实际使用的令牌），也发布每个阶段的完整源数据池——预训练为 9T 令牌的清洗后源令牌，中期训练与长上下文扩展分别为 2T 与 640B 令牌的专门数据。对于预训练，除了 Olmo 3 Base 实际使用的训练混合之外，我们还发布了更小的样本混合，以便用更少的算力进行可及性实验（预训练 150B、中期训练 10B）。

2.2 Post-training

2.2 后训练


Source: p.5 S015

Original: We post-train Olmo 3 Base into three model variants:

中文: 我们将 Olmo 3 Base 后训练为三种模型变体：


Source: p.5 S016

Original: Olmo 3 Think (Section §4) is trained to perform extended reasoning by generating a structured thinking trace before a final answer. We train it via SFT, DPO, and RLVR, observing gains at each stage. We introduce Dolci Think SFT (Section §4.2), Dolci Think DPO (Section §4.3), and Dolci Think RL (Section §4.4), new post-training datasets designed to target a broad range of key capabilities such as math, coding, instruction following, and general conversation. The dataset includes synthetic examples with long thinking traces for supervised finetuning, high-quality contrastive data following the insights from Delta Learning (Geng et al., 2025), and challenging prompts for reinforcement learning across both verifiable and non-verifiable domains. In particular, our new approach to curating contrastive instances for preference tuning expands the reasoning frontier of the model beyond what SFT alone can provide and primes the model for effective reinforcement learning.

中文: Olmo 3 Think（第 §4 节）被训练为通过在给出最终答案之前生成结构化的思维轨迹来进行扩展推理。我们通过 SFT、DPO 和 RLVR 训练它，并在每个阶段都观察到收益。我们提出 Dolci Think SFT（第 §4.2 节）、Dolci Think DPO（第 §4.3 节）和 Dolci Think RL（第 §4.4 节），这些是新的后训练数据集，旨在覆盖广泛的关键能力，如数学、编码、指令遵循和通用对话。该数据集包括用于监督微调的、带长思维轨迹的合成示例；遵循 Delta Learning（Geng et al., 2025）洞见的高质量对比数据；以及用于强化学习的、覆盖可验证与不可验证领域的挑战性提示。特别是，我们为偏好调优而构造对比样本的新方法，将模型的推理前沿扩展到了 SFT 单独所能提供的范围之外，并为有效的强化学习做好了铺垫。


Source: p.6 S017

Original: We introduce algorithmic and infrastructural advances in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (Section §4.4). This approach generalizes verifiable reasoning to multiple domains, expanding beyond the settings explored in OLMo 2 to include code and general chat. Our improvements enable longer and more stable RL runs across diverse domains and increase the overall efficiency of training cycles, leading to a 4x speedup in RL training. Notably, we introduce Olmo 3.1 Think 32B to illustrate that extended OlmoRL training leads to improved performance.

中文: 我们在可验证奖励强化学习（第 §4.4 节）中引入算法与基础设施层面的进展。该方法将可验证推理推广到多个领域，扩展了 OLMo 2 所探索的设置范围，纳入了代码和通用对话。我们的改进使得跨多种领域的 RL 训练能够更长久、更稳定地进行，并提高了训练循环的整体效率，使 RL 训练速度提升 4 倍。值得注意的是，我们提出 Olmo 3.1 Think 32B 来说明，延长 OlmoRL 训练会带来性能的提升。


Source: p.6 S018

Original: Olmo 3 Instruct (Section §5) is trained to produce efficient and helpful responses to user queries without generating internal thinking traces. This model prioritizes typical user needs, such as avoiding excessive verbosity for easy user understanding and function-calling for user information seeking. In such settings, thinking traces are unnecessary, and inference-time efficiency matters more than inference-time scaling. We introduce Dolci Instruct SFT, our new dataset enriched with data specifically created for function calling (Section §5.2.1). To directly optimize model interactivity on top of capabilities, we extend our Delta Learning preference pipeline in Dolci Instruct DPO, incorporating multi-turn preference data and targeted data length interventions that encourage concise responses (Section §5.3.1). Finally, we use reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (Section §5.4) to further refine core capabilities, where preference tuning synergizes with RL to improve model performance while maintaining learned brevity.

中文: Olmo 3 Instruct（第 §5 节）被训练为对用户查询生成高效且有用的响应，而不生成内部思维轨迹。该模型优先考虑典型用户需求，例如避免过度冗长以方便用户理解，以及通过函数调用帮助用户获取信息。在这些场景下，思维轨迹并非必需，推理时的效率比推理时的扩展更重要。我们提出 Dolci Instruct SFT，这是我们的新数据集，富含专门为函数调用创建的数据（第 §5.2.1 节）。为了在能力之上直接优化模型交互性，我们在 Dolci Instruct DPO 中扩展了 Delta Learning 偏好流水线，纳入多轮偏好数据以及有针对性的数据长度干预，以鼓励简洁响应（第 §5.3.1 节）。最后，我们使用可验证奖励强化学习（第 §5.4 节）进一步精炼核心能力，其中偏好调优与 RL 协同作用，在提升模型性能的同时保持习得的简洁性。


Source: p.6 S019

Original: Olmo 3 RL-Zero (Section §6). To date, all leading open RLVR benchmarks and algorithms train on top of open-weight models that do not reveal their pretraining or mid-training data (Chu et al., 2025; Yang et al., 2025a). This limits the community’s ability to study the role of pretraining data on RLVR performance. It can lead to myriad issues with benchmark evaluations being contaminated, e.g., mid-training data containing the evaluation, which makes spurious rewards as effective as true reward (Shao et al., 2025b; Wu et al., 2025c), or improvements from fixing prompt templates outweighing the improvements from RL (Liu et al., 2025b). We therefore release a fully open dataset Dolci RL-Zero, an algorithmic RL zero setup for Olmo 3, and open-source OlmoRL code to enable clear benchmarking in the RL research community. We perform RLVR from Olmo 3 Base over four benchmarking domains to create the Olmo 3 RL-Zero family: math, code, precise instruction following (IF) and a general mix. In all cases, we further decontaminate Dolci RL-Zero from pretraining and midtraining data to guarantee our setup carefully studies the effect of RLVR without data leakage confounding our conclusions.

中文: Olmo 3 RL-Zero（第 §6 节）。迄今为止，所有领先的开放 RLVR 基准与算法都建立在那些不公开其预训练或中期训练数据的开放权重模型之上（Chu et al., 2025; Yang et al., 2025a）。这限制了社区研究预训练数据对 RLVR 性能影响的能力。它还可能导致基准评测被污染等一系列问题，例如：中期训练数据中包含评测内容，使得虚假奖励与真实奖励同样有效（Shao et al., 2025b; Wu et al., 2025c）；或者修复提示模板所带来的提升超过了 RL 本身的提升（Liu et al., 2025b）。因此，我们发布一个完全开放的数据集 Dolci RL-Zero、一套面向 Olmo 3 的算法化 RL zero 设置，并开源 OlmoRL 代码，以便在 RL 研究社区中实现清晰的基准评测。我们在四个基准领域上从 Olmo 3 Base 执行 RLVR，构建 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 家族：数学、代码、精确指令遵循（IF）以及一个通用混合。在所有情况下，我们都进一步将 Dolci RL-Zero 与预训练和中期训练数据去污染，以确保我们的设置能够在不被数据泄漏干扰结论的前提下，仔细研究 RLVR 的效果。

It is better than Qwen2.5-Instruct, Gemma 2 and 3 27B, DeepSeek R1, and Distilled Qwen 32B; it is also close to Qwen 3 and Qwen 3 VL 32B models, narrowing the gap to the best open-weight models of similar scale while training on roughly 6x fewer tokens.

2.4 Costs

2.4 成本


Source: p.6 S022

Original: The cost of training large models is often reported as a single dollar figure, typically by converting GPU-hours at market rates to dollars, such as $5.576M in H800-hours for DeepSeek V3 (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025). To provide a more representative view of the resources required to train Olmo 3 32B, we instead report the wall-clock time that elapses during training.

中文: 训练大型模型的成本通常被报告为一个单一的美元数字，一般做法是把 GPU 时按市场价换算成美元，例如 DeepSeek V3 的 H800 时约为 557.6 万美元（DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025）。为了更真实地反映训练 Olmo 3 32B 所需的资源，我们改为报告训练过程中实际流逝的墙钟时间。


Source: p.7 S023

Original: In total, approximately 56 days elapsed from the start of training to the evaluation of the Olmo 3 Think 32B checkpoint, on a cluster with 1024 H100 GPUs dedicated to Olmo 3. The 32B 3.1 Think and Instruct checkpoints were trained after this time period. This training time is largely a reflection of applying our best recipe to the model, and does not include any substantial modifications or research ideas that could expand the timeline substantially. At a price of $2/H100 hour, this would cost $2.75M. Runtime breakdown is as follows:

中文: 总体而言，从训练开始到 Olmo 3 Think 32B 检查点完成评测，大约过去了 56 天，所用集群拥有 1024 块专用于 Olmo 3 的 H100 GPU。32B 的 3.1 Think 与 Instruct 检查点是在此时间段之后训练的。这一训练时间大体上反映的是将我们最佳配方应用到模型上的耗时，不包含任何可能大幅延长进度的实质性修改或研究思路。按每小时 H100 2 美元的价格计算，这大约花费 275 万美元。运行时间拆解如下：


Source: p.7 S024

Original: Pretraining: ~47 days (including midtraining and long-context stages). The initial pretraining phase on 5.5T tokens took about 9.5 days on 512 GPUs, followed by an additional 35 days on 1024 GPUs. These durations include all crash resumptions and other engineering concerns that kept us from running at full speed. Midtraining consisted of two parallel runs on 512 GPUs each, covering 100B tokens per run, followed by model merging and evaluations to decide on final checkpoints, taking about 1.5 days in total. Long-context extension was executed as a single run on 1024 GPUs; the full long-context stage—including training and all associated merges and evaluations—added approximately one additional day.

中文: 预训练：约 47 天（含中期训练与长上下文阶段）。最初在 5.5T 令牌上的预训练阶段在 512 块 GPU 上耗时约 9.5 天，随后在 1024 块 GPU 上又用了 35 天。这些时长包含了所有崩溃恢复以及其他使我们无法全速运行的工程问题。中期训练由两个各用 512 块 GPU 的并行运行组成，每次运行覆盖 100B 令牌，随后进行模型合并与评测以确定最终检查点，总共约 1.5 天。长上下文扩展在 1024 块 GPU 上以单次运行执行；整个长上下文阶段——包括训练及所有相关的合并与评测——又增加了大约一天。


Source: p.7 S025

Original: Post-training: ~9 days (SFT, DPO, and RL). Post-training follows a different operational pattern in which we run each stage multiple times, sweeping over learning rates and other hyperparameters. The theory for post-training, particularly RL, is less developed, so we have to run multiple experiments to identify the optimal hyperparameters for a given base model. We hope to address this in future work. During post-training, checkpoint evaluation consumes a larger proportion of compute resources, in part due to long generations from reasoning models on core benchmarks. For SFT, we swept over four candidate learning rates, on 256 GPUs each, in parallel for 36 hours. Then approximately 12 hours was spent on evaluation, merging, and checkpoint confirmation, totaling approximately two days. DPO training takes less time per run (about 18 hours for a full learning-rate sweep on 64 GPUs per job) but in practice extended over multiple days due to cluster instability. The final RL runs for the initial Olmo 3 Think 32B spanned approximately 5 days with at least a day of training time lost due to stability issues. After the initial release of Olmo 3, we continued our best RL run for another 21 days on 224 GPUs to produce Olmo 3.1 Think 32B.

中文: 后训练：约 9 天（SFT、DPO 与 RL）。后训练遵循不同的运行模式，我们对每个阶段运行多次，对学习率及其他超参数进行扫描。后训练（尤其是 RL）的理论尚不成熟，因此我们必须运行多个实验来确定给定基座模型的最优超参数。我们希望在未来工作中解决这一点。在后训练期间，检查点评测占用了更大比例的算力资源，部分原因是推理模型在核心基准上会生成很长的输出。对于 SFT，我们在 4 个候选学习率上，每个用 256 块 GPU 并行扫描了 36 小时；随后约 12 小时用于评测、合并与检查点确认，合计约两天。DPO 训练每次运行耗时较少（每个任务在 64 块 GPU 上做一次完整学习率扫描约 18 小时），但实际由于集群不稳定而延长到了好几天。最初 Olmo 3 Think 32B 的最终 RL 运行跨度约 5 天，其中至少有一天因稳定性问题而损失了训练时间。在 Olmo 3 首次发布之后，我们又用 224 块 GPU 将最佳 RL 运行延续了 21 天，从而产出 Olmo 3.1 Think 32B。


Source: p.7 S026

Original: While pretraining accounts for the majority of total GPU hours, a non-trivial share is consumed by post-training.

中文: 虽然预训练占据了总 GPU 时数的大部分，但后训练也消耗了不可忽视的份额。

3. Olmo 3 Base


Source: p.8 S027

Original: The goal of Olmo 3 Base is to establish a strong foundation that supports a diversity of general capabilities while enabling downstream capabilities like thinking, tool-use, and instruction-following to be easily elicited during post-training. In this section, we describe our recipe for Olmo 3 Base, organized as follows:

中文: Olmo 3 Base 的目标是建立坚实的基座，既能支撑多元的通用能力，又能让思考、工具使用和指令遵循等下游能力在后训练阶段被轻松激发。在本节中，我们按如下结构描述 Olmo 3 Base 的配方：

Original: Modeling (Section §3.2) — Olmo 3 Base closely follows OLMo 2 in that it is a dense model at 7B and 32B sizes, with largely identical hyperparameters. Apart from engineering improvements that enable better training throughput, we focus on enabling a larger context window. We lay out the details in Section §3.2. Evaluation (Section §3.3) — To guard against overfitting Olmo 3 Base to any one capability, we greatly expand on our evaluation suite from OLMo 2 to include more benchmarks. We make small-scale experiments more reliable by systematically refining benchmark selection and usage throughout development. Data — We introduce Dolma 3, a collection of data to support multiple stages of base model development: Pretraining (Section §3.4) — We train on Dolma 3 Mix, a mix of 5.9T tokens of diverse, natural data including sources like web pages, academic PDFs, code repositories, and more. Midtraining (Section §3.5) — We train on Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix, a mix of 100B tokens combining our highest-quality pretraining data with substantial task data for math and code problems, general knowledge QA, instruction following, and more. Long-context extension (Section §3.6) — We train on Dolma 3 Longmino Mix, a mix of 50B (Olmo 3 Base 7B) or 100B (Olmo 3 Base 32B) tokens combining long documents with our midtraining data.

中文: 建模（第 §3.2 节）——Olmo 3 Base 紧密遵循 OLMo 2，是一个 7B 与 32B 规模的稠密模型，超参数基本一致。除了实现更好训练吞吐的工程改进外，我们专注于支持更大的上下文窗口。细节见第 §3.2 节。评测（第 §3.3 节）——为了防止 Olmo 3 Base 对某一能力过拟合，我们在 OLMo 2 的评测套件基础上大幅扩展，纳入更多基准。通过在开发过程中系统性地优化基准的选择与使用，我们让大规模小模型实验更加可靠。数据——我们提出 Dolma 3，一组支持基座模型多个开发阶段的数据：预训练（第 §3.4 节）——我们在 Dolma 3 Mix 上训练，它是 5.9T 令牌的多样化自然数据混合，涵盖网页、学术 PDF、代码仓库等来源。中期训练（第 §3.5 节）——我们在 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix 上训练，它是 100B 令牌的混合，将我们最高质量的预训练数据与大量面向数学与代码问题、通用知识问答、指令遵循等的任务数据相结合。长上下文扩展（第 §3.6 节）——我们在 Dolma 3 Longmino Mix 上训练，它是 50B（Olmo 3 Base 7B）或 100B（Olmo 3 Base 32B）令牌的混合，将长文档与我们的中期训练数据相结合。

3.1 Main Results for Olmo 3 Base

3.1 Olmo 3 Base 的主要结果


Source: p.8 S028

Original: Tables 2 and 3 compare Olmo 3 Base 32B and 7B with leading fully-open and open-weights base models, demonstrating both the effectiveness of our evaluation design and the strong performance of Olmo 3 Base across a broad set of capabilities. Olmo 3 Base is the best fully-open model at 32B parameters, outperforming Stanford Marin 32B and Apertus 70B. On Math and Code evaluation composites, it achieves double-digit improvements over the other fully-open 32B models and is within a few points of strong open-weight baselines. On MCQA benchmarks, its STEM and Non-STEM scores closely track Marin 32B and OLMo 2 32B and sit a few points behind the top open-weight models, while on GenQA Olmo 3 Base forms the top fully-open cluster with Marin 32B and OLMo 2 32B and is only narrowly behind Llama 3.1 70B among the open-weight baselines.

中文: 表 2 和表 3 将 Olmo 3 Base 32B 和 7B 与领先的全开放及开放权重基座模型进行比较，既展示了我们评测设计的有效性，也展示了 Olmo 3 Base 在广泛能力上的强劲表现。Olmo 3 Base 是 32B 参数量级上最好的全开放模型，超越了 Stanford Marin 32B 和 Apertus 70B。在数学与代码评测综合项上，它相比其他全开放 32B 模型取得了两位数的提升，并且与强劲的开放权重基线仅差几个百分点。在 MCQA 基准上，其 STEM 与 Non-STEM 得分与 Marin 32B 和 OLMo 2 32B 紧密一致，仅比顶尖开放权重模型落后几个百分点；而在 GenQA 上，Olmo 3 Base 与 Marin 32B、OLMo 2 32B 共同构成全开放模型的第一梯队，在开放权重基线中仅以微弱差距落后于 Llama 3.1 70B。

Original: At the 7B scale, Olmo 3 Base achieves the strongest Math and Code performance among fully-open models, with sizable margins over Marin 8B, Apertus 8B, and OLMo 2 7B. Compared to open-weight models, it trails only the strongest models such as Qwen and Nemotron Nano on Math and Code. In MCQA, Olmo 3 Base 7B is on par with the strongest fully-open models in both STEM and Non-STEM areas. Finally, on GenQA tasks, Olmo 3 Base outperforms all but Marin among listed fully-open models, and outperforms all but the larger Gemma 2 9B and Llama3.1 8B among listed open-weight models.

中文: 在 7B 规模上，Olmo 3 Base 在全开放模型中取得了最强的数学与代码性能，对 Marin 8B、Apertus 8B 和 OLMo 2 7B 有相当大的领先优势。与开放权重模型相比，它仅在数学与代码上落后于 Qwen 和 Nemotron Nano 等最强模型。在 MCQA 上，Olmo 3 Base 7B 在 STEM 与 Non-STEM 两个领域都与最强的全开放模型持平。最后，在 GenQA 任务上，Olmo 3 Base 在所列举的全开放模型中仅落后于 Marin，在所列举的开放权重模型中仅落后于规模更大的 Gemma 2 9B 和 Llama3.1 8B。

Table 3. Olmo 3 Base 7B 与其他基座模型的对比（OlmoBaseEval Main 套件）

Placed near: p.8 S029
Source: p.10 C005

Original caption: Results comparing Olmo 3 Base 7B to other base models using the OlmoBaseEval Main suite (details in §3.3). Olmo 3 was not evaluated on held-out benchmarks prior to release.

中文表注: 使用 OlmoBaseEval Main 套件将 Olmo 3 Base 7B 与其他基座模型对比的结果（细节见 §3.3）。Olmo 3 在发布前未在留出基准上评测。

Reading note: 结构与表 2 一致；Olmo 3 7B 在 OlmoBaseEval Math 上达 54.7、Code 达 40.0，为 7B/8B 规模全开放模型中的最佳。

3.2 Modeling and Architecture

3.2 建模与架构


Source: p.8 S030

Original: Olmo 3 modeling and training largely follows that of OLMo 2. We focus this section on the key differences and refer to the appendix for further details. Architecture — We adopt a decoder-only transformer architecture based on Vaswani et al. (2017). Details of the architecture are presented in Table 33 in Appendix A.2. Compared to OLMo 2: We train with a context window of 8192 tokens (increased from 4096 tokens for OLMo 2) during pretraining and midtraining stages.

中文: Olmo 3 的建模与训练大体遵循 OLMo 2。本节聚焦于关键差异，更多细节见附录。架构——我们采用基于 Vaswani et al. (2017) 的纯解码器 transformer 架构。架构细节见附录 A.2 的表 33。与 OLMo 2 相比：我们在预训练与中期训练阶段使用 8192 令牌的上下文窗口（由 OLMo 2 的 4096 令牌增加而来）。


Source: p.9 S031

Original: To support scalable pretraining at longer sequence lengths, and to keep inference costs manageable, we introduce a sliding window attention (SWA) pattern (Beltagy et al., 2020) in which each token can attend to previous tokens in a window of size 4096. We add SWA at three out of every four layers, and ensure that the last layer always uses full attention.

中文: 为了支持在更长序列长度上的可扩展预训练，同时控制推理成本，我们引入滑动窗口注意力（SWA）模式（Beltagy et al., 2020），其中每个令牌可以关注窗口中大小为 4096 的前序令牌。我们在每四层中的三层加入 SWA，并确保最后一层始终使用全注意力。


Source: p.9 S032

Original: Training — Olmo 3 Base is trained using the OLMo-core codebase. With this stack, we train the 7B model at 7700 tokens per second per GPU and the 32B model at 1960 tokens per second per GPU at a sequence length of 8192, using bfloat16 precision throughout. This corresponds to roughly 43% and 41% MFU, respectively. We achieve this performance by combining PyTorch’s built-in torch.compile(), custom kernels for operations such as attention (Dao, 2024) and the language modeling head (Hsu et al., 2025), asynchronous and batched gathering of metrics, and asynchronous checkpoint writing, among other optimizations. OLMo-core supports pretraining, midtraining, long-context extension, and SFT, along with auxiliary tools for checkpoint conversion to and from Hugging Face Transformers format and for merging model checkpoints. Support for DPO and RL is planned but not yet complete.

中文: 训练——Olmo 3 Base 使用 OLMo-core 代码库进行训练。借助这套技术栈，我们在 8192 序列长度、全程 bfloat16 精度下，以每 GPU 每秒 7700 令牌的速度训练 7B 模型、以每 GPU 每秒 1960 令牌的速度训练 32B 模型。这分别对应约 43% 和 41% 的 MFU。我们通过结合 PyTorch 内置的 torch.compile()、针对注意力（Dao, 2024）和语言建模头（Hsu et al., 2025）等操作的定制内核、异步批量采集指标、以及异步检查点写入等优化来达成这一性能。OLMo-core 支持预训练、中期训练、长上下文扩展和 SFT，并提供检查点与 Hugging Face Transformers 格式互转、以及合并模型检查点的辅助工具。对 DPO 和 RL 的支持尚在规划中，尚未完成。


Source: p.9 S033

Original: Hyperparameters for training Olmo 3 Base 7B and 32B are presented in Table 35 in Appendix A.2. As in OLMo 2, we train in stages defined by the data curriculum and learning rate schedule (see Appendix Table 35 for details). Infrastructure and distributed training configurations for each stage are summarized in Appendix Table 34.

中文: 训练 Olmo 3 Base 7B 和 32B 的超参数见附录 A.2 的表 35。与 OLMo 2 一样，我们按数据课程与学习率调度所定义的阶段进行训练（细节见附录表 35）。每个阶段的基础设施与分布式训练配置总结于附录表 34。


Source: p.10 S034

Original: Tokenizer — We process data for each stage using the same tokenizer as OLMo 2, which is derived from OpenAI’s cl100k (OpenAI, 2023a,b).

中文: 分词器——我们在每个阶段使用与 OLMo 2 相同的分词器处理数据，该分词器源自 OpenAI 的 cl100k（OpenAI, 2023a,b）。

3.3 Experimental Design and Evaluation

3.3 实验设计与评测


Source: p.10 S035

Original: Model development requires many iterative data and training decisions. However, benchmarks are not perfect decision-making tools: different evaluations are only sensitive for making development decisions across specific ranges of scale and capability (Magnusson et al., 2025). Models trained at small compute scales are known to exhibit random-chance performance on math, code, and multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) tasks (Wei et al., 2022; Gu et al., 2024b), and benchmark noise can reduce the ability to trust small differences in scores (Heineman et al., 2025). To address these problems, we develop OlmoBaseEval, a collection of benchmark suites to support decision-making during base model development. OlmoBaseEval features the following improvements:

中文: 模型开发需要大量迭代式的数据与训练决策。然而，基准并不是完美的决策工具：不同的评测只在特定的规模与能力范围内才足以支撑开发决策（Magnusson et al., 2025）。以较小算力规模训练的模型，已知在数学、代码和多项选择问答（MCQA）任务上会表现出随机水平（Wei et al., 2022; Gu et al., 2024b），而基准噪声会削弱人们对分数微小差异的信任（Heineman et al., 2025）。为解决这些问题，我们开发了 OlmoBaseEval，一套用于支撑基座模型开发决策的基准套件。OlmoBaseEval 具备以下改进：


Source: p.11 S036

Original: We aggregate scores over task clusters that group benchmarks by assessed capability (Section §3.3.1); we develop proxy metrics for evaluating small-scale models by identifying when capabilities “emerge” during training (Section §3.3.2); and we improve the overall signal-to-noise ratio by evaluating more examples from noisy tasks or even removing them entirely (Section §3.3.3). We start by targeting a high coverage of capabilities; we select benchmarks to prioritize science knowledge, medical/lab knowledge, math, and code tasks. Because our data interventions are targeted to a core capability rather than a specific benchmark (e.g., “Code” rather than “DS-1000”), we group tasks into clusters, where we expect the benchmarks within a cluster to behave similarly to particular data changes. To handle evaluation of models trained using small compute budgets (e.g., up to our largest experiment scale of 1B parameters at 100B tokens), we perform a scaling analysis to determine which benchmarks show signal at a small scale and find proxy metrics which we use to make decisions. Finally, we analyze the signal-to-noise ratio of each benchmark—we select benchmark metrics to improve SNR, remove benchmarks that were too noisy for making decisions, and move benchmarks out of the average if the noise of one particular benchmark dominated the aggregate scores.

中文: 我们对按所评测能力分组的任务簇聚合分数（第 §3.3.1 节）；我们通过识别能力在训练中何时「涌现」来开发用于评测小规模模型的代理指标（第 §3.3.2 节）；我们通过对噪声较大的任务评测更多样例、甚至完全移除它们，来改进整体信噪比（第 §3.3.3 节）。我们首先追求高能力覆盖；选择基准时优先考虑科学知识、医学/实验知识、数学和代码任务。由于我们的数据干预针对的是核心能力而非某个具体基准（例如「代码」而非「DS-1000」），我们将任务分组为簇，并期望同一簇内的基准对特定数据变化表现出相似的反应。为了处理用小算力预算训练出的模型的评测（例如，最高到我们 1B 参数、100B 令牌的最大实验规模），我们进行缩放分析以确定哪些基准在小规模下能显示信号，并找到用于决策的代理指标。最后，我们分析每个基准的信噪比——选择基准指标以改进 SNR，移除噪声过大而无法用于决策的基准，并在某个基准的噪声主导聚合分数时将该基准移出平均值。

3.3.1 Clustering Tasks

3.3.1 任务聚类


Source: p.11 S037

Original: To handle the large number of tasks, we cluster similar tasks into macro-averages. We aim for task clusters to match the granularity at which we perform data interventions, and for tasks within each cluster to behave similarly. Our clustering procedure requires a process to determine the similarity of two evaluations—we do this by collecting a pool of 23K benchmark scores from 70 external, open-weight models. Using our dataset of evaluation results, we assume that two benchmarks evaluate similar constructs if they rank models similarly. We perform hierarchical clustering using Ward’s variance-minimization (Ward Jr, 1963), which iteratively merges evaluation scores to minimize the variance of scores between benchmarks within a cluster. Figure 5 shows the result of the clustering procedure, where we manually select a threshold to balance the amount and granularity of clusters. Importantly, we do not use the exact result of the clustering procedure—we manually move a few tasks to ensure the format of the task is the same within each cluster (e.g., tasks requiring code execution all occur in the same cluster). The resulting task clusters are: MCSTEM, MCNon-STEM, GenQA, Math, Code, and Code FIM.

中文: 为了处理数量庞大的任务，我们将相似的任务聚类为宏观平均。我们希望任务簇与我们执行数据干预的粒度相匹配，并希望每个簇内的任务行为相似。我们的聚类过程需要一个判定两次评测相似程度的方法——我们通过从 70 个外部开放权重模型收集 23K 个基准分数来实现。利用这些评测结果数据，我们假设：如果两个基准对模型的排序相似，那么它们评测的是相似的结构。我们使用 Ward 方差最小化方法（Ward Jr, 1963）进行层次聚类，它迭代地合并评测分数，以最小化簇内基准之间分数的方差。图 5 展示了聚类结果，其中我们手动选择阈值以平衡簇的数量与粒度。重要的是，我们并不直接使用聚类过程的精确结果——我们会手动移动少数任务，以确保每个簇内任务的格式一致（例如，需要代码执行的任务都在同一个簇内）。最终得到的任务簇为：MCSTEM、MCNon-STEM、GenQA、Math、Code 和 Code FIM。

Fig. 5. OlmoBaseEval 的任务聚类

Placed near: p.11 S037
Source: p.12 C006

Original caption: Task clustering for OlmoBaseEval. Using a set of 23K benchmark results, the clustering method iteratively merges tasks which rank models similarly, until arriving at a stop condition. To arrive at OlmoBaseEval, we move tasks in the same format into the same cluster and split MC into STEM and Non-STEM tasks.

中文图注: OlmoBaseEval 的任务聚类。利用一组 23K 基准结果，聚类方法迭代地合并那些对模型排序相似的任务，直到满足停止条件。为得到 OlmoBaseEval，我们将相同格式的任务移入同一簇，并将 MC 拆分为 STEM 与 Non-STEM 任务。

Reading note: 该图是层次聚类的树状图；最终簇为 MCSTEM、MCNon-STEM、GenQA、Math、Code、Code FIM。

3.3.2 Scaling analysis

3.3.2 缩放分析


Source: p.12 S038

Original: We evaluate open-weight models across compute scales from 10¹⁸ to 10²⁵ training FLOPs to determine the compute scale at which particular metrics and tasks are useful for development decisions. On some evaluation benchmarks, it is too difficult to see signal when training models at small scales (Wei et al., 2022), and other benchmarks “saturate” near the labeling error of the benchmark (Vendrow et al., 2025). However, while many tasks appear emergent, continuous proxy metrics have been shown to be a better decision-making tool for model performance before we exit the noise floor (Schaeffer et al., 2023; Huang et al., 2024b; Magnusson et al., 2025). We propose a Base Easy task suite which measures bits-per-byte (BPB) over tasks from the Base Main suite that have gold labels or human-written answers, calculated as the negative log-likelihood of the answer divided by the number of UTF-8 bytes in the answer string, as described in Gao et al. (2020). We evaluate on the suite of 25 OLMo 2 scaling law models from Bhagia et al. (2024) to understand the scaling behavior in the low-compute regime, and 70 open-weight models to understand scaling behavior in the high-compute regime. Figure 6 shows the scaling behavior for our resulting Base Main benchmarks. For each task family, the Base Easy task suite shows signal at the small data ablation scale, and the Base Main task suites were not saturated at the large scale, leaving headroom for data experiments in midtraining.

中文: 我们在 10¹⁸ 到 10²⁵ 训练 FLOPs 的算力范围内评测开放权重模型，以确定特定指标与任务在何种算力规模下对开发决策有用。在某些评测基准上，以小规模训练模型时很难看到信号（Wei et al., 2022）；另一些基准则在接近基准标注误差时「饱和」（Vendrow et al., 2025）。然而，尽管许多任务看起来是涌现的，连续的代理指标已被证明在脱离噪声底线之前是更好的模型性能决策工具（Schaeffer et al., 2023; Huang et al., 2024b; Magnusson et al., 2025）。我们提出 Base Easy 任务套件，它测量 Base Main 套件中具有金标签或人工书写答案的任务上的每字节比特数（BPB），计算方式为答案的负对数似然除以答案字符串的 UTF-8 字节数，如 Gao et al. (2020) 所述。我们在 Bhagia et al. (2024) 的 25 个 OLMo 2 缩放律模型上评测，以理解低算力区间的缩放行为；并在 70 个开放权重模型上评测，以理解高算力区间的缩放行为。图 6 展示了我们最终 Base Main 基准的缩放行为。对每个任务族，Base Easy 任务套件在小数据消融规模上即显示信号，而 Base Main 任务套件在大规模下并未饱和，为中期训练中的数据实验留出了空间。

Fig. 6. OlmoBaseEval Math 套件的缩放分析

Placed near: p.12 S038
Source: p.12 C007

Original caption: Scaling analysis on the OlmoBaseEval Math suite. We use the OLMo 2 scaling models (Bhagia et al., 2024) to find benchmarks and metrics that show signal for small-scale models (left and center). Then, we use the small-scale OlmoBaseEval Easy suite as a proxy-metric for making data decisions.

中文图注: 对 OlmoBaseEval Math 套件的缩放分析。我们使用 OLMo 2 缩放模型（Bhagia et al., 2024）来寻找对大规模小模型显示出信号的基准与指标（左、中）。随后，我们使用小规模的 OlmoBaseEval Easy 套件作为做出数据决策的代理指标。

Reading note: 纵轴为每字节比特数（BPB）与 pass@1，横轴为估计算力（FLOPs）；该图说明 BPB 在低算力下即可给出信号，适合作为小规模数据消融的代理指标。

3.3.3 Signal-to-Noise Analysis

3.3.3 信噪比分析


Source: p.13 S039

Original: When reporting a macro-average, we aim to exclude tasks from each cluster that were too noisy to be helpful for development. We calculate the signal-to-noise ratio of each benchmark following the method from Heineman et al. (2025), where we evaluate the final 50 checkpoints of OLMo 2 13B training, and 10 external base models trained at roughly the same compute scale (4·10²³ FLOPs). From our findings, we transition from using 1K instance subsets to full evaluation sets when available. We remove some benchmarks from our evaluation suite entirely, particularly binary benchmarks such as BoolQ (Clark et al., 2019), as we found that models usually oscillate between predicting the majority and minority class. We repeat the same analysis for midtraining, instead using intermediate checkpoints from 5 preliminary pretraining runs. One important finding was to separate some benchmarks from the macro-average, like CruxEval (Gu et al., 2024a), which measures a relevant and unique capability (code input/output prediction) but would introduce too much noise into the macro-average. We show an example of the SNR of three individual benchmarks compared to the base main task averages across intermediate checkpoints during midtraining in Figure 7.

中文: 在报告宏观平均时，我们旨在从每个簇中排除噪声过大、对开发无益的任务。我们按照 Heineman et al. (2025) 的方法计算每个基准的信噪比，其中我们对 OLMo 2 13B 训练的最后 50 个检查点、以及 10 个以大致相同算力规模（4·10²³ FLOPs）训练的外部基座模型进行评测。根据我们的发现，我们从此前使用的 1K 实例子集过渡到（可用时的）完整评测集。我们从评测套件中完全移除了一些基准，尤其是 BoolQ（Clark et al., 2019）这类二分类基准，因为我们发现模型通常会在预测多数类与少数类之间来回摇摆。我们对中期训练重复了同样的分析，改用来自 5 个初步预训练运行的中间检查点。一个重要发现是，需要将一些基准从宏观平均中分离出来，例如 CruxEval（Gu et al., 2024a），它测量一种相关且独特的能力（代码输入/输出预测），但会给宏观平均引入过多噪声。图 7 展示了在中期训练的中间检查点上，三个单独基准的信噪比与基座主任务平均值的对比示例。

Fig. 7. OlmoBaseEval 在代码多任务平均上的信噪比分析

Placed near: p.13 S039
Source: p.13 C008

Original caption: OlmoBaseEval signal-to-noise analysis on the code multi-task average using intermediate checkpoints from midtraining. First, we aggregate into multi-task averages and remove tasks with high noise, such as CruxEval (left → center). Then, we tune generation hyperparameters to improve SNR, e.g., by increasing the n in pass@k (center → right).

中文图注: 使用中期训练的中间检查点，对代码多任务平均进行 OlmoBaseEval 信噪比分析。首先，我们聚合为多任务平均，并移除噪声较大的任务（如 CruxEval）（左→中）。然后，我们调优生成超参数以改进 SNR，例如增大 pass@k 中的 n（中→右）。

Reading note: 该图展示了通过「聚合多任务平均 + 过滤噪声任务 + 调优生成参数」三步来提升信噪比（SNR）。

3.3.4 OlmoBaseEval

3.3.4 OlmoBaseEval 套件


Source: p.13 S040

Original: The resulting OlmoBaseEval consists of a Base Easy suite for making development decisions using small compute budgets (e.g., less than 1B parameters) and a Base Main suite for development decisions for the final pretraining run and midtraining. We provide detail on the Chat suite later in §4.1. OlmoBaseEval contains 43 tasks, which is over 4 times more benchmarks than OLMo 2—including tracking math and code benchmarks in pretraining. To prevent overfitting on the development suite, we include a Held-out set of 4 benchmarks—MMLU Pro, DeepMind Math, LBPP, and BBH—each benchmark matching one broad capability we target during pretraining.

中文: 最终得到的 OlmoBaseEval 由两部分组成：Base Easy 套件，用于在小算力预算（例如低于 1B 参数）下做开发决策；以及 Base Main 套件，用于最终预训练运行与中期训练的决策。Chat 套件的细节我们将在第 §4.1 节提供。OlmoBaseEval 包含 43 个任务，比 OLMo 2 的基准多出 4 倍以上——包括在预训练中跟踪数学与代码基准。为防止对开发套件过拟合，我们加入了一个包含 4 个基准的留出集——MMLU Pro、DeepMind Math、LBPP 和 BBH——每个基准对应我们在预训练中所针对的一个宽泛能力。


Source: p.13 S041

Original: The suite includes four new benchmarks: BasicSkills, a set of 6 tasks to isolate the development of skills during pretraining (e.g., basic arithmetic, reasoning, and coding); Gen2MC, a multiple-choice version of 5 short-form generative tasks; MT MBPP, a translated BPB set for MBPP in 17 code languages; and Masked Perplexity, a new evaluation method applying token masking and calculating perplexity only on tokens that are difficult to learn. We evaluate with masked perplexity using UltraChat and WildChat, which provides a wide coverage of real user interaction evaluation in pretraining. Additional design and implementation details for OlmoBaseEval are included in Appendix A.4.

中文: 该套件包含四个新基准：BasicSkills，一组 6 个任务，用于隔离预训练期间技能的发展（例如基础算术、推理和编码）；Gen2MC，5 个短形式生成式任务的多项选择版本；MT MBPP，MBPP 在 17 种代码语言上的翻译版 BPB 集合；以及 Masked Perplexity，一种应用令牌掩码、仅对难以学习的令牌计算困惑度的新评测方法。我们使用 UltraChat 和 WildChat 进行掩码困惑度评测，从而在预训练中广泛覆盖真实用户交互的评测。OlmoBaseEval 的更多设计与实现细节见附录 A.4。

3.4 Stage 1: Pretraining

3.4 阶段 1：预训练


Source: p.13 S042

Original: We first train Olmo 3 Base on Dolma 3 Mix, our 6T token pretraining data mix. While Dolma 3 Mix is comprised of largely the same types of data sources used in other open pretraining recipes (Soldaini et al., 2024; Bakouch et al., 2025; OLMo et al., 2024), we demonstrate three key novelties: (i) new tooling for fast and scalable global deduplication at the trillion-token scale; (ii) two new methods for optimizing selection of training tokens: token-constrained mixing and quality-aware upsampling; (iii) a novel source of academic PDFs—olmOCR science PDFs—converted to linearized plain text using olmOCR (Section §3.4.2) (Poznanski et al., 2025a).

中文: 我们首先在 Dolma 3 Mix 上训练 Olmo 3 Base，这是我们 6T 令牌的预训练数据混合。虽然 Dolma 3 Mix 大部分由与其他开放预训练配方相同类型的数据源组成（Soldaini et al., 2024; Bakouch et al., 2025; OLMo et al., 2024），但我们展示了三项关键创新：（i）在万亿令牌规模上实现快速、可扩展全局去重的新工具；（ii）两种优化训练令牌选择的新方法：令牌约束混合与质量感知上采样；（iii）一种新的学术 PDF 来源——olmOCR 科学 PDF——使用 olmOCR 转换为线性化纯文本（第 §3.4.2 节）（Poznanski et al., 2025a）。


Source: p.14 S043

Original: Table 4 summarizes our data sources, pool sizes, and final training mix. As developing a base model is the most compute-intensive part of our development process, requiring training over trillions of tokens and consuming over 90% of overall compute, we adhere to two major principles to guide our data strategy: (i) We consider a source of data for pretraining if it has potential to yield enough tokens to impact model capabilities at pretraining scale. Valuable data sources that are small may not be impactful in pretraining and are better reserved for midtraining. (ii) While we embrace exploration of structured “task” data (e.g. QA pairs, chat instances) for training base models, we reserve their use only for later stages of midtraining (Section §3.5) and long-context extension (Section §3.6). Task data often does not meet the pool size needed to impact our pretraining stage, even with synthetic generation, and task data also tends to have an outsized impact on evaluation results, potentially confounding data ablations for other sources.

中文: 表 4 总结了我们的数据源、数据池规模与最终训练混合。由于开发基座模型是我们开发过程中算力最密集的部分（需要训练数万亿令牌、消耗超过 90% 的整体算力），我们遵循两条主要原则来指导数据策略：（i）只有当一个数据源有潜力产出足够多的令牌、能够在预训练规模上影响模型能力时，我们才考虑将其用于预训练。规模较小的有价值数据源在预训练中可能无法产生影响，更适合留作中期训练之用。（ii）虽然我们乐于探索用于训练基座模型的结构化「任务」数据（例如 QA 对、对话实例），但我们仅将其保留用于中期训练（第 §3.5 节）与长上下文扩展（第 §3.6 节）的后期阶段。任务数据通常达不到影响预训练阶段所需的数据池规模（即便借助合成生成），并且任务数据往往会对评测结果产生不成比例的影响，可能干扰对其他来源的数据消融。

Table 4. Dolma 3 Mix 的组成（9T 数据池 / 6T 训练混合 / 150B 实验混合）

Placed near: p.14 S043
Source: p.14 C009

Original caption: Composition of Dolma 3 Mix including our 9T pool of data, the 6T mix we used for final model training, and the 150B mix we used for experimentation.

中文表注: Dolma 3 Mix 的组成，包括我们 9T 的数据池、用于最终模型训练的 6T 混合、以及用于实验的 150B 混合。

Reading note: 数据源包括 Common Crawl（网页，占比最大，76.1%）、olmOCR science PDFs（学术文档，13.6%）、Stack-Edu（GitHub 代码，6.89%）、FineMath 3+（数学网页，2.56%）、arXiv、Wikipedia & Wikibooks 等。
