Reading note: 该图展示三类来源（Common Crawl 网页、学术 PDF、GitHub 仓库 + FineMath/ArXiv/Wiki）各自经过提取、启发式过滤、去重、主题与质量分类、质量上采样，最终汇入 Mixing 得到 Dolma 3 Mix。

3.4.1 Preparing our Web Data Pool

3.4.1 网页数据池的制备


Source: p.15 S044

Original: We took the following steps to curate pretraining data from CommonCrawl (Common Crawl Foundation), which constituted the majority of our pretraining corpus. Text extraction — We start with 104 dumps from the CommonCrawl corpus, with a cutoff date of December 31, 2024. Following DCLM (Li et al., 2024a), we remove HTML artifacts and extract the semantic text from WARC files using Resiliparse (Bevendorff et al., 2018). Where applicable, we directly leverage the raw Resiliparse-extracted data from DCLM-pool (Li et al., 2024a) and apply Resiliparse extraction on dumps not contained within the DCLM-pool.

中文: 我们采取以下步骤从 CommonCrawl（Common Crawl Foundation）整理预训练数据，它构成了我们预训练语料的主体。文本提取——我们从 CommonCrawl 语料的 104 个转储开始，截止日期为 2024 年 12 月 31 日。遵循 DCLM（Li et al., 2024a），我们移除 HTML 痕迹，并使用 Resiliparse（Bevendorff et al., 2018）从 WARC 文件中提取语义文本。在适用的情况下，我们直接利用 DCLM-pool（Li et al., 2024a）中已用 Resiliparse 提取好的原始数据，并对 DCLM-pool 未包含的转储应用 Resiliparse 提取。


Source: p.15 S045

Original: Heuristic filtering — We apply a pipeline of heuristic filtering steps to prune our initial collection of 252.6B documents to a size amenable for pretraining. Our process closely follows that of DCLM (Li et al., 2024a) with minor modifications to improve data quality and computational efficiency. We first apply URL filtering to remove spam and adult-content from an expanded blocklist. We then remove documents that were either too short or too long, followed by filtering documents that contain excessive symbols or insufficient quantities of alphabetic characters. Next we remove documents containing large amounts of internal repetition and apply filtering to remove common spam phrases, fully removing any documents that are identified by these heuristics. We then use a fastText classifier to identify the language of each document, keeping only documents that contain English text. As a final step, we apply sentence-level heuristics from Madlad400 (Li et al., 2024a). In aggregate, this process reduces the size of our data pool by 84.6%, yielding a corpus of 38.8B documents. More details are provided in Appendix §A.2.

中文: 启发式过滤——我们应用一套启发式过滤流水线，将初始的 252.6B 文档剪枝到适合预训练的规模。我们的流程紧密遵循 DCLM（Li et al., 2024a），并做了少量修改以改进数据质量与计算效率。我们首先应用 URL 过滤，从扩充后的黑名单中移除垃圾与成人内容。随后移除过短或过长的文档，再过滤掉包含过多符号或字母字符数量不足的文档。接下来，我们移除包含大量内部重复的文档，并应用过滤以移除常见垃圾短语，彻底删除任何被这些启发式规则识别出的文档。然后我们使用 fastText 分类器识别每个文档的语言，仅保留包含英文文本的文档。最后一步，我们应用 Madlad400（Li et al., 2024a）的句子级启发式规则。总体而言，这一过程将我们的数据池规模减少了 84.6%，得到 38.8B 文档的语料。更多细节见附录 §A.2。


Source: p.15 S046

Original: Deduplication — The web data we collect from CommonCrawl naturally contains an abundance of duplicated documents. This duplication arises from repeated crawls of the same website, near-copies of documents appearing across multiple web pages, and highly-repeated boilerplate text. Our deduplication strategy is motivated by three observations from prior work: 1) deduplication generally leads to more token-efficient training (Lee et al., 2022); 2) duplicate count serves as a weak signal of data quality, with higher duplicate counts indicating higher quality (Fang et al., 2025a); 3) repeating documents more than a handful of times provides rapidly diminishing returns (Muennighoff et al., 2025a). Given these observations, we design our deduplication strategy to enable a future quality-based upsampling step (Section 3.4.4). We aggressively deduplicate our dataset at multiple granularities, targeting the removal of exact replicas, near-duplicates, and repeated filler text. While this necessarily discards the quality signal from duplicate counts, it produces a clean base dataset from which we can later selectively reintroduce repetition for high-quality documents. Our goal is a final dataset with minimal repetition overall, with any duplication concentrated in high-quality data. We implement our deduplication procedure in three distinct stages:

中文: 去重——我们从 CommonCrawl 收集的网页数据天然包含大量重复文档。这种重复来自对同一网站的反复爬取、跨多个网页出现的近似副本、以及大量重复的样板文本。我们的去重策略受到先前工作三项观察的启发：1）去重通常能带来更高的令牌效率训练（Lee et al., 2022）；2）重复计数是数据质量的弱信号，重复计数越高代表质量越高（Fang et al., 2025a）；3）将文档重复超过少数几次会带来迅速递减的收益（Muennighoff et al., 2025a）。基于这些观察，我们设计去重策略以支撑后续的质量型上采样步骤（第 3.4.4 节）。我们在多个粒度上积极去重，目标是移除精确副本、近似重复以及重复的填充文本。虽然这必然丢弃了重复计数中的质量信号，但它产出了一个干净的基础数据集，我们日后可以据此为高质量文档选择性地重新引入重复。我们的目标是得到一个整体重复最少、任何重复都集中在高质量数据中的最终数据集。我们分三个独立阶段实现去重：


Source: p.15 S047

Original: 1. Exact deduplication — We apply global deduplication based on document text hashes to remove all exact copies. This step identifies 67% of the pool as duplicates, reducing the dataset from 38.7B to 12.8B documents. 2. Fuzzy deduplication — We apply MinHash-based deduplication to identify and remove near-identical documents, such as documents copied across multiple domains that differ only in headers or footers. We partition the dataset into 32 shards, ran MinHash deduplication on each shard, then performed exhaustive pairwise Jaccard similarity checks within each identified cluster. From each cluster, we retain the most recent document by crawl date. This procedure identified 23% of the pool as duplicates, yielding 9.8B documents. 3. Substring deduplication — The previous steps remove whole duplicate documents but did not address repeated content within individual documents. Many documents contain substantial boilerplate text or HTML artifacts (e.g., headers and footers) of limited training value. To remove these repeated substrings, we apply a novel fuzzy suffix-array-based deduplication procedure. We partition the dataset into 57 shards and apply this procedure to each, marking any substring of 500 or more bytes that occurred multiple times. Unlike previous suffix-array methods, we preserve at least one occurrence of each repeated substring in the corpus. We then merge the intervals marking repeated substrings to also remove short substrings sandwiched between longer repeated segments. This procedure removes 14% of text bytes, yielding 9.7B documents totaling 36.5T bytes of uncompressed text.

中文: 1. 精确去重——我们基于文档文本哈希进行全局去重，以移除所有精确副本。此步骤识别出数据池中 67% 的重复项，将数据集从 38.7B 减至 12.8B 文档。2. 模糊去重——我们应用基于 MinHash 的去重来识别并移除近似相同的文档，例如跨多个域复制、仅在页眉页脚有差异的文档。我们将数据集划分为 32 个分片，对每个分片运行 MinHash 去重，然后在每个识别出的簇内进行穷举的成对 Jaccard 相似度检查。对每个簇，我们保留按爬取日期最新的文档。此过程识别出数据池中 23% 的重复项，得到 9.8B 文档。3. 子串去重——前两步移除了整篇重复文档，但未处理单个文档内部的重复内容。许多文档包含大量训练价值有限的样板文本或 HTML 痕迹（如页眉页脚）。为移除这些重复子串，我们应用一种新颖的、基于模糊后缀数组的去重过程。我们将数据集划分为 57 个分片并对每个分片应用此过程，标记任何出现多次的 500 字节及以上子串。与先前的后缀数组方法不同，我们在语料中至少保留每个重复子串的一次出现。随后我们合并标记重复子串的区间，以同时移除夹在更长重复片段之间的短子串。此过程移除了 14% 的文本字节，得到 9.7B 文档、合计 36.5T 字节的未压缩文本。


Source: p.16 S048

Original: This three-stage procedure reduces the web corpus from 38.7B to 9.7B documents—a 75% reduction in document count. The resulting aggressively deduplicated dataset can then be partitioned by topic and quality and controllably upsampled for training. To scale our deduplication strategy, we develop the Duplodocus tool, a native-rust toolkit for large-scale distributed execution of both hash-based exact deduplication and MinHash fuzzy deduplication.

中文: 这一三阶段过程将网页语料从 38.7B 减少到 9.7B 文档——文档数减少了 75%。由此得到的、经过积极去重的数据集，随后可以按主题与质量进行划分，并有控制地进行上采样用于训练。为了扩展我们的去重策略，我们开发了 Duplodocus 工具，一个用于大规模分布式执行基于哈希的精确去重与 MinHash 模糊去重的原生 Rust 工具包。


Source: p.16 S049

Original: Topic and quality classification — We use our WebOrganizer tool (Wettig et al., 2025) to partition the deduplicated corpus into 24 topics (e.g., “Adult Content”, “Politics”, or “Science and Technology”). To speed up processing of the Dolma 3 pool, we distill the transformer-based models by Wettig et al. 2025 into a simpler fastText model. We only partition by topic, not format. We also train and apply a fastText-based quality classifier to assign each document a quality score. Following DCLM (Li et al., 2024a), we use OpenHermes-2.5 (Teknium, 2023) and ELI5 (Fan et al., 2019) as positive training examples, supplemented with UltraChat-200k (Ding et al., 2023) and WildChat-1M (Zhao et al., 2024a). Negative training examples consist of 30GB sampled from DCLM-RefinedWeb. We apply both the topic and quality classifiers to the full deduplicated corpus in order to partition the dataset. Documents are first partitioned by topic, then within each topic partition we compute quality score percentiles and subdivide documents into vigintile buckets (5-percentile intervals). This two-stage partitioning yields 480 disjoint subsets (24 topics × 20 quality tiers), enabling fine-grained control over the topic and quality distribution of our pretraining mixture.

中文: 主题与质量分类——我们使用 WebOrganizer 工具（Wettig et al., 2025）将去重后的语料划分为 24 个主题（例如「成人内容」「政治」「科学与技术」）。为加速 Dolma 3 数据池的处理，我们将 Wettig et al. 2025 的基于 transformer 的模型蒸馏为一个更简单的 fastText 模型。我们仅按主题划分，不按格式。我们还训练并应用一个基于 fastText 的质量分类器，为每个文档分配质量分数。遵循 DCLM（Li et al., 2024a），我们使用 OpenHermes-2.5（Teknium, 2023）和 ELI5（Fan et al., 2019）作为正训练样本，并辅以 UltraChat-200k（Ding et al., 2023）和 WildChat-1M（Zhao et al., 2024a）。负训练样本由从 DCLM-RefinedWeb 采样的 30GB 数据组成。我们将主题与质量两个分类器都应用于整个去重语料，以划分数据集。文档首先按主题划分，然后在每个主题分区内计算质量分数百分位，并将文档细分为「vigintile」桶（5 百分位区间）。这一两阶段划分产生了 480 个互不相交的子集（24 主题 × 20 质量层），从而能精细控制预训练混合的主题与质量分布。


Source: p.16 S050

Original: Final web data pool — The above steps result in an 8T-token pool of annotated data, partitioned into buckets according to topic and text quality. This pool serves as the foundation for our pretraining mixture, though additional processing is required to construct the final training data. Specifically, we apply quality-based filtering and topic reweighting to generate a balanced, high-quality mixture, as discussed in Section §3.4.4.

中文: 最终网页数据池——上述步骤产出一个 8T 令牌的带标注数据池，按主题与文本质量划分为桶。该数据池是我们预训练混合的基础，不过要构建最终训练数据还需要进一步处理。具体而言，我们应用基于质量的过滤与主题重加权来生成平衡、高质量的混合，如第 §3.4.4 节所述。

3.4.2 Preparing our olmOCR science PDFs Data Pool

3.4.2 olmOCR 科学 PDF 数据池的制备


Source: p.16 S051

Original: We curate a novel dataset of academic PDFs, replacing our previous use of peS2o (Soldaini and Lo, 2023). These documents are crawled “politely”: we identify our crawler as AI2Bot, we adhere to robots.txt, and do not bypass paywalls. The crawler is seeded with a focus on academic sites and paper repositories. We process all PDFs using the first version of olmOCR (Poznanski et al., 2025a). Ultimately this crawl generates a collection of 238 million unique PDF documents with a cutoff date of December 2024.

中文: 我们构建了一个新颖的学术 PDF 数据集，取代了此前使用的 peS2o（Soldaini and Lo, 2023）。这些文档以「礼貌」方式爬取：我们将自己的爬虫标识为 AI2Bot，遵守 robots.txt，且不绕过付费墙。爬虫的种子聚焦于学术站点与论文仓库。我们使用第一版 olmOCR（Poznanski et al., 2025a）处理所有 PDF。最终，这次爬取生成了一个包含 2.38 亿篇唯一 PDF 文档的集合，截止日期为 2024 年 12 月。


Source: p.16 S052

Original: olmOCR text extraction — To convert PDFs to a format usable by our trainer, we apply pre-filtering and text extraction. If a document contains born-digital text, we used the Lingua language detector to retain only English documents and remove documents where spam or SEO-optimization keywords exceeded 0.4% of total words. We then extract text using olmOCR (Poznanski et al., 2025a) (versions 0.1.49-0.1.53). If olmOCR fails, we use Poppler’s pdftotext as a fallback; documents requiring this fallback for more than 1 in 250 pages are excluded from the corpus. This yields a dataset of 160 million PDF documents.

中文: olmOCR 文本提取——为将 PDF 转换为训练器可用的格式，我们应用预过滤与文本提取。如果文档包含原生数字文本，我们使用 Lingua 语言检测器仅保留英文文档，并移除垃圾或 SEO 优化关键词超过总词数 0.4% 的文档。随后我们使用 olmOCR（Poznanski et al., 2025a）（版本 0.1.49–0.1.53）提取文本。若 olmOCR 失败，我们回退使用 Poppler 的 pdftotext；凡超过 1/250 页面需要回退的文档均被排除出语料。由此得到 1.6 亿篇 PDF 文档的数据集。


Source: p.16 S053

Original: Deduplication — We then identify and remove any fuzzy-duplicates using a MinHash algorithm. This differs slightly from the MinHash step we apply to the web text corpus in Section §3.4.1: we use the MinHash parameters as in FineWeb (Penedo et al., 2024), which targets document pairs with at least 75% similarity; and we omit an exhaustive pairwise Jaccard similarity check. After this deduplication step, we were left with a corpus of 156M documents for a removal rate of 2.3%.

中文: 去重——随后我们使用 MinHash 算法识别并移除模糊重复项。这与我们在第 §3.4.1 节对网页文本语料所应用的 MinHash 步骤略有不同：我们使用 FineWeb（Penedo et al., 2024）中的 MinHash 参数，其目标是相似度至少 75% 的文档对；并且我们省略了穷举的成对 Jaccard 相似度检查。经过此去重步骤后，我们剩下了 1.56 亿篇文档，移除率为 2.3%。


Source: p.17 S054

Original: PII filtering — Next we remove documents containing PII from the pool of PDFs. Our goal was to remove documents that contained sensitive standalone PII, such as government IDs and login information, as well as documents that link biographical, medical, location, employment, or educational information to a specific individual. Through iteration, we determine that PII detection must be document type-aware to be effective. For example, a conference paper might contain name and place of employment of authors; however, as research articles are intended for publication, removal would not make sense. At the same time, a bank statement might contain the same name and employer information, and is clearly a document a language model should not be trained on. The rule we follow is: is this document type intended for public dissemination? We use manual annotators to iterate which documents types are not suitable for public dissemination, and what PII attributes we should consider. The resulting taxonomy is used as part of a multi-stage model-based PII filtering pipeline.

中文: PII 过滤——接下来我们从 PDF 数据池中移除包含个人身份信息（PII）的文档。我们的目标是移除包含敏感独立 PII（如政府证件号与登录信息）的文档，以及将生物、医疗、位置、职业或教育信息关联到特定个人的文档。通过迭代，我们确定 PII 检测必须区分文档类型才能有效。例如，一篇会议论文可能包含作者的姓名与工作单位；然而，由于研究文章本就用于发表，移除它们并不合理。与此同时，一份银行对账单可能包含相同的姓名与雇主信息，而这显然是语言模型不应训练的文档。我们所遵循的规则是：这一文档类型是否面向公开发布？我们使用人工标注者来迭代确定哪些文档类型不适合公开发布，以及我们应考虑哪些 PII 属性。由此得到的分类体系被用于一个多阶段、基于模型的 PII 过滤流水线。


Source: p.17 S055

Original: First we classify documents using a prompt to Gemma 3 12B (Gemma 3 Team, 2025) on the first page of each document to determine if they contain any sensitive standalone PII, or link sensitive information to an individual. Next, we use Gemma 3 4B on the first 5,000 characters of each document to arrive at a set of flags describing the type of document. From these classification results, we develop a set of rules to identify which types of documents containing PII should be publicly available and which should be filtered. Ultimately this removes 4.9% of the remaining pool and yields a pool of 148 million documents. See Poznanski et al. (2025a) for a more complete overview of the PII removal pipeline.

中文: 首先，我们对每篇文档的第一页使用提示词询问 Gemma 3 12B（Gemma 3 Team, 2025）进行分类，以判断其是否包含任何敏感的独立 PII，或将敏感信息关联到个人。接下来，我们对每篇文档的前 5000 个字符使用 Gemma 3 4B，得到一组描述文档类型的标记。基于这些分类结果，我们制定一套规则，以识别哪些含 PII 的文档类型应公开可用、哪些应被过滤。最终这一步移除了剩余数据池的 4.9%，得到 1.48 亿篇文档。关于 PII 移除流水线的更完整概述，参见 Poznanski et al. (2025a)。


Source: p.17 S056

Original: Heuristic filtering — After PII removal, we apply a round of heuristic filtering to further remove low-quality documents. Filters applied in this step include checking for: non-English documents not originally caught by the Lingua filter; documents that were more than 30% tables; and documents that contain more than 20% numbers. Next we apply modifications that convert markdown tables to HTML and remove URL references. The combination of these filtration steps yield a corpus of 108 million documents. This corpus is then partitioned into 24 topical buckets, according to the WebOrganizer topic classifier (Wettig et al., 2025), and passed off to the mixing (Section §3.4.4).

中文: 启发式过滤——在 PII 移除之后，我们再应用一轮启发式过滤，以进一步移除低质量文档。此步应用的过滤器包括检查：未被 Lingua 过滤器捕获的非英文文档；表格占比超过 30% 的文档；数字占比超过 20% 的文档。接下来我们应用转换，将 Markdown 表格转为 HTML 并移除 URL 引用。这些过滤步骤的组合得到 1.08 亿篇文档的语料。随后按 WebOrganizer 主题分类器（Wettig et al., 2025）将该语料划分为 24 个主题桶，并交给混合阶段（第 §3.4.4 节）。

3.4.3 Preparing Code, Math, and other sources

3.4.3 代码、数学与其他来源的制备


Source: p.17 S057

Original: Code — For code data, we use Stack-Edu (Allal et al., 2025), an improved curation of GitHub repositories from the-stack-v2 dataset (Lozhkov et al., 2024) with additional filtering for educational programming content. We keep partitions of the data by programming language for subsequent mixing. Math — As in OLMo 2, we include arXiv documents from the Proof-Pile-2 dataset (Azerbayev et al., 2023), which in turn are from the RedPajama dataset (Together AI, 2023) and have a cutoff date of April 2023. We use this source primarily because it preserves the original LaTeX notation, enabling the model to learn both mathematical content and how to properly format it. Furthermore, we replace our previous use of OpenWebMath (Paster et al., 2023) with FineMath (Allal et al., 2025), a subset of Common Crawl documents that contain mathematical educational content and have been reprocessed to preserve proper mathematical notation. We include all documents that have a quality score of at least 3 (out of 4), according to the FineMath classifier. This data has a cutoff date of September 2024.

中文: 代码——对于代码数据，我们使用 Stack-Edu（Allal et al., 2025），它是 the-stack-v2 数据集（Lozhkov et al., 2024）中 GitHub 仓库的改进整理版，并对教育性编程内容做了额外过滤。我们按编程语言保留数据分区，以供后续混合。数学——与 OLMo 2 一样，我们纳入来自 Proof-Pile-2 数据集（Azerbayev et al., 2023）的 arXiv 文档，这些文档又来自 RedPajama 数据集（Together AI, 2023），截止日期为 2023 年 4 月。我们使用该来源主要是因为其保留了原始 LaTeX 记法，使模型既能学习数学内容，也能学习如何正确排版。此外，我们用 FineMath（Allal et al., 2025）取代了此前使用的 OpenWebMath（Paster et al., 2023）。FineMath 是 Common Crawl 文档的一个子集，包含数学教育内容，并经重新处理以保留正确的数学记法。根据 FineMath 分类器，我们纳入所有质量分数至少为 3（满分 4）的文档。该数据的截止日期为 2024 年 9 月。


Source: p.17 S058

Original: Other — Finally, we include the Wikipedia and Wikibooks sources from Dolma (Soldaini et al., 2024) as base sources of encyclopedic knowledge. These are both the “English” and “Simple” editions of Wikipedia and Wikibooks with a cutoff date of March 2023. These sources were processed using WikiExtractor (Attardi, 2015) to remove markup formatting, and all documents with 25 or fewer words were filtered out to exclude template pages or pages that encountered XML parsing errors.

中文: 其他——最后，我们纳入来自 Dolma（Soldaini et al., 2024）的 Wikipedia 与 Wikibooks 来源，作为百科知识的基础来源。这些是 Wikipedia 与 Wikibooks 的「英语」和「简单英语」两个版本，截止日期为 2023 年 3 月。这些来源使用 WikiExtractor（Attardi, 2015）处理以移除标记格式，并且所有词数不超过 25 的文档都被过滤掉，以排除模板页或遇到 XML 解析错误的页面。

3.4.4 Sampling and Mixing over Data Pools

3.4.4 数据池上的采样与混合


Source: p.17 S059

Original: The data sources described above collectively provide over 9 trillion tokens of diverse text data. Transforming this collection into a training dataset requires a mixing and sampling pipeline to prescribe exactly how much of each source to include in a final training mix, and how much, if any, upsampling to apply to each source. We apply a mixing strategy that draws on swarm-based methods to train and evaluate many smaller proxy models, using these results to inform an optimal mix. Further, we apply a novel conditional mixing procedure to account for the fact that our data sources were being constantly refined and updated throughout the development cycle. In this section, we describe how we derive the final mixing ratios for each source; for web text, we only optimize ratios at the topic category level and apply quality-aware upsampling to obtain the final mix.

中文: 上述数据源合计提供了超过 9 万亿令牌的多样化文本数据。将这一集合转化为训练数据集，需要一个混合与采样流水线，来精确规定每个来源在最终训练混合中应占多少比例、以及对每个来源应用多少（如果有的话）上采样。我们应用一种借鉴集群方法（swarm-based）的混合策略，训练并评测许多更小的代理模型，用这些结果来确定最优混合。此外，我们应用一种新颖的条件混合（conditional mixing）过程，以应对我们的数据源在整个开发周期中被不断精炼与更新的现实。在本节中，我们描述如何推导每个来源的最终混合比例；对于网页文本，我们仅在主题类别层面优化比例，并应用质量感知上采样来得到最终混合。


Source: p.18 S060

Original: Constrained data mixing — We applied data mixing across all pretraining sources, as well as across the WebOrganizer topics within the web data and PDF sources, and the Stack-Edu programming languages. Our mixing procedure (Chen et al., 2026) consists of two components: a base procedure that constructs a high-quality mix over a fixed set of data domains, and a meta-procedure called conditional mixing that efficiently updates an existing mix when domains change. Together, these allow us to iteratively build an optimal mix and adapt to data refinements or additions without starting from scratch.

中文: 约束数据混合——我们在所有预训练来源上应用数据混合，也在网页数据与 PDF 来源内部的 WebOrganizer 主题、以及 Stack-Edu 编程语言上应用。我们的混合过程（Chen et al., 2026）由两部分组成：一个基础过程，在固定的一组数据域上构建高质量混合；以及一个名为条件混合（conditional mixing）的元过程，它在域发生变化时高效地更新现有混合。二者结合，使我们能够迭代地构建最优混合，并在无需从零开始的情况下适应数据的精炼或新增。


Source: p.18 S061

Original: The base procedure follows a swarm-based approach inspired by RegMix (Liu et al., 2024a), Data Mixing Laws (Ye et al., 2025), and CLIMB (Diao et al., 2025); it consists of three stages: 1. Swarm construction — We sample the space of possible mixes by training many small proxy models, each with a different mixing ratio. Specifically, we train 30M-parameter models following the Olmo 3 architecture for 3B tokens (5x Chinchilla), sampling each mix from a Dirichlet distribution centered on the natural (no-mixing) distribution. As a rule of thumb, we launch a swarm of size 5x that of the number of domains. We then evaluate each proxy model on the Base Easy suite. 2. Per-task regression — Each proxy model provides a datapoint mapping mixture weights to task performance—measured in bits-per-byte (BPB)—for each task. We fit a separate generalized linear model for each task, enabling us to predict how any candidate mix will perform. 3. Mix optimization — We find the mixture that minimizes the average task BPB, as predicted by the per-task regression models. Since we ultimately seek a corpus with a 6T token budget, and we avoid repeating any domain more than approximately 4−7 times, this naturally imposes maximum ratio constraints on certain domains based on their available token counts. We solve this constrained optimization using a guided search initialized from a prior or natural distribution.

中文: 基础过程遵循受 RegMix（Liu et al., 2024a）、Data Mixing Laws（Ye et al., 2025）和 CLIMB（Diao et al., 2025）启发的集群方法；它由三个阶段组成：1. 集群构建——我们通过训练许多小代理模型（每个使用不同的混合比例）来采样可能的混合空间。具体而言，我们遵循 Olmo 3 架构训练 30M 参数的模型、训练 3B 令牌（5 倍 Chinchilla），每个混合都从一个以自然（无混合）分布为中心的 Dirichlet 分布中采样。按经验法则，我们启动规模为域数量 5 倍的集群。随后我们在 Base Easy 套件上评测每个代理模型。2. 逐任务回归——每个代理模型为每个任务提供一个数据点，将混合权重映射到任务性能——以每字节比特数（BPB）衡量。我们为每个任务拟合一个独立的广义线性模型，从而能够预测任何候选混合的表现。3. 混合优化——我们找到使平均任务 BPB 最小化的混合，如逐任务回归模型所预测的那样。由于我们最终寻求一个 6T 令牌预算的语料，并且避免任何域重复超过约 4–7 次，这自然地对某些域施加了基于其可用令牌数的最大比例约束。我们使用从先验或自然分布初始化的引导式搜索来解决这一约束优化问题。


Source: p.19 S062

Original: The base procedure assumes fixed domains, but real preprocessing workflows evolve continuously as we refine filters, add domains, or discover and mitigate quality issues. Rather than recomputing an entire swarm each time domains change, we introduce a new procedure called conditional mixing to efficiently adapt the base method to an evolving data landscape. The key idea is to treat the existing optimized mix as a single virtual domain with frozen mixing ratios, then re-run the base procedure over this virtual domain plus any new or modified domains. This effectively restricts the base mixing procedure to a lower-dimensional subspace of the mixture weight space, reducing swarm size and computational cost. Further details and justification of this procedure can be found in Chen et al. (2026).

中文: 基础过程假设域是固定的，但真实的预处理工作流会随着我们精炼过滤器、增加域、或发现并缓解质量问题的过程而持续演进。与其在每次域变化时重新计算整个集群，我们引入了一种名为条件混合的新过程，以高效地将基础方法适配到不断演进的数据格局。其核心思想是：将现有的已优化混合视为一个具有冻结混合比例的单一「虚拟域」，然后在此虚拟域加上任何新增或修改的域上重新运行基础过程。这实际上将基础混合过程限制在混合权重空间的一个更低维子空间中，从而减小集群规模与计算成本。此过程的更多细节与论证见 Chen et al. (2026)。


Source: p.19 S063

Original: To construct the Dolma 3 Mix weights, we perform three rounds of our conditional mixing procedure, with weights over the 24 WebOrganizer categories within the DCLM Baseline mix as well as the source-level mix, each stage building incrementally on frozen mixtures from prior stages. We first obtain optimized mixture weights over WebOrganizer categories. Web text serves as the starting point because it constitutes the largest data pool and because we use it to develop the base mixing methodology. As finalizing the bespoke web data pool described in Section §3.4.1 occurs concurrently with these initial mixing rounds, we perform this first round of mixing on DCLM-Baseline, expecting that learned preferences would transfer to our final web data. Having frozen a mixture across WebOrganizer categories over web text, we turn our attention to mixtures of programming languages from Stack-Edu. Diverging slightly from the conditional mixing procedure, we fix the web text ratio to be 75% of the pool and force a 25% mixture of Stack-Edu data and only optimize over the composition of programming languages within this 25%. Finally, we perform one more round of conditional mixing to integrate the 24 WebOrganizer categories of the PDF data, conditioned on the DCLM, Stack-Edu, and source-level mixes. This incremental approach towards mixing is essential: for example, we complete PDF curation substantially later than other sources, and conditional mixing enables us to incorporate late-arriving data while reusing prior optimization results rather than restarting the expensive swarm-based base procedure.

中文: 为构建 Dolma 3 Mix 的权重，我们执行三轮条件混合过程，权重覆盖 DCLM Baseline 混合内的 24 个 WebOrganizer 类别以及来源级混合，每一阶段都在前几阶段的冻结混合之上增量构建。我们首先在 WebOrganizer 类别上得到优化后的混合权重。网页文本之所以是起点，是因为它构成了最大的数据池，也是我们开发基础混合方法所用。由于第 §3.4.1 节所述定制网页数据池的最终确定与这些初始混合轮次是并行的，我们在 DCLM-Baseline 上执行这第一轮混合，期望学到的偏好能迁移到我们最终的网页数据。在网页文本的 WebOrganizer 类别混合冻结之后，我们转向 Stack-Edu 编程语言的混合。略微偏离条件混合过程，我们将网页文本比例固定为数据池的 75%，并强制 Stack-Edu 数据占 25%，仅在这 25% 内优化编程语言的构成。最后，我们再执行一轮条件混合，以纳入 PDF 数据的 24 个 WebOrganizer 类别，并以 DCLM、Stack-Edu 和来源级混合为条件。这种增量的混合方式至关重要：例如，我们完成 PDF 整理的时间显著晚于其他来源，条件混合使我们能在复用先前优化结果的同时纳入迟到的数据，而无需重启昂贵的集群式基础过程。

Fig. 9. 约束数据混合的示例与效果

Placed near: p.18 S063
Source: p.18 C011

Original caption: Examples and effects of constrained data mixing for Olmo 3. On the left, comparison of the natural distribution of data sources in the Dolma 3 pool versus our learned data mixture in Dolma 3 Mix (Figures 9a and 9c). On the right, the improvement on downstream evaluations resulting from training on our data mix compared to the natural distribution (Figures 9b and 9d).

中文图注: Olmo 3 约束数据混合的示例与效果。左侧：Dolma 3 数据池中数据源的自然分布与我们学得的 Dolma 3 Mix 数据混合的对比（图 9a 与 9c）。右侧：与自然分布相比，在我们数据混合上训练所带来的下游评测改进（图 9b 与 9d）。

Reading note: 上图（a/b）按 WebOrganizer 主题优化网页文本混合；下图（c/d）按编程语言优化 Stack-Edu。优化后的混合显著上浮 STEM 主题与 Python。


Source: p.19 S064

Original: Figure 9 presents mixing outcomes and their performance results relative to the natural data distribution. For web text (top panels), the optimized mixture dramatically upweights STEM domains (e.g. “Science, Math, and Technology” and “Software Development”). On 1B-parameter models trained for 5x Chinchilla, this mixture obtains an average improvement of 0.056 and max of 0.209 (in BPB), while only 13 out of 54 tasks show degradations, none of which exceed 0.035. For rebalancing of programming languages in Stack-Edu (bottom panels), the optimized mix favors Python over Java and Markdown, yielding modest improvements in all but two coding benchmarks. Table 38 further demonstrates our method’s adaptability: swapping development suites to emphasize QA, math, or coding produces mixtures that preferentially optimize these respective capabilities.

中文: 图 9 展示了混合结果及其相对于自然数据分布的性能结果。对于网页文本（上图），优化后的混合显著上浮了 STEM 域（如「科学、数学与技术」和「软件开发」）。在以 5 倍 Chinchilla 训练的 1B 参数模型上，该混合取得了 0.056 的平均改进与 0.209 的最大改进（以 BPB 计），而 54 个任务中只有 13 个出现退化，且均未超过 0.035。对于 Stack-Edu 中编程语言的再平衡（下图），优化后的混合更偏好 Python 而非 Java 和 Markdown，在除两个编码基准外的所有基准上都取得了适度改进。表 38 进一步展示了我们方法的适应性：切换开发套件以强调 QA、数学或编码，会得到优先优化相应能力的混合。

Quality-aware upsampling

质量感知上采样


Source: p.19 S065

Original: The data mixing procedure described in the previous section determines optimal proportions across different data sources and topics, but does not account for quality variations within each topic. For web text sources like CommonCrawl, we initially derive these proportions from DCLM, which applies only flat filtering based on quality classifier scores. However, in a separate set of experiments, we found that quality-aware upsampling improves performance in data-constrained settings (see Appendix). For example, when constructing a 250B token mix from a 1T token pool, flat quality-filtering (as in DCLM) would simply select the top quartile. We achieve better results by upsampling the highest-quality data: including multiple copies of the top 5% and single copies of the remaining data to reach the target token count.

中文: 上一节所述的数据混合过程确定了不同数据源与主题之间的最优比例，但未考虑每个主题内部的质量差异。对于 CommonCrawl 等网页文本来源，我们最初从 DCLM 推导这些比例，而 DCLM 仅应用基于质量分类器分数的平坦过滤。然而，在一组独立的实验中，我们发现质量感知上采样在数据受限的设置下能改进性能（见附录）。例如，当从 1T 令牌池构建 250B 令牌混合时，平坦质量过滤（如 DCLM 那样）只会简单地选择前四分之一。我们通过上采样最高质量的数据取得更好的结果：纳入前 5% 数据的多份副本，以及其余数据的单份副本，以达到目标令牌数。

Fig. 10. 质量感知上采样曲线与平坦上采样曲线的对比

Placed near: p.19 S065
Source: p.20 C012

Original caption: Example of quality-aware upsampling curve compared to a flat upsampling curve. The x-axis denotes quality of data in terms of percentiles and the y-axis denotes how much the data is repeated. In this instance, the bottom 40% of data is discarded, and the top 5% of data is resampled 7 times.

中文图注: 质量感知上采样曲线与平坦上采样曲线的对比示例。横轴表示以百分位计的数据质量，纵轴表示数据被重复的程度。在此示例中，底部 40% 的数据被丢弃，而顶部 5% 的数据被重采样 7 次。

Reading note: 平坦过滤对应图中的阶跃函数；质量感知上采样对应单调递增曲线。


Source: p.20 S066

Original: We formalize this approach using upsampling curves, as in Figure 10. The x-axis represents data quality in percentiles, while the y-axis shows the upsampling factor. Flat filtering corresponds to a step function on this plot, and quality-based upsampling would correspond to a monotonically increasing curve. For the purposes of generating a training data corpus, we generate separate upsampling curves for each of the 24 WebOrganizer-defined topics in our web text pool. The integral of each curve determines the total tokens extracted from that topic: for example, an integral of 2.0 indicates an average upsampling rate of 2x, yielding twice the token count from that data bucket. To define an upsampling curve for each web text topic bucket, we leverage three constraints: 1) the optimal topic proportion, as determined by the mixing experiments; 2) the total desired training duration in terms of tokens; and 3) a maximum upsampling factor of 7 (empirically determined). The first two of these constraints control the target integral (average upsampling rate) for each topic bucket. The third constraint dictates an upper bound on the upsampling curve. Given these constraints, we can search over the space of curves to find a parametric curve that meets these constraints, which becomes the upsampling curve for this topic-bucket.

中文: 我们使用如图 10 所示的上采样曲线来形式化这一方法。横轴表示以百分位计的数据质量，纵轴表示上采样因子。平坦过滤对应图中的阶跃函数，而基于质量的上采样对应单调递增曲线。为生成训练数据语料，我们为网页文本池中 24 个由 WebOrganizer 定义的主题各自生成单独的上采样曲线。每条曲线的积分决定了从该主题提取的总令牌数：例如，积分为 2.0 表示平均上采样率为 2 倍，即从该数据桶产出的令牌数翻倍。为每个网页文本主题桶定义上采样曲线，我们利用三个约束：1）由混合实验确定的最优主题比例；2）以令牌计的期望总训练时长；3）最大上采样因子 7（经验确定）。前两个约束控制每个主题桶的目标积分（平均上采样率）。第三个约束规定了上采样曲线的上限。给定这些约束，我们可以在曲线空间中搜索一条满足约束的参数化曲线，它就成为该主题桶的上采样曲线。


Source: p.20 S067

Original: In practice, our data is organized into discrete quality buckets that partition the quality percentile range. For each quality bucket, we compute its upsampling rate by integrating the upsampling curve over the corresponding percentile interval and dividing by the interval width. More details regarding this procedure can be found in Appendix §A.2.

中文: 在实践中，我们的数据被组织为离散的质量桶，将质量百分位区间进行划分。对每个质量桶，我们通过在对应百分位区间上对上采样曲线求积分、再除以区间宽度来计算其重采样率。此过程的更多细节见附录 §A.2。

Evaluation during pretraining

预训练期间的评测


Source: p.20 S068

Original: It can be difficult to obtain a reliable estimate of model performance in the middle of a pretraining run, since the quality of a run is highly influenced by the learning rate (see OLMo et al. (2024), Section 4.1). For a 7B model, we can anneal the learning rate to zero at regular intervals throughout training to assess progress, but this is prohibitively expensive for a 32B model. To monitor performance of our 32B model during the training run, we use the technique from Li et al. (2025), and average the weights from four checkpoints, chosen 1,000 steps apart at regular intervals.

中文: 在预训练运行中途获得可靠的模型性能估计可能很困难，因为一次运行的质量高度受学习率影响（见 OLMo et al. (2024) 第 4.1 节）。对于 7B 模型，我们可以在训练过程中每隔固定间隔将学习率退火到零来评估进展，但这对于 32B 模型而言成本高得难以承受。为在训练运行期间监控 32B 模型的性能，我们使用 Li et al. (2025) 的技术，将四个检查点的权重取平均，这些检查点在固定间隔下相隔 1000 步选取。

Fig. 3. Olmo 3 Base 7B 的学习率调度与损失

Placed near: p.20 S068
Source: p.11 C013

Original caption: Learning rate schedule and loss for Olmo 3 Base 7B. The first half of the learning rate schedule is a cosine schedule over 5T tokens. We stretch the second half of the schedule to reach a target length of one epoch (5.93T tokens). Warm-up is 2000 steps, the peak learning rate is 3 × 10⁻⁴, and the final learning rate is 10% of the peak LR.

中文图注: Olmo 3 Base 7B 的学习率调度与损失。学习率调度的前半部分是 5T 令牌上的余弦调度。我们拉长调度的后半部分以达到一个 epoch（5.93T 令牌）的目标长度。预热为 2000 步，峰值学习率为 3 × 10⁻⁴，最终学习率为峰值 LR 的 10%。

Reading note: 该图展示 7B 预训练的余弦学习率曲线及对应损失。

Fig. 4. Olmo 3 Base 32B 的学习率调度与损失

Placed near: p.20 S068
Source: p.11 C014

Original caption: Learning rate schedule and loss for Olmo 3 Base 32B. The learning rate schedule is a cosine schedule over one epoch (5.93T tokens), truncated at 5.5T tokens. Warm-up is 2000 steps, and the peak learning rate is 6 × 10⁻⁴. The schedule targets a final learning rate of 10% of the peak. Due to the truncation, the real final learning rate is 6.210 × 10⁻⁵. Unintuitively, the learning rate for the 32B is higher than for the 7B, but this is somewhat compensated for by the larger batch size of the 32B (8M tokens vs. 4M tokens per batch).

中文图注: Olmo 3 Base 32B 的学习率调度与损失。学习率调度是一个 epoch（5.93T 令牌）上的余弦调度，截断于 5.5T 令牌。预热为 2000 步，峰值学习率为 6 × 10⁻⁴。该调度目标最终学习率为峰值的 10%。由于截断，实际最终学习率为 6.210 × 10⁻⁵。反直觉的是，32B 的学习率高于 7B，但这在一定程度上被 32B 更大的批量（每批 8M 令牌 vs 4M 令牌）所补偿。

Reading note: 该图展示 32B 预训练的余弦学习率曲线及对应损失；32B 的峰值学习率（6×10⁻⁴）高于 7B（3×10⁻⁴）。

3.5 Stage 2: Midtraining

3.5 阶段 2：中期训练


Source: p.20 S069

Original: After pretraining, Olmo 3 Base is further trained to improve key fundamental capabilities. During this midtrain stage, we use 100B high-quality tokens sampled from a brand new data pool we introduce in this work, Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix. This midtraining data significantly expands and improves upon OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix, which we curated for our previous model OLMo 2. The improvement comes from two key elements: (i) a new two-part methodological framework combining 1) lightweight, distributed feedback loops on individual data sources, with 2) centralized integration tests to assess candidate mixes on base model quality and post-trainability; (ii) expansion to targeted data curation efforts across code, math, and general knowledge QA domains (broadening from the math-focused efforts in OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix); (iii) more intentional inclusion of data types—instruction data and thinking traces—to lay groundwork for supporting post-training of Olmo 3 Think, Olmo 3 Instruct, and Olmo 3 RL-Zero models.

中文: 在预训练之后，Olmo 3 Base 被进一步训练以改进关键基础能力。在此中期训练阶段，我们使用 100B 高质量令牌，它们采样自我们在本工作中提出的全新数据池 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix。这一中期训练数据显著扩展并改进了我们为此前模型 OLMo 2 所整理的 OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix。改进来自三个关键要素：（i）一个新的两部分方法学框架，将 1）针对单个数据源的轻量级分布式反馈回路，与 2）集中式集成测试（用于评估候选混合在基座模型质量与后训练能力上的表现）结合起来；（ii）将定向数据整理扩展到代码、数学和通用知识问答领域（相比 OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix 专注数学的努力有所拓宽）；（iii）更有意识地纳入指令数据与思维轨迹这两类数据，为支持 Olmo 3 Think、Olmo 3 Instruct 和 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 模型的后训练打下基础。


Source: p.22 S070

Original: The resulting midtraining data is a diverse mixture that combines novel synthetic sources with data from the pretraining stage, but quality-filtered and rewritten to better suit capabilities we target at this stage. Through midtraining, we achieve improvements across the board in our target capability domains, as well as improvements in performance resulting from subsequent SFT training.

中文: 由此得到的中期训练数据是一个多样化的混合，将新颖的合成来源与预训练阶段的数据相结合，但经过了质量过滤与重写，以更好地适配我们在该阶段所针对的能力。通过中期训练，我们在目标能力领域全面取得了改进，同时也取得了后续 SFT 训练所带来的性能提升。

We employ a distributed system of lightweight feedback loops to explore datasets for targeted boosts across capabilities, and combine these with centralized integration tests and SFT training for assessment of candidate mix quality (discussion in Section §3.5.1). 3.5.1 Methodological framework

3.5.1 方法学框架


Source: p.22 S071

Original: Targeted capability boosts — In the midtraining stage, we aim to make targeted improvements to capabilities spanning a wide range of domains: prioritizing significant gains in code and math, but also aiming for focused improvements in QA and general knowledge access capabilities, and to lay groundwork for instruction and thinking capabilities in post-training. This requires a lightweight, distributed framework for dataset testing, to allow us to investigate many domains of datasets efficiently and in parallel (Figure 11). For lightweight testing we use the microanneal methodology introduced with OLMo 2, which we further modify for more systematic baselining. For a standard microanneal we use the following setup: 1) select a target dataset, 2) sample 5B tokens, 3) match this with 5B web tokens, 4) anneal on the resulting 10B mix. We then compare the performance of the resulting checkpoint against that of a baseline microanneal on 10B web-only data, for a cheap and efficient assessment of the impact of the dataset on base model performance, over and above the impact of continued training on web data alone.

中文: 定向能力提升——在中期训练阶段，我们的目标是对跨越广泛领域的能力进行定向改进：优先在代码和数学上取得显著收益，同时也追求在 QA 和通用知识访问能力上的集中改进，并为后训练中的指令与思考能力奠定基础。这需要一个轻量级、分布式的数据集测试框架，使我们能够高效、并行地研究多个数据域（图 11）。对于轻量级测试，我们使用随 OLMo 2 引入的微退火（microanneal）方法，并对其做了进一步修改以实现更系统的基线对比。对于标准微退火，我们使用如下设置：1）选择一个目标数据集；2）采样 5B 令牌；3）将其与 5B 网页令牌匹配；4）在得到的 10B 混合上退火。随后，我们将所得检查点的性能与在 10B 纯网页数据上的基线微退火进行对比，从而廉价而高效地评估该数据集对基座模型性能的影响——即在仅持续训练网页数据的影响之上的额外影响。


Source: p.22 S072

Original: This methodology allows us to make rapid, targeted assessments of the quality of datasets being considered for the midtraining mix, and to iterate on many data domains in parallel. Our workflow operates as follows: for each capability that we target for improvement (in categories of math, code, QA, instruction, and thinking), we generate or collect new datasets as candidates to boost performance for this capability; we assess each via microanneals—if the results are promising, new datasets can be incorporated into the larger integration tests described next.

中文: 这一方法使我们能够快速、定向地评估被考虑纳入中期训练混合的数据集的质量，并并行地迭代许多数据域。我们的工作流如下：对于我们针对改进的每项能力（分为数学、代码、QA、指令和思考等类别），我们生成或收集新数据集作为提升该能力的候选；我们通过微退火对每个进行评估——如果结果有前景，新数据集就可以纳入下述更大规模的集成测试。


Source: p.22 S073

Original: Integration tests — In parallel with the microanneal process, we conduct integration tests involving full annealing runs on candidate mixes for the 100B-token midtraining mix. These integration tests evaluate how candidate data sources perform when combined together; further, we can assess the effect of longer 100B midtrain runs (as compared to shorter, 5–10B tokens used in microanneals). Finally, checkpoints from integration runs can be quickly instruction-tuned and evaluated on the post-train eval suite; we use this additional step to verify that gains we observe in midtrain yield improvements beyond base model capabilities. We run these integration tests periodically as we reach a critical mass of microanneal results for new candidate data sources. For each integration test, new sources that show promise in microanneals are incorporated into an updated 100B mix, retaining strong sources from previous iterations. We carry out five major rounds of integration tests; we report three in this manuscript: Round 1, Round 3, and Round 5. Round 5 folds in the newly-developed decontamination process (Section §3.5.3). For each mix we evaluate the resulting midtrained model on our OlmoBaseEval Main evaluation suite, and additionally run the midtrained model through SFT for post-training assessment.

中文: 集成测试——与微退火过程并行，我们对 100B 令牌中期训练混合的候选混合执行完整的退火运行，进行集成测试。这些集成测试评估候选数据源组合在一起时的表现；此外，我们还能评估更长的 100B 中期训练运行（相对于微退火中更短的 5–10B 令牌）的效果。最后，集成运行的检查点可以快速做指令微调并在后训练评测套件上评测；我们用这一额外步骤来验证中期训练中观察到的收益能带来超越基座模型能力的改进。当新候选数据源的微退火结果达到临界规模时，我们定期运行这些集成测试。对于每次集成测试，在微退火中显示出前景的新来源会被纳入更新后的 100B 混合，同时保留前几轮迭代中的强来源。我们执行了五个主要轮次的集成测试；本文报告其中三轮：Round 1、Round 3 和 Round 5。Round 5 纳入了新开发的去污染过程（第 §3.5.3 节）。对每个混合，我们在 OlmoBaseEval Main 评测套件上评测所得的中期训练模型，并额外让中期训练模型经过 SFT 以进行后训练评估。

3.5.2 Capability Improvements for Final Data Mix

3.5.2 最终数据混合的能力改进


Source: p.22 S074

Original: With Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix, we target five core capabilities during midtraining: improved math and coding, better knowledge elicitation through QA, and bootstrapping instruction following and reasoning ability ahead of post-training stages. To maintain continuity with pretraining, we keep web and PDF data from the first stage of Olmo 3, albeit after filtering for higher quality documents; this approach prevents excessive shift in training data distribution. Table 5 outlines the composition of the final mix, which includes a combination of newly-introduced synthetic data and refinements of existing data. Below we give an overview, for each capability category, of our curation efforts and final selected data. Additional details are in Appendix A.3.

中文: 借助 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix，我们在中期训练期间针对五项核心能力：改进数学与编码、通过 QA 更好地激发知识、以及在进入后训练阶段之前引导指令遵循与推理能力。为保持与预训练的连续性，我们保留了 Olmo 3 第一阶段中的网页与 PDF 数据（尽管经过了针对更高质量文档的过滤）；这一做法防止了训练数据分布的过度偏移。表 5 概述了最终混合的组成，它结合了新引入的合成数据与对现有数据的精炼。下面我们按能力类别概述我们的整理工作与最终选定数据。更多细节见附录 A.3。


Source: p.23 S075

Original: Math capabilities — For math capability, we expand efforts from OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix. We consider a total of 25 data sources, which we evaluate over 80 microanneal runs. We ultimately settle on a combination of 5 top math-specific sources, 4 of which were newly synthesized. For high-performing existing datasets without permissive licensing, we synthesize new data modeled after those datasets. We will outline and briefly summarize the math-targeted data sources that are included in the final mix. More details about data generation procedure and microanneal results can be found in the Appendix. Dolmino-1 math — We include the entirety of the 10.7B-token OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix Math subset. The version we use differs from the original only in additional filtering for decontamination. As described for OLMo 2 (OLMo et al., 2024), this set was generated to lift general-purpose math capabilities, measured in terms of improvements on the GSM8K test set. A 10B microanneal, using 5B of the available 10.7B tokens in isolation, achieves a lift of 10.4 points in MATH and 38.2 points in the GSM8K benchmark.

中文: 数学能力——对于数学能力，我们扩展了 OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix 的努力。我们总共考虑了 25 个数据源，并通过 80 次微退火运行对它们进行评估。我们最终选定 5 个顶尖数学专用来源的组合，其中 4 个是新合成的。对于性能强劲但缺乏宽松许可的现有数据集，我们仿照这些数据集合成新数据。下面概述并简要总结纳入最终混合的数学定向数据源。关于数据生成过程与微退火结果的更多细节见附录。Dolmino-1 math——我们完整纳入了 10.7B 令牌的 OLMo 2 Dolmino Mix Math 子集。我们使用的版本与原始版本仅在额外的去污染过滤上有所不同。如 OLMo 2（OLMo et al., 2024）所述，该集合的生成目的是提升通用数学能力，以 GSM8K 测试集的改进来衡量。一次使用可用 10.7B 令牌中 5B 的 10B 微退火，实现了 MATH 上 10.4 分、GSM8K 上 38.2 分的提升。


Source: p.23 S076

Original: TinyMATH — For each of the 7500 examples in the MATH training set, we generate 100 new, similar problems. We then create Python code solutions to the newly generated problems (TinyMATH-PoT), and two flavors of conversational English discussing these solutions (TinyMATH-MIND). In aggregate, this yields 1.14B tokens of novel, synthetic data targeted to improve performance on the MATH benchmark. A microanneal consisting of all of these new tokens in a 50/50 ratio with web data yields 13.2 points of improvement in the MATH benchmark and 13.9 points in GSM8K. CraneMath — The recently published SwallowMath dataset (Fujii et al., 2025) demonstrates the potential of rewriting already finely-curated naturally-occurring mathematical web data—in this case, FineMath4+ (Allal et al., 2025). We corroborate this strong performance with a microanneal over SwallowMath that showed a lift of 16.0 points in MATH and 24.5 points in GSM8K using only 3.6B high quality tokens. Because SwallowMath comes with additional license restrictions—having been generated with the Llama suite of models—we generate an independent reproduction of SwallowMath by rewriting FineMath4+ with the SwallowMath prompt, using Qwen3 (Yang et al., 2025a) for generation. We denote this new mix as CraneMath, which yields 5.6B tokens of high-quality math. Microanneals demonstrate a lift of 18.5 points in MATH and 27.4 points in GSM8K. MegaMatt — Similar to SwallowMath, Megamath-Web-Pro-Max (Wang et al., 2025) applies Llama rewrites to naturally-occurring mathematical web text—in this case a filtered version of MegaMath-Web (Zhou et al., 2025). Our microannealing procedure demonstrates that MegaMath-Web-Pro-Max was able to improve MATH by 7.0 points and GSM8K by 13.3 points using only 5B tokens of high-quality data. However, in order to use this dataset, we re-generate it using open source models. Specifically, we collect the Megamath-Web-Pro data occurring after June 2023, apply filtering as in Megamath-Web-Pro-Max, and rewrite it using Qwen3 (Yang et al., 2025a). This yields 3.88B tokens of high-quality data, which we refer to as MegaMatt. In microanneals, this data yields a lift of 8.0 points in MATH and 13.0 points in GSM8K.

中文: TinyMATH——对 MATH 训练集中的 7500 个示例，我们各生成 100 道新的相似题目。随后我们为新生成的题目创建 Python 代码解答（TinyMATH-PoT），以及两种讨论这些解答的对话式英文（TinyMATH-MIND）。总计得到 1.14B 令牌的新颖合成数据，旨在提升 MATH 基准上的表现。一次由所有这些新令牌与网页数据按 50/50 比例构成的微退火，在 MATH 基准上取得 13.2 分改进、在 GSM8K 上取得 13.9 分改进。CraneMath——最近发布的 SwallowMath 数据集（Fujii et al., 2025）展示了重写已精心整理的自然数学网页数据（此处为 FineMath4+，Allal et al., 2025）的潜力。我们通过对 SwallowMath 的微退火印证了其强劲性能：仅用 3.6B 高质量令牌就在 MATH 上取得 16.0 分、在 GSM8K 上取得 24.5 分的提升。由于 SwallowMath 附带额外许可限制（其使用 Llama 系列模型生成），我们通过用 SwallowMath 提示词重写 FineMath4+、并使用 Qwen3（Yang et al., 2025a）生成，来独立复现 SwallowMath。我们将这一新混合记为 CraneMath，它产出 5.6B 令牌的高质量数学数据。微退火显示其在 MATH 上取得 18.5 分、GSM8K 上取得 27.4 分的提升。MegaMatt——与 SwallowMath 类似，Megamath-Web-Pro-Max（Wang et al., 2025）对自然出现的数学网页文本（此处为 MegaMath-Web 的过滤版本，Zhou et al., 2025）应用 Llama 重写。我们的微退火过程表明，Megamath-Web-Pro-Max 仅用 5B 令牌高质量数据就能将 MATH 提升 7.0 分、GSM8K 提升 13.3 分。然而，为使用该数据集，我们使用开源模型重新生成它。具体而言，我们收集 2023 年 6 月之后的 Megamath-Web-Pro 数据，按 Megamath-Web-Pro-Max 的方式过滤，并使用 Qwen3（Yang et al., 2025a）重写。这产出 3.88B 令牌的高质量数据，我们称之为 MegaMatt。在微退火中，该数据在 MATH 上取得 8.0 分、GSM8K 上取得 13.0 分的提升。


Source: p.23 S077

Original: Code capabilities — Our efforts to improve code capabilities include two major threads: 1) curation of higher-quality general code data, and 2) introduction of fill-in-the-middle (FIM) code capabilities. The top-performing datasets included in the final mix are the following: Stack-Edu (FIM) — We include a modified version of Stack-Edu, in which 50% of documents reflect fill-in-the-middle (FIM) transformation via the infilling procedure from StarCoder2 (Lozhkov et al., 2024). This transformation splits code documents into prefix, middle, and suffix segments in order to train on prediction of the concealed middle segment. To further improve the quality of this code data, we apply quality filtering by performing reservoir sampling and bucketing of documents based on educational value score, followed by weighted random sampling of the upper 20% of buckets from each language subset. Microanneals validate that this quality filtering combined with the sampling procedure improves code benchmark performance over both the natural distribution of Stack-Edu and more naive sampling procedures such as sampling the top document per language based on classifier score.

中文: 代码能力——我们改进代码能力的努力包含两条主线：1）整理更高质量的通用代码数据；2）引入中间填充（FIM）代码能力。纳入最终混合的表现最佳的数据集如下：Stack-Edu（FIM）——我们纳入 Stack-Edu 的修改版本，其中 50% 的文档通过 StarCoder2（Lozhkov et al., 2024）的填充过程反映中间填充（FIM）变换。该变换将代码文档拆分为前缀、中间和后缀片段，以训练对隐藏中间片段的预测。为进一步提升该代码数据的质量，我们通过基于教育价值分数的蓄水池采样与分桶来应用质量过滤，随后对每种语言子集中前 20% 的桶进行加权随机采样。微退火验证了这一质量过滤与采样过程的组合，在代码基准上的表现优于 Stack-Edu 的自然分布，也优于更朴素的采样方式（如按分类器分数对每种语言采样排名靠前的文档）。


Source: p.24 S078

Original: CraneCode — As with our math datasets, we find strong performance from the SwallowCode dataset, and generate a permissively-licensed recreation for use in our midtraining. Like Fujii et al. (2025), we source data from the Python subset of the-stack-v2-smol, then filter for syntax errors and filter based on linter outputs. Then, we apply the SwallowCode two-stage rewriting pipeline, with one stage to augment style, and another to optimize the code itself. This yields 18.8B tokens of high-quality python code. In a microanneal using 5B tokens of high-quality data, CraneCode results in a lift in HumanEval of 5.0 points relative to pre-anneal baseline, compared to the 10.3 seen for SwallowCode. When using a larger microanneal with 12.5B tokens of CraneCode, the lift in HumanEval improves to 13.5.

中文: CraneCode——与我们的数学数据集一样，我们发现 SwallowCode 数据集性能强劲，并生成一个许可宽松的复刻版用于我们的中期训练。与 Fujii et al. (2025) 一样，我们从 the-stack-v2-smol 的 Python 子集获取数据，然后过滤语法错误，并基于 linter 输出进行过滤。随后，我们应用 SwallowCode 的两阶段重写流水线，一个阶段增强风格，另一个阶段优化代码本身。这产出 18.8B 令牌的高质量 Python 代码。在一次使用 5B 令牌高质量数据的微退火中，CraneCode 相对于退火前基线在 HumanEval 上取得 5.0 分的提升，而 SwallowCode 为 10.3。当使用更大的、含 12.5B 令牌 CraneCode 的微退火时，HumanEval 的提升达到 13.5。


Source: p.24 S079

Original: QA and knowledge access capabilities — We target improvements in question-answering and general knowledge access capabilities through synthesis of two novel datasets focused on particular QA capabilities, as well as inclusion of high-quality existing QA data. The final datasets included for these capabilities are the following: Reddit-to-Flashcards — We synthesize this dataset in response to the need to handle diverse content categories and question structures in multiple-choice QA tasks. We first identify a subset of academically-relevant subreddits, and then use GPT 4o-mini to rewrite submission-comment pairs from those subreddits into multiple-choice QA pairs. We use seven task formats to increase diversity. Microanneals show that inclusion of 5B tokens of this data in a 10B-token microanneal resulted in over 2 points of improvement in the MCNon-STEM task cluster—relative to a 10B-token web-only baseline microanneal—with 3 points of improvement in MMLU. Wiki-to-RCQA — We synthesize this dataset in response to the need for improvements in passage-based reading comprehension QA. We collect Wikipedia passages and prompted Qwen2.5 32B Instruct to generate QA pairs based on these passages, meeting a range of constraints inspired by instructions given to annotators of reading comprehension QA datasets. Microanneals show that 4.2B tokens of this data in a 10B microanneal results in nearly 2 points of improvement in the GenQA task cluster relative to a 10B web-only baseline, with improvements focused on the DROP, SQuAD and CoQA reading comprehension QA benchmarks. Nemotron — We include the “diverse QA pairs” synth subset of the Nemotron CC dataset (Su et al., 2025a), as, in microanneals, it improved GenQA tasks by 1.5 points, MCNon-STEM by 1.9 points, and it had equal MCSTEM performance compared to a microanneal run of web documents from the top quality (5%) bucket. All other Nemotron synth subsets performed worse than natural data, so we did not use them.

中文: QA 与知识访问能力——我们通过合成两个聚焦特定 QA 能力的新数据集，并纳入高质量现有 QA 数据，来改进问答与通用知识访问能力。纳入这些能力的最终数据集如下：Reddit-to-Flashcards——我们合成该数据集，以应对多项选择 QA 任务中多样内容类别与问题结构的需求。我们首先确定一组与学术相关的 subreddit，然后使用 GPT 4o-mini 将这些 subreddit 中的发帖-评论对重写为多项选择 QA 对。我们使用七种任务格式以增加多样性。微退火显示，在 10B 令牌微退火中纳入该数据的 5B 令牌，相对于 10B 纯网页基线微退火，在 MCNon-STEM 任务簇上取得超过 2 分的改进，在 MMLU 上取得 3 分改进。Wiki-to-RCQA——我们合成该数据集，以应对基于篇章的阅读理解 QA 的改进需求。我们收集 Wikipedia 篇章，并提示 Qwen2.5 32B Instruct 基于这些篇章生成 QA 对，满足一系列受阅读理解 QA 数据集标注者说明所启发的约束。微退火显示，10B 微退火中纳入该数据的 4.2B 令牌，相对于 10B 纯网页基线，在 GenQA 任务簇上取得近 2 分改进，改进集中于 DROP、SQuAD 和 CoQA 阅读理解 QA 基准。Nemotron——我们纳入 Nemotron CC 数据集（Su et al., 2025a）的「多样化 QA 对」合成子集，因为在微退火中，它使 GenQA 任务提升 1.5 分、MCNon-STEM 提升 1.9 分，并且其 MCSTEM 表现与使用顶部质量（5%）桶网页文档的微退火相当。所有其他 Nemotron 合成子集的表现均不如自然数据，因此我们未使用它们。


Source: p.24 S080

Original: Cross-Capability instruction data — To lay the groundwork for post-training, we include cross-domain instruction datasets to prime models for instruction-tuning. Tulu3 SFT data — We sample instruction data from the SFT set from Tülu 3. Compared to the dataset released by Lambert et al. (2024), we lightly process these data as follows: 1) we use an expanded set of examples that were created and subsequently filtered out for the final Tülu 3 data, 2) instead of relying on post-train syntax, such as <|im_start|> and <|im_end|>, we concatenate messages using double newlines. We choose this format, rather than using special tokens after microanneal experiments comparing them. More details are provided in the discussion of special tokens in Section §3.5.4. Flan — Through microanneals, we also find the Flan dataset (Wei et al., 2021; Longpre et al., 2023) improves performance in QA tasks, and as a result included a subset of the Flan dataset in the final mix. We use the same subset and preprocessing from OLMo 2 (OLMo et al., 2024).

中文: 跨能力指令数据——为给后训练奠定基础，我们纳入跨域指令数据集，为模型的指令微调做准备。Tulu3 SFT 数据——我们从 Tülu 3 的 SFT 集合中采样指令数据。与 Lambert et al. (2024) 发布的数据集相比，我们对其做了如下轻度处理：1）我们使用一组扩展的示例，这些示例原本为最终 Tülu 3 数据创建、随后被过滤掉；2）我们不依赖 <|im_start|> 和 <|im_end|> 等后训练语法，而是使用双换行来连接消息。我们在比较二者的微退火实验之后选择了这种格式，而非使用特殊令牌。更多细节见第 §3.5.4 节关于特殊令牌的讨论。Flan——通过微退火，我们还发现 Flan 数据集（Wei et al., 2021; Longpre et al., 2023）能改进 QA 任务的表现，因此在最终混合中纳入了一个 Flan 数据集的子集。我们使用与 OLMo 2（OLMo et al., 2024）相同的子集与预处理。


Source: p.24 S081

Original: Cross-capability thinking traces — We also curate a diverse collection of thinking traces across a variety of domains to lay the foundation for Olmo 3 Think and Olmo 3 RL-Zero. This includes two new synthetic datasets, as well as rewritten and filtered versions of existing thinking trace datasets. Meta-reasoning — The first of the two new datasets introduced in this work; we create it to target seven core cognitive capabilities from Kargupta et al. (2025) that are foundational to mathematical and programming expertise: self-awareness, evaluation, goal management, hierarchical organization, backward chaining, backtracking, and conceptual reasoning. These categories are inspired by work suggesting that meta-reasoning capabilities in base models may be associated with superior reinforcement learning trajectories (Kargupta et al., 2025; Gandhi et al., 2025). We express these capabilities into tasks that require leveraging meta-reasoning, such as backtracking from an answer back to its original math problem, or debugging a program. To generate our meta-reasoning data for each of these tasks, we synthetically augmented existing math and code problems with detailed annotations such as “problem classification”, “difficulty analysis”, “solution approaches”, “common pitfalls”, and “verification methods”, modeled after the Pandalla-Math dataset. Using these annotations as foundation, we prompt GPT-4.1 and o4-mini to generate thinking traces for each capability-targeted task. Microanneals show that inclusion of this data results in substantial improvements to math and coding tasks, resulting in approximately 14 points of boost—relative to a strong math/code baseline microanneal—in Minerva Math, and 14 and 20 points of boost on Codex HumanEval and MBPP benchmarks, respectively.

中文: 跨能力思维轨迹——我们还整理了一系列跨多个领域的多样化思维轨迹，为 Olmo 3 Think 和 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 奠定基础。这包括两个新的合成数据集，以及现有思维轨迹数据集的重写与过滤版本。Meta-reasoning（元推理）——这是本工作中引入的两个新数据集中的第一个；我们创建它来针对 Kargupta et al. (2025) 提出的、构成数学与编程专长基础的七项核心认知能力：自我意识、评估、目标管理、层级组织、逆向链（backward chaining）、回溯（backtracking）与概念推理。这些类别受到如下工作的启发：基座模型中的元推理能力可能与更优的强化学习轨迹相关（Kargupta et al., 2025; Gandhi et al., 2025）。我们将这些能力表达为需要运用元推理的任务，例如从答案回溯到其原始数学问题，或调试程序。为每个任务生成元推理数据时，我们仿照 Pandalla-Math 数据集，用「问题分类」「难度分析」「求解思路」「常见陷阱」「验证方法」等详细标注对现有数学与代码问题做合成增强。以这些标注为基础，我们提示 GPT-4.1 和 o4-mini 为每个针对能力定制的任务生成思维轨迹。微退火显示，纳入该数据会给数学与编码任务带来显著改进——相对于强数学/代码基线微退火，在 Minerva Math 上取得约 14 分提升，在 Codex HumanEval 和 MBPP 基准上分别取得 14 分和 20 分提升。


Source: p.25 S082

Original: Program-verifiable data — Our second new synthetic reasoning dataset consists of program-verifiable tasks (Zeng et al., 2025b) for which we can use a Python program to deterministically verify whether an answer to a problem is correct. Solving these problems naturally requires a wide range of meta-reasoning strategies that are well-suited to be learned during midtraining. We 1) programmatically generate these problems, 2) distill thinking traces from GPT-4.1 and o4-mini models, and 3) finally filter those for correctness using an output verifier (Python programs). Microanneals show that including about 250M verifiable data tokens (in a 5B microanneal) led to 1-2 points of improvement on math and code tasks, including GSM8K and MBPP, relative to a math/code microanneal baseline.

中文: 程序可验证数据——我们的第二个新合成推理数据集由程序可验证任务（Zeng et al., 2025b）组成，对这些任务，我们可以用 Python 程序确定性地验证某个问题答案是否正确。解决这些问题自然需要广泛的元推理策略，非常适合在中期训练期间学习。我们 1）以编程方式生成这些问题；2）从 GPT-4.1 和 o4-mini 模型蒸馏思维轨迹；3）最后使用输出验证器（Python 程序）过滤其正确性。微退火显示，在 5B 微退火中纳入约 250M 可验证数据令牌，相对于数学/代码微退火基线，在数学与代码任务（包括 GSM8K 和 MBPP）上带来 1–2 分的改进。


Source: p.25 S083

Original: OMR rewrite full-thoughts — We also consider 9 different versions of rewriting of the OpenMathReasoning dataset (Moshkov et al., 2025), and find top performance for what we call the Full-Thoughts rewrite. This is a light rewrite of the OpenMathReasoning dataset, instructing GPT-4.1 to edit items for clarity, flow, and formatting (e.g., converting to LaTeX) while preserving all reasoning, explanations, and thoughts of the original. In microanneals, training on all 850M OMR Full-Thoughts tokens and an equal amount of web text, we see a lift of 5.5 points in the MATH benchmark and 8.4 in GSM8K. Existing thinking traces — We also draw on a variety of existing synthetic thinking trace datasets, to which we apply a range of filtering steps to reduce noise and increase quality. These sources have coverage over a broad variety of domains, including math, code, natural sciences, social sciences, humanities, and puzzles. These datasets are listed in Table 5, and more details are provided in Appendix A.3. Microanneals show that inclusion of these datasets yielded improvements especially in math and code domains, with improvements of up to 8 points in GSM8K, and approximately 2 points in HumanEval and MBPP, relative to a math/code microanneal baseline.

中文: OMR 重写全思考（Full-Thoughts）——我们还考虑了 OpenMathReasoning 数据集（Moshkov et al., 2025）的 9 种不同重写版本，并发现我们称之为 Full-Thoughts 重写的版本性能最佳。这是对 OpenMathReasoning 数据集的轻度重写，指示 GPT-4.1 为了清晰度、流畅性与格式（例如转换为 LaTeX）而编辑条目，同时保留原始的所有推理、解释与思考。在微退火中，用全部 850M OMR Full-Thoughts 令牌与等量网页文本训练，我们在 MATH 基准上看到 5.5 分、在 GSM8K 上看到 8.4 分的提升。现有思维轨迹——我们还利用了多种现有的合成思维轨迹数据集，并对其应用一系列过滤步骤以减少噪声、提升质量。这些来源覆盖了广泛领域，包括数学、代码、自然科学、社会科学、人文学科与谜题。这些数据集列于表 5，更多细节见附录 A.3。微退火显示，纳入这些数据集尤其在数学与代码领域带来了改进——相对于数学/代码微退火基线，GSM8K 上最多提升 8 分，HumanEval 和 MBPP 上约提升 2 分。


Source: p.25 S084

Original: Table 10 provides further results showing the impacts of inclusion of instruction and thinking data in our midtraining mix, at the level of full integration tests. High quality web and PDF data — Finally, we include three types of web / pretraining data to avoid skewing too far from the pretraining distribution. Stage 1 web data — We sample documents from the top two quality buckets (top 10% quality). We sample according to natural distribution, not the optimal ratio described in Appendix §A.2.4. In tests, the optimal ratio from the pretrain stage results in no improvement over natural distribution; since it introduces additional implementation complexity, we abandon it for the midtraining stage. Stage 1 olmOCR science PDFs — From our PDF documents (Section §3.4.2) we create a further filtered version, which we use both for midtraining and for long-context extension. Instead of discussing details here, the reader will have to hold their breath till Section §3.6.1. Stem-heavy crawl — We also create a separate high-quality web collection, crawled between September 12, 2024 and June 3rd, 2025 using our in-house crawler. The crawler ingested scientific, educational, and general domains based on domain-level seeds sourced from manual lists of websites deemed high value. We use the same crawling policy described for olmOCR science PDFs (Section §3.4.2). Through microanneal experiments, we choose to filter this set using the quality classifier introduced in Section §3.4.1; in detail, we use a threshold score of 0.6, which corresponds to the top 2.83% of the data we crawled, and would put these sources in the top 0.79% of web data in the Dolma 3 pool. Relative to a web-only baseline, our crawled data yields an improvement of approximately 2 points each for MCNon-STEM, MCSTEM, and Math subsets of OlmoBaseEval.

中文: 表 10 提供了进一步结果，在完整集成测试层面展示了在中期训练混合中纳入指令与思维数据的影响。高质量网页与 PDF 数据——最后，我们纳入三类网页/预训练数据，以避免过于偏离预训练分布。阶段 1 网页数据——我们从顶部两个质量桶（前 10% 质量）采样文档。我们按自然分布采样，而非附录 §A.2.4 中描述的最优比例。在测试中，预训练阶段的最优比例相对于自然分布并无改进；由于它引入了额外的实现复杂度，我们在中期训练阶段放弃了它。阶段 1 olmOCR 科学 PDF——从我们的 PDF 文档（第 §3.4.2 节）中，我们创建一个进一步过滤的版本，既用于中期训练，也用于长上下文扩展。这里不展开细节，读者需留待第 §3.6.1 节。STEM 密集爬取——我们还创建了一个独立的高质量网页集合，使用我们内部的爬虫于 2024 年 9 月 12 日至 2025 年 6 月 3 日期间爬取。该爬虫基于从人工认定的高价值网站清单中获取的域级种子，采集科学、教育和通用领域内容。我们使用与 olmOCR 科学 PDF（第 §3.4.2 节）所述相同的爬取策略。通过微退火实验，我们选择使用第 §3.4.1 节引入的质量分类器过滤该集合；具体而言，我们使用 0.6 的阈值分数，它对应我们所爬取数据的前 2.83%，并使这些来源进入 Dolma 3 池中网页数据的前 0.79%。相对于纯网页基线，我们爬取的数据在 OlmoBaseEval 的 MCNon-STEM、MCSTEM 和 Math 子集上各取得约 2 分的改进。

3.5.3 Decontamination

3.5.3 去污染


Source: p.26 S085

Original: Earlier Olmo models have enabled research on benchmark contamination in base model training, such as decontamination of perplexity evaluations (Magnusson et al., 2024) or measuring the impact of quality filters on evaluation leakage (Godey et al., 2025). In Olmo 3 midtraining we use a decontamination tool to ensure minimal contamination with evaluation datasets. We focus our decontamination efforts on the midtraining stage (and the long-context extension, which drew from the same data pools) in light of results suggesting that memorization occurs most strongly near the end of training (Magar and Schwartz, 2022; Bordt et al., 2024).

中文: 此前的 Olmo 模型已推动了关于基座模型训练中基准污染的研究，例如对困惑度评测的去污染（Magnusson et al., 2024），或测量质量过滤器对评测泄漏的影响（Godey et al., 2025）。在 Olmo 3 中期训练中，我们使用一个去污染工具，以确保与评测数据集的污染最小。鉴于有结果表明记忆在训练接近尾声时最为强烈（Magar and Schwartz, 2022; Bordt et al., 2024），我们将去污染工作重点放在中期训练阶段（以及从相同数据池取材的长上下文扩展）。


Source: p.26 S086

Original: Method and tooling — For decontamination, we search for and remove matches of any split of any benchmark dataset that are part of our evaluation harness, as for some we increased sample size by evaluating on training splits. We detect and remove contamination between midtraining data and benchmark documents by developing a new decon package. Briefly, decon operates in two phases: 1. Detection phase — For each midtraining document, decon samples n-grams at a regular stride, checking whether the current n-gram matches known n-grams for any benchmark in the evaluation suite. 2. Cluster expansion phase — If a match is found, the matching text is expanded on both sides, counting the number of adjacent n-grams that are also contaminated; if the value is above a specified threshold, the document is deemed contaminated and removed. The two phases approach is key for efficiency: the detection phase checks at non-overlapping intervals to speed up processing, while the cluster expansion phase thoroughly checks for matches to compute an accurate contamination score.

中文: 方法与工具——对于去污染，我们搜索并移除评测工具链中任何基准数据集的任何切分的匹配项，因为对于其中一些基准，我们通过在训练切分上评测来增大样本量。我们开发了一个新的 decon 包，来检测并移除中期训练数据与基准文档之间的污染。简而言之，decon 分两个阶段运行：1. 检测阶段——对每篇中期训练文档，decon 以固定步长采样 n-gram，检查当前 n-gram 是否与评测套件中任何基准的已知 n-gram 匹配。2. 簇扩展阶段——若发现匹配，则将匹配文本向两侧扩展，统计相邻 n-gram 中同样被污染的数量；若该值高于指定阈值，则该文档被视为污染并被移除。两阶段方法对效率至关重要：检测阶段以不重叠的间隔检查以加速处理，而簇扩展阶段彻底检查匹配以计算准确的污染分数。


Source: p.26 S087

Original: We tune the contamination score to balance precision and recall based on numerous qualitative reviews. We iteratively refine our decontamination protocol; for example, the first version fails to decontaminate against SQuAD v2 due to a preprocessing issue; DROP is also incorrectly processed due to its short-question-about-a-passage format. We address these issues by evaluating question, answer, and passage components separately—matching primarily on questions, but using answer/passage matches as supporting information for shorter or edited questions. We also improve precision for multiple-choice evals by matching against full answers rather than just A/B/C/D labels. The decon repository includes configuration files that reproduce both the earlier and final approaches. Appendix A.5 provides a detailed overview of decon.

中文: 我们基于大量定性审查来调优污染分数，以平衡精确率与召回率。我们迭代地精炼去污染协议；例如，第一版因预处理问题未能对 SQuAD v2 去污染；DROP 也因其「关于篇章的短问题」格式而被错误处理。我们通过分别评估问题、答案和篇章三个部分来解决这些问题——主要按问题匹配，但将答案/篇章匹配作为较短或经编辑问题的辅助信息。我们还通过匹配完整答案（而非仅 A/B/C/D 标签）来改进多项选择评测的精确率。decon 仓库包含复现早期与最终两种方法的配置文件。附录 A.5 提供了 decon 的详细概述。

3.5.4 Key findings

3.5.4 关键发现


Source: p.26 S088

Original: Our two-part methodological framework for evaluating midtraining enables us to track closely the quality of our candidate mixes and the behaviors of individual data sources in interaction with others. Here we detail some of the key findings from that process.

中文: 我们评估中期训练的两部分方法学框架，使我们能够密切跟踪候选混合的质量、以及各个数据源在与其他数据源相互作用时的行为。下面我们详述该过程中的一些关键发现。


Source: p.27 S089

Original: Candidate mix quality improves over time — Our integration tests allow us to verify progressive improvements in our candidate midtraining mixes over time: Table 6 shows this improvement across a sample of three candidate mixes illustrating the development trajectory. (Since midtraining development operates in tandem with pretraining, we develop mixes on earlier pretrained checkpoints—thus the comparisons here are given to illustrate progress in data curation, and should not be confused with final midtraining numbers.) We see in Table 6 that across all base model metrics, as well as in evaluations of subsequent SFT training, newer candidate mixes consistently improve performance. Notably, between Round 3 and Round 5 we also introduce our decontamination process, which means that the gains of Round 5 relative to Round 1 and Round 3 are likely underestimated in this table, given that only Round 5 reflects decontaminated data.

中文: 候选混合质量随时间提升——我们的集成测试使我们能够验证候选中期训练混合随时间的逐步改进：表 6 通过三个候选混合的样本展示了这一改进，说明发展轨迹。（由于中期训练开发与预训练并行进行，我们是在较早的预训练检查点上开发混合的——因此这里的对比是为了说明数据整理的进展，不应与最终中期训练的数字混淆。）我们在表 6 中看到，在所有的基座模型指标、以及后续 SFT 训练的评测上，较新的候选混合都持续改进性能。值得注意的是，在 Round 3 与 Round 5 之间，我们还引入了去污染过程，这意味着 Round 5 相对于 Round 1 和 Round 3 的收益在此表中可能被低估了，因为只有 Round 5 反映的是去污染后的数据。

Original: Table 6 — Performance across candidate 100B-token midtraining mixes on the OlmoBaseEval Main suite, and in evals after subsequent SFT.

中文表注: 表 6 —— 候选 100B 令牌中期训练混合在 OlmoBaseEval Main 套件、以及后续 SFT 评测上的表现。

Source: p.27 S090

Original: Performance shows substantial domain tradeoffs — Alongside our central integration tests, we also conduct exploratory 100B anneals with heavy skews toward particular domains, to better understand domain tradeoffs. We treat code/math/thinking capabilities as one domain group, and generative/QA capabilities as another domain group—and create modified mixes each prioritizing one of these groups while omitting the other. Our Gen-QA mix increases proportions of web, QA, and instruction data while omitting math, code, and thinking, and our math-code-thinking mix increases proportions of math, code, and thinking data while omitting QA and instruction data (but keeping web to avoid excessive skew away from pretraining distribution). Table 7 shows results from these runs, compared against our final Round 5 midtraining mix. We see that training on our Gen-QA mix results in a substantial drop in math and code performance, while approximately matching the final mix in MCSTEM, MCNon-STEM, and GenQA performance. By contrast, in our math-code-thinking mix, math and code performance substantially exceeds that of our final mix—however, MCSTEM, MCNon-STEM, and GenQA performance take a notable hit. These results indicate that there are real tradeoffs when skewing toward certain of these domains over others during midtraining. We see in particular that there is clear potential to further improve math and code performance by increasing weight of these domains in the mix—however, this comes at a significant cost to our MCQA and GenQA performance. Increasing weight on Gen-QA domains, on the other hand, yields minimal improvement on QA tasks, while predictably hurting math and code performance. Overall, these results suggest that our final midtraining mix strikes a healthy balance across these domains, avoiding too heavy of a domain skew and enabling strong final performance across metrics.

中文: 性能表现出显著的领域权衡——除了核心集成测试之外，我们还执行了向特定领域重度倾斜的探索性 100B 退火，以更好地理解领域权衡。我们将代码/数学/思考能力视为一个域组，将生成式/QA 能力视为另一个域组——并创建了各自优先其中一个组、同时省略另一个组的修改混合。我们的 Gen-QA 混合提高网页、QA 和指令数据的比例，同时省略数学、代码和思考数据；而我们的 math-code-thinking 混合提高数学、代码和思考数据的比例，同时省略 QA 和指令数据（但保留网页，以避免过度偏离预训练分布）。表 7 展示了这些运行的结果，并与我们最终的 Round 5 中期训练混合对比。我们看到，用 Gen-QA 混合训练会导致数学与代码性能大幅下降，同时在 MCSTEM、MCNon-STEM 和 GenQA 上大致与最终混合持平。相比之下，在 math-code-thinking 混合中，数学与代码性能显著超过我们的最终混合——但 MCSTEM、MCNon-STEM 和 GenQA 性能明显受损。这些结果表明，在中期训练期间向某些领域倾斜会带来真实的权衡。我们尤其看到，通过提高混合中这些领域的权重，确实有进一步提升数学与代码性能的潜力——但这会以显著牺牲 MCQA 和 GenQA 性能为代价。反之，提高 Gen-QA 领域的权重，对 QA 任务的改进甚微，同时如预期般损害数学与代码性能。总体而言，这些结果表明我们的最终中期训练混合在这些领域之间达成了健康的平衡，避免了过度的领域倾斜，从而在各种指标上实现强劲的最终性能。

Original: Table 7 — Demonstration of tradeoffs in domain-skewed mixes using the OlmoBaseEval Main suite.

中文表注: 表 7 —— 使用 OlmoBaseEval Main 套件展示领域倾斜混合的权衡。

Gen-QA mix 66.3 78.1 72.5 27.5 11.9 0.1
Math-code-thinking mix 62.5 69.6 65.9 60.8 35.6 37.7

Original: Thinking/instruct data benefits base performance — We also investigate the overall impact of inclusion of our post-training-oriented data—instruction and thinking trace data—through 100B integration tests on one of our intermediate midtraining mixes both with and without inclusion of these data subsets (holding total mix tokens constant). Table 10 shows base eval performance after each of these training runs—we see that the mix that includes these post-training elements performs better on every base eval measure. This suggests that although individual sources and domains present performance tradeoffs, the inclusion of these cross-domain post-training data types in aggregate is consistently beneficial, and this benefit begins even before post-training.

中文: 思考/指令数据有益于基座性能——我们还通过 100B 集成测试，在其中一个中间中期训练混合上（保持总混合令牌数不变），分别纳入与不纳入这些面向后训练的数据子集，来研究其整体影响。表 10 展示了每次训练运行后的基座评测性能——我们看到，纳入这些后训练要素的混合在每一项基座评测指标上都表现得更好。这表明，尽管单个来源和领域存在性能权衡，但总体纳入这些跨域后训练数据类型始终是有益的，而且这种益处甚至在后训练开始之前就已显现。

Source: p.28 S093

Original: Leave special tokens for SFT stage — To inform our formatting for instruction datasets, we also conduct an investigation to determine the impacts of inclusion or omission of special chat tokens such as <|im_start|> and <|im_end|> in our midtraining data. We test this via microanneals on the Tulu3-SFT data, comparing versions with and without these tokens. Experiments show that when training on data containing chat templates and special tokens, models consistently output these special tokens at inference time, resulting in evaluation scores that are dramatically reduced (e.g. GSM8K drops from 49.43 to 0, and CruxEval drops from 32.89 to 18.91). Further analysis highlights that simply including a chat template, with ordinary text in place of special tokens, did not produce the same performance drop (46.02 on GSM8K and 29.65 on CruxEval), suggesting that this disruption in model behavior is not due to inclusion of a chat template more generally, but is rather due specifically to the introduction of special tokens to the embedding vocabulary when they have not been seen in pretraining. Though the degradation in model evaluation scores can be attributed primarily to disruption in answer parsing, these results highlight the broader issue that inclusion of these tokens at midtraining time results in emission of these tokens by the base model at inference time. Since this is an undesirable behavior, we ultimately remove both the chat template and special tokens from our instruct data, and revert to simple newline-based formatting.

中文: 将特殊令牌留给 SFT 阶段——为确定指令数据集的格式，我们还进行了一项调查，以确定在中期训练数据中纳入或省略 <|im_start|> 和 <|im_end|> 等特殊对话令牌的影响。我们通过在 Tulu3-SFT 数据上的微退火来测试，比较含与不含这些令牌的版本。实验表明，当在包含对话模板与特殊令牌的数据上训练时，模型在推理时会持续输出这些特殊令牌，导致评测分数急剧下降（例如 GSM8K 从 49.43 降至 0，CruxEval 从 32.89 降至 18.91）。进一步分析强调，仅仅纳入对话模板、用普通文本替代特殊令牌，并不会产生同样的性能下降（GSM8K 为 46.02，CruxEval 为 29.65），这表明模型行为的这种扰乱并非普遍地源于纳入对话模板，而是特别源于在嵌入词表中引入了预训练中未见过的特殊令牌。尽管模型评测分数的下降主要可归因于答案解析的扰乱，但这些结果突显了一个更广泛的问题：在中期训练时纳入这些令牌会导致基座模型在推理时输出这些令牌。由于这是不希望出现的行为，我们最终从指令数据中移除了对话模板与特殊令牌，并回归简单的基于换行的格式。


Source: p.28 S094

Original: Extent and impact of decontamination are variable — Figure 12 shows the top ten midtraining data sources containing the most occurrences of benchmark contamination. We find that much of the contamination occurs in existing datasets such as Flan and Nemotron. Not all contamination was subtle—we found many templated contamination instances, in which fields from benchmarks were exactly matched, with templated content inserted between them. Furthermore, many of these were not isolated instances, but complete validation or test splits. For instance, Flan is constructed from templates on benchmark data, and can include validation data that is used for model development decisions since test sets are hidden (e.g., DROP). Performance is sometimes, but not always, inflated by contamination. We investigate this by comparing our final decontaminated 100B anneal with a matched 100B anneal using the non-decontaminated data versions. Figure 12 also shows the extent to which benchmark performance after midtraining drops when contamination is removed (Perf Δ). Some differences are substantial—such as validation or test performance changes in DROP, Minerva, SQuAD. Note that we remove contamination of all splits for all benchmarks, such as for DROP removing over 60,000 training examples from sources such as Flan. So performance differences may indicate that decontamination is preventing memorization or also removing in-distribution training examples.

中文: 去污染的程度与影响是可变的——图 12 展示了基准污染出现次数最多的前十个中期训练数据源。我们发现大量污染发生在 Flan 和 Nemotron 等现有数据集中。并非所有污染都很隐蔽——我们发现了许多模板化污染实例，其中基准的字段被精确匹配，并在其间插入了模板化内容。此外，其中许多并非孤立实例，而是完整的验证或测试切分。例如，Flan 由基准数据上的模板构建而成，可能包含用于模型开发决策的验证数据（因为测试集是隐藏的，例如 DROP）。污染有时（但并非总是）会抬高性能。我们通过将最终去污染的 100B 退火与使用未去污染数据版本的匹配 100B 退火进行对比来研究这一点。图 12 还展示了移除污染后，中期训练后的基准性能下降的程度（Perf Δ）。有些差异相当可观——例如 DROP、Minerva、SQuAD 的验证或测试性能变化。注意，我们对所有基准的所有切分都移除了污染，例如对于 DROP，从 Flan 等来源中移除了超过 60,000 个训练示例。因此，性能差异可能表明去污染防止了记忆化，或同时移除了分布内的训练示例。

Fig. 12. 十大最严重污染中期训练来源中的基准实例出现次数

We decontaminate against all splits of all benchmarks, as some (right) include training data when evaluated to reduce noise. Some but not all contaminated benchmarks show substantial Perf Δ between contaminated and decontaminated runs (discussion in Section §3.5.4).

中文图注: 十大最严重污染中期训练来源中基准实例的出现次数。我们对所有基准的所有切分去污染，因为其中一些（右侧）在评测时包含训练数据以减少噪声。部分（而非全部）被污染的基准在污染与去污染运行之间显示出显著的 Perf Δ（讨论见第 §3.5.4 节）。

Source: p.29 S095

Original: We remove all splits because some of our development benchmarks increase sample size by evaluating on train and held out splits (Figure 12, right) and several of these also show performance overestimation with contamination of any of the evaluated benchmark splits. However, other benchmarks do not show inflated performance, despite contamination: we see that DeepSeek LeetCode performance is close to 0 with or without contamination, and SQuAD under the easier MC metric is saturated in either case. Finally, similarly to reports from Marin 32B (Hall et al., 2025), we find that despite the fact that our decontamination procedure detected complete leakage of GSM8K in our data, this does not result in better performance with the contaminated data. Instead we see that performance is in fact better with the decontaminated data, a phenomenon that the Marin authors explain occurs due to the contaminated formatting not matching the evaluated format.

中文: 我们移除所有切分，是因为我们的一些开发基准通过在训练与留出切分上评测来增大样本量（图 12 右侧），其中一些基准还表现出：任何被评测基准切分的污染都会导致性能高估。然而，其他基准尽管有污染，却并未显示被抬高的性能：我们看到 DeepSeek LeetCode 的性能无论有无污染都接近 0，而 SQuAD 在更简单的 MC 指标下两种情况都饱和。最后，与 Marin 32B（Hall et al., 2025）的报告类似，我们发现尽管我们的去污染过程检测到 GSM8K 在我们的数据中完全泄漏，但这并未让污染数据带来更好的性能。相反，我们看到去污染后的数据性能实际上更好——Marin 作者将这一现象解释为：污染的格式与评测格式不匹配所致。


Source: p.30 S096

Original: Model souping can improve midtraining performance — For Olmo 3 Base 32B, we observe noteworthy performance improvement from merging two independent midtraining runs with differing seeds. Relative to the individual midtraining runs, the merged model yields nearly a full point of improvement in the MCSTEM task cluster, 0.4 improvement in the GenQA task cluster, and in the Math task cluster results in improvements of 2.9 and 1.6 relative to the first and second midtraining runs, respectively. Other noteworthy improvements include approximately 1 point of improvement in MMLU, and 5 and 2 points of improvement in GSM Symbolic relative to the first and second runs. For this reason, we select the merged model as our final midtrained 32B checkpoint.

中文: 模型「汤」（model souping）可以改进中期训练性能——对于 Olmo 3 Base 32B，我们观察到合并两个使用不同种子、独立进行的中期训练运行，能带来值得注意的性能改进。相对于各自的中期训练运行，合并后的模型在 MCSTEM 任务簇上取得近 1 分改进，在 GenQA 任务簇上取得 0.4 改进，在 Math 任务簇上相对第一次与第二次中期训练运行分别取得 2.9 与 1.6 的改进。其他值得注意的改进包括 MMLU 约 1 分改进，以及 GSM Symbolic 相对第一次与第二次运行分别 5 分与 2 分的改进。因此，我们选择合并后的模型作为最终的中期训练 32B 检查点。

3.6 Stage 3: Long-context Extension

3.6 阶段 3：长上下文扩展


Source: p.30 S097

Original: A crucial ability for modern language models is the capacity to operate over long sequences. This capability is necessary to process the long inputs required by many real-world tasks. Moreover, generating long sequences of intermediate tokens is a common technique to achieve test-time scaling (Muennighoff et al., 2025b). In this section, we provide an overview of the methodology we used to scale Olmo 3’s context window from 8,192 to 65,536 tokens. We also describe Dolma 3 Longmino Mix, a high-quality dataset of both naturally-occurring and synthetically-augmented long texts. Dolma 3 Longmino Mix consists of over 600 billion tokens; statistics in Table 11.

中文: 现代语言模型的一项关键能力是能够在长序列上运行。这一能力对于处理许多现实世界任务所需的长输入是必要的。此外，生成长序列的中间令牌是实现测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的常用技术（Muennighoff et al., 2025b）。在本节中，我们概述将 Olmo 3 的上下文窗口从 8192 扩展到 65,536 令牌所用方法的概览。我们还描述了 Dolma 3 Longmino Mix，一个既含自然出现、也含合成增强的长文本的高质量数据集。Dolma 3 Longmino Mix 由超过 6000 亿令牌组成；统计见表 11。

Table 11. Dolma 3 Longmino Mix 的组成
