Source: p.30 S098

Original: Long-context extension strategy — Because training with long sequence lengths is computationally costly, most language models are pretrained with shorter sequences and extended only in a later stage of model development. During the extension phase, models are trained on longer documents and the hyperparameters of positional embeddings are typically adjusted to ease positional generalization.

中文: 长上下文扩展策略——由于用长序列长度训练计算成本高昂，大多数语言模型以较短序列预训练，并仅在模型开发的后期阶段进行扩展。在扩展阶段，模型在更长的文档上训练，并且通常调整位置嵌入的超参数以缓解位置泛化问题。


Source: p.30 S099

Original: High variance in open-model recipes — The recipes for performing this long-context extension vary dramatically between models. The context extension phase for many language models ranges from hundreds of billions (SmolLM3: 100B; GLM 4.5: 100B; DeepSeek V3: 123B; Apertus: 225B) to almost one trillion tokens (Kimi K2: 400B; Llama 3.1: 800B; DeepSeek V3.1: 840B). However, there are outliers: AFM (Goddard, 2025) and Nemotron Nano 2 (NVIDIA et al., 2025) both use fewer than 20 billion tokens to extend to 64K and 128K, respectively. Standalone extension recipes have also been proposed, many emphasizing token efficiency. For instance, ProLong (Gao et al., 2025) uses 20B tokens drawn from books and code, whereas LongAttn (Wu et al., 2025b) constructs a 5B-token corpus using self-attention scores from existing language models to select documents exhibiting long-range dependencies. Another key point of divergence across model families is when in the development pipeline the extension is performed: Llama 3.1 models apply long-context extension prior to midtraining, Qwen 2.5 and 3 perform it afterwards, and GLM 4.5 applies extension only after supervised finetuning.

中文: 开放模型配方的巨大差异——执行这种长上下文扩展的配方在不同模型之间差异巨大。许多语言模型的上下文扩展阶段从数千亿令牌（SmolLM3：100B；GLM 4.5：100B；DeepSeek V3：123B；Apertus：225B）到近一万亿令牌（Kimi K2：400B；Llama 3.1：800B；DeepSeek V3.1：840B）不等。然而，也有离群值：AFM（Goddard, 2025）和 Nemotron Nano 2（NVIDIA et al., 2025）分别仅用不到 200 亿令牌就扩展到 64K 和 128K。此外还有人提出独立扩展配方，其中许多强调令牌效率。例如，ProLong（Gao et al., 2025）使用取自书籍与代码的 20B 令牌，而 LongAttn（Wu et al., 2025b）使用现有语言模型的自注意力分数构建一个 5B 令牌语料，以选择表现出长程依赖的文档。各模型家族之间的另一个关键分歧点是扩展在开发流水线中的时机：Llama 3.1 模型在中期训练之前应用长上下文扩展，Qwen 2.5 和 3 在之后执行，而 GLM 4.5 仅在监督微调之后才应用扩展。


Source: p.31 S100

Original: Olmo 3 long-context recipe — To extend Olmo 3’s context, we use long documents from the olmOCR science PDFs pool (Section §3.6.1) with additional filtering and synthetic data augmentation applied (Section §3.6.2). We call this collection Dolma 3 Longmino Pool. We mix 34% long-context data with 66% high-quality short-context data sampled from Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix, and train using this mix for an additional 50B tokens for Olmo 3 7B and 100B tokens for Olmo 3 32B, as described in Section §3.6.3. During long-context extension, we apply YaRN (Peng et al., 2023) to full attention layers, and do not adjust positional embeddings on sliding-window attention layers; we use document packing and inter-document masking (Section §3.6.3). We summarize the key aspects of our recipe in Figure 13. While developing this recipe, we carefully analyze and isolate architectural design decisions that have profound impact on long-context performance; our investigation is presented in Bertsch et al. (2026).

中文: Olmo 3 长上下文配方——为扩展 Olmo 3 的上下文，我们使用来自 olmOCR 科学 PDF 数据池的长文档（第 §3.6.1 节），并应用额外过滤与合成数据增强（第 §3.6.2 节）。我们将这一集合称为 Dolma 3 Longmino Pool。我们将 34% 的长上下文数据与 66% 从 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix 采样的高质量短上下文数据混合，并如第 §3.6.3 节所述，用此混合额外训练 Olmo 3 7B 50B 令牌、Olmo 3 32B 100B 令牌。在长上下文扩展期间，我们对全注意力层应用 YaRN（Peng et al., 2023），且不调整滑动窗口注意力层上的位置嵌入；我们使用文档打包（document packing）与文档间掩码（第 §3.6.3 节）。图 13 总结了配方的关键方面。在开发该配方的过程中，我们仔细分析并隔离了对长上下文性能有深远影响的架构设计决策；我们的研究见 Bertsch et al. (2026)。


Source: p.31 S101

Original: Overall results — We evaluate our context-extended models on two popular long-context benchmarks. RULER (Hsieh et al., 2024) is a benchmark of synthetic long-context tasks including challenging variations of the Needle-in-a-Haystack task (Nelson et al., 2024) and simple aggregation tasks that require counting over inputs; we use RULER as the primary metric to guide our long-context recipe development. HELMET (Yen et al., 2025) is a suite of long-context benchmarks across a diverse set of task types, including retrieval, in-context learning, and summarization tasks, which we evaluate on to represent more general long-context capabilities. We keep HELMET as an unseen evaluation suite and test our final checkpoints on it. We report results in Table 12.

中文: 总体结果——我们在两个流行的长上下文基准上评测上下文扩展后的模型。RULER（Hsieh et al., 2024）是一个合成长上下文任务基准，包含大海捞针任务（Needle-in-a-Haystack，Nelson et al., 2024）的挑战性变体、以及需要跨输入计数的简单聚合任务；我们使用 RULER 作为指导长上下文配方开发的主要指标。HELMET（Yen et al., 2025）是一套覆盖多种任务类型的长上下文基准，包括检索、上下文学习与摘要任务，我们在其上评测以代表更通用的长上下文能力。我们将 HELMET 作为未见过的评测套件，并在其上测试最终检查点。结果报告于表 12。

Fig. 13. Olmo 3 长上下文扩展配方的五个关键要素（RULER 基准）

Placed near: p.31 S101
Source: p.32 C019

Original caption: Five key components of the Olmo 3 long-context extension recipe measured on the RULER benchmark. Applying YaRN to full attention layers only gives the best results (Figure 13a); olmOCR science PDFs are more effective than other recipes (Figure 13b); synthetic data augmentation improves performance over natural documents alone (Figure 13c); document packing boosts performance for longer context lengths (Figure 13d); longer extensions improve RULER scores, especially for longer sequences (Figure 13e).

中文图注: 在 RULER 基准上测得的 Olmo 3 长上下文扩展配方五个关键要素。仅在完整注意力层上应用 YaRN 效果最佳（图 13a）；olmOCR 科学 PDF 比其他配方更有效（图 13b）；合成数据增强比仅用自然文档更优（图 13c）；文档打包在更长上下文长度上提升性能（图 13d）；更长的扩展改善 RULER 分数，尤其对更长序列（图 13e）。

Reading note: 五个子图分别对应注意力缩放、更好的数据、合成数据增强、文档打包、扩展令牌预算五个要素。

3.6.1 Sourcing Long Context Data

3.6.1 长上下文数据的来源


Source: p.31 S102

Original: olmOCR science PDFs — The backbone of our long-context data pool is scientific PDFs scraped from the web and processed by olmOCR. Figure 14 describes the distribution by topic in each length bucket shown in Table 11. Data filtering — We filter this data using gzip compressibility as a metric. gzip has been used for text classification (Jiang et al., 2022) and as a feature in fine-grained scaling laws (Pandey, 2024). We use gzip for data filtering by excluding the extremes: removing the 20% of text that is most compressible and the 20% of text that is least compressible. We also consider applying filters based on LongPpl (Fang et al., 2025b), which identifies tokens that rely most on long-range dependencies by measuring, for each token, the change in perplexity under an existing long-context model when additional preceding context is provided. We compute LongPpl over 10B tokens of Dolma 3 Longmino Mix using Gemma 3 4B (Gemma 3 Team, 2025) as the reference model, and comparing contextualization using 4K or 128K context windows. We use the same threshold as Fang et al. (2025b) for determining whether a token is a “key” token that requires long context dependencies.

中文: olmOCR 科学 PDF——我们长上下文数据池的骨干是从网络抓取、并经 olmOCR 处理的科学 PDF。图 14 描述了表 11 中每个长度桶按主题的分布。数据过滤——我们使用 gzip 可压缩性作为指标来过滤数据。gzip 已被用于文本分类（Jiang et al., 2022），并作为细粒度缩放律中的一个特征（Pandey, 2024）。我们使用 gzip 进行数据过滤，方式是排除极端：移除最可压缩的 20% 文本和最不可压缩的 20% 文本。我们还考虑应用基于 LongPpl（Fang et al., 2025b）的过滤器。LongPpl 通过为每个令牌测量在提供额外前文时、在现有长上下文模型下困惑度的变化，来识别最依赖长程依赖的令牌。我们在 Dolma 3 Longmino Mix 的 10B 令牌上，使用 Gemma 3 4B（Gemma 3 Team, 2025）作为参考模型、并比较使用 4K 或 128K 上下文窗口的上下文化来计算 LongPpl。我们使用与 Fang et al. (2025b) 相同的阈值来判断某个令牌是否是需依赖长上下文的「关键」令牌。


Source: p.31 S103

Original: We compute two statistics over each document: the fraction of tokens marked as key tokens, and the spread of key tokens across the document (which we compute as the standard deviation of key token locations, which are measured relative to the document length). In a sweep of experiments, we consider excluding the bottom 20% of documents with the least key tokens or lowest spread, and excluding both the top and bottom 20% as outliers; none of these possibilities outperform the gzip filter, so we do not use this for the final run.

中文: 我们对每篇文档计算两个统计量：被标记为关键令牌的令牌比例，以及关键令牌在文档中的分布广度（我们以关键令牌位置的标准差来计算，这些位置是相对于文档长度测量的）。在一组实验中，我们考虑排除关键令牌最少或广度最低的底部 20% 文档，以及将顶部与底部各 20% 作为离群值排除；这些方案都不优于 gzip 过滤器，因此我们在最终运行中不使用它。

Fig. 14. olmOCR 科学 PDF 中按长度桶划分的主题令牌占比分布

Placed near: p.31 S103
Source: p.33 C020

Original caption: Distribution of token counts over WebOrganizer (Wettig et al., 2025) topics in olmOCR science PDFs, partitioned by length.

中文图注: olmOCR 科学 PDF 中，按长度划分的 WebOrganizer（Wettig et al., 2025）主题令牌数分布。

Reading note: 各长度桶（4K–1M）中「科学与技术」「软件」「教育」「健康」等主题的令牌占比；科学/数学/技术主题占比最大。

3.6.2 Experiments with Synthetic Augmentation

3.6.2 合成增强实验


Source: p.32 S104

Original: A common use case for extended-context language models is information extraction and synthesis over long inputs (Bai et al., 2024, 2025). However, most long documents do not provide supervision for such tasks. Directly inspired by CLIPPER (Pham et al., 2025), we modify a portion of our science PDF pool by injecting synthetically generated aggregation tasks at randomly sampled intervals. Our approach also shares similarities with Qwen 2.5 1M (Yang et al., 2025b). Generation pipeline — The main challenge in generating synthetic data for long-context understanding is the bootstrap problem: how can we create effective data without having access to models that can process long context? Our pipeline uses document statistics to identify the most important terms and then extracts snippets containing those terms. Those snippets are subsequently provided to a language model to create aggregation tasks. In detail: 1. For a given document of length n tokens, we partition the document into m sections of length 8K to 32K tokens. We attempt to place these partitions near natural breaks in the document flow, such as right before new sections.

中文: 扩展上下文语言模型的一个常见用例是对长输入进行信息抽取与综合（Bai et al., 2024, 2025）。然而，大多数长文档并不为此类任务提供监督。直接受 CLIPPER（Pham et al., 2025）启发，我们通过在随机采样间隔处注入合成生成的聚合任务，修改了部分科学 PDF 数据池。我们的方法也与 Qwen 2.5 1M（Yang et al., 2025b）有相似之处。生成流水线——为长上下文理解生成合成数据的主要挑战是「冷启动」问题：在没有能处理长上下文的模型的情况下，如何创建有效数据？我们的流水线使用文档统计来识别最重要的术语，然后提取包含这些术语的片段。这些片段随后被提供给语言模型以创建聚合任务。具体而言：1. 对于长度为 n 令牌的给定文档，我们将文档划分为 m 段、每段 8K 到 32K 令牌。我们尝试将这些划分置于文档流程中的自然断点附近，例如正好在新章节之前。


Source: p.34 S105

Original: 2. For each partition, we normalize and tokenize the text, extract one- and two-word noun phrases, and use tf-idf to identify the most salient noun phrases; 3. For each noun phrase, we select k = 8 snippets of text from the partition, ranked by tf-idf; 4. We pass the noun phrases, (optional) snippets, and one or more prompts describing the aggregation task to a language model. For Olmo 3, we use documents where 32,768 ≤ n < 65,536 tokens, resulting in 2 to 8 partitions per document. While we experimented with several closed and open language models, we ultimately use OLMo 2 Instruct 32B for all generations.

中文: 2. 对每个分区，我们规范化并分词文本，提取一个词与两个词的名词短语，并使用 tf-idf 识别最显著的名词短语；3. 对每个名词短语，我们从该分区选择按 tf-idf 排序的 k = 8 个文本片段；4. 我们将名词短语、（可选的）片段、以及一个或多个描述聚合任务的提示词传递给语言模型。对于 Olmo 3，我们使用满足 32,768 ≤ n < 65,536 令牌的文档，从而每篇文档得到 2 到 8 个分区。虽然我们实验了多种闭源与开放语言模型，但最终为所有生成统一使用 OLMo 2 Instruct 32B。


Source: p.34 S106

Original: Synthetic aggregation tasks — We consider two aggregation tasks; we refer the reader to the code implementation for the exact prompts used. CWE (Common Word Extraction) — We prompt OLMo 2 Instruct with 5 commonly occurring single-word noun phrases in the partition, and ask the model to generate diverse QA pairs that require the answer to be the exact number of times each unigram occurs in the partition. REX (Rewriting EXpressions) — For each noun phrase and corresponding snippets, we prompt OLMo 2 Instruct to generate an aggregation task matching one of the following 12 vignettes discussing the noun phrase: a short summary, a dialogue between a professor and student, a simple paragraph for high school students, a set of flashcards, a school quiz, a game show, a dinner party, a debate, a list of true or false claims, a movie scene, an encyclopedic description, or an explainer in the style of conversations on the r/explainlikeimfive subreddit.

中文: 合成聚合任务——我们考虑两种聚合任务；确切使用的提示词请参阅代码实现。CWE（常见词抽取）——我们向 OLMo 2 Instruct 提供分区中 5 个常见的单词名词短语，并要求模型生成多样的 QA 对，其答案必须是每个一元词（unigram）在分区中出现的精确次数。REX（表达式重写）——对每个名词短语及相应片段，我们提示 OLMo 2 Instruct 生成一个聚合任务，需匹配讨论该名词短语的以下 12 种小场景之一：简短摘要、教授与学生对话、面向高中生的简单段落、一组抽认卡、学校测验、游戏节目、晚宴、辩论、真假命题列表、电影场景、百科式描述、或以 r/explainlikeimfive 子版块对话风格写成的解释。

3.6.3 Choosing Data Mix and Token Budget

3.6.3 数据混合与令牌预算的选择


Source: p.34 S107

Original: Interleaving long- and short-context data — Rather than training on only long-context data, we mix high-quality short-context data from midtraining (stage two) to ensure that performance on short-context tasks is not meaningfully degraded. Early experiments on a 10B-token extension show that a 66% / 34% mix of long-context to short-context data drops performance on a subset of OlmoBaseEval by 2.5 points; in comparison, a 34% long-context, 66% short-context mix only drops performance by 0.8 points.

中文: 长、短上下文数据的交织——我们并非只在长上下文数据上训练，而是混合来自中期训练（阶段二）的高质量短上下文数据，以确保短上下文任务上的性能不会显著下降。在一个 10B 令牌扩展上的早期实验表明，长上下文与短上下文 66%/34% 的混合会使 OlmoBaseEval 的一个子集性能下降 2.5 分；相比之下，34% 长上下文、66% 短上下文的混合仅使性能下降 0.8 分。


Source: p.34 S108

Original: Longer extension helps — Figure 13e shows that allocating more tokens to the long-context extension stage improves performance on long-context tasks, particularly at longer sequence lengths. We extend the context of Olmo 3 7B through a 50B stage 3 training; for Olmo 3 32B, we extend for 100B tokens for better long-context capabilities.

中文: 更长的扩展有帮助——图 13e 表明，向长上下文扩展阶段分配更多令牌会改进长上下文任务上的性能，尤其是在更长的序列长度上。我们通过 50B 的阶段 3 训练扩展 Olmo 3 7B 的上下文；对于 Olmo 3 32B，我们扩展 100B 令牌以获得更好的长上下文能力。

3.6.4 Curating a Training Recipe for Extension

3.6.4 整理扩展训练配方


Source: p.34 S109

Original: RoPE extension — Olmo 3 uses RoPE (Su et al., 2024) to encode positional information within the transformer architecture. We experiment with several methods for extending RoPE beyond the original pretraining context length, including adjusted base frequency scaling (Xiong et al., 2023; Rozière et al., 2024), position interpolation (Chen et al., 2023), and YaRN (Peng et al., 2023). Each approach is applied either to all RoPE instances or is restricted to RoPE used in full attention layers. We find that applying YaRN only to full attention layers yields the best overall performance.

中文: RoPE 扩展——Olmo 3 使用 RoPE（Su et al., 2024）在 transformer 架构内编码位置信息。我们实验了多种将 RoPE 扩展到原始预训练上下文长度之外的方法，包括调整基频缩放（Xiong et al., 2023; Rozière et al., 2024）、位置插值（Chen et al., 2023）和 YaRN（Peng et al., 2023）。每种方法要么应用于所有 RoPE 实例，要么仅限于完整注意力层中使用的 RoPE。我们发现仅在完整注意力层上应用 YaRN 能带来最佳的总体性能。


Source: p.34 S110

Original: Document packing — During pretraining and midtraining, we follow the standard approach of concatenating documents and splitting them into fixed-length training sequences. However, when extending the context length, this strategy produces training instances that are, on average, shorter than the underlying document length distribution. To address this, we adopt best-fit document packing (Ding et al., 2024), which reduces the number of split documents while adding a negligible amount of padding. Compared to the naive concatenate-then-split approach, best-fit packing yields substantially improved performance on long-context benchmarks.

中文: 文档打包——在预训练与中期训练期间，我们遵循标准的做法：连接文档并将其切分为固定长度的训练序列。然而，当扩展上下文长度时，这一策略产生的训练实例平均短于底层文档的长度分布。为解决这一点，我们采用最佳拟合文档打包（best-fit document packing，Ding et al., 2024），它在增加可忽略的填充量的同时减少了被切分的文档数量。与朴素的「先连接再切分」方法相比，最佳拟合打包在长上下文基准上带来了显著改进。


Source: p.35 S111

Original: Intra-document masking — During long-context extension, we apply intra-document masking to ensure that each training sequence attends only to tokens originating from the same underlying document (Zhao et al., 2024b; Grattafiori et al., 2024). This prevents the model from being distracted by cross-document signals, which can otherwise introduce spurious attention patterns and degrade long-range performance. LC training infrastructure — To extend the model to a 65K-token context window, we employ 8-way context parallelism (CP) so that each device processes 8K tokens from each training instance. We adopt the all-gather-based CP attention strategy introduced by Chu et al. (2025), which makes it straightforward to support irregular attention masks, including sliding-window and intra-document masking. For parallelism configurations, infrastructure details, and throughput measurements, see Appendix Table 34.

中文: 文档内掩码——在长上下文扩展期间，我们应用文档内掩码，以确保每个训练序列仅关注来自同一底层文档的令牌（Zhao et al., 2024b; Grattafiori et al., 2024）。这防止模型被跨文档信号干扰，否则可能引入虚假的注意力模式并降低长程性能。长上下文训练基础设施——为将模型扩展到 65K 令牌的上下文窗口，我们采用 8 路上下文并行（CP），使每个设备从每个训练实例中处理 8K 令牌。我们采用 Chu et al. (2025) 引入的基于 all-gather 的 CP 注意力策略，它使我们能直接支持不规则注意力掩码，包括滑动窗口与文档内掩码。并行配置、基础设施细节与吞吐测量见附录表 34。


Source: p.35 S112

Original: Model souping — Following performance improvements from merging midtraining runs for Olmo 3 Base 32B, we experiment with averaging long-context checkpoints. In this case, rather than running long-context extension multiple times with different seeds, we merge the last three checkpoints from the end of the extension run (at steps 10,000, 11,000, and 11,921) to produce our final long-context Olmo 3 Base 32B.

中文: 模型「汤」——在合并中期训练运行为 Olmo 3 Base 32B 带来性能改进之后，我们实验了对长上下文检查点取平均。在此情形下，我们不是用不同种子多次运行长上下文扩展，而是合并扩展运行末尾的最后三个检查点（在第 10,000、11,000 和 11,921 步），以产出最终的长上下文 Olmo 3 Base 32B。

Table 12. Olmo 3 与其他开放基座模型的长上下文性能对比

Placed near: p.31 S112
Source: p.33 C021

Original caption: Performance of Olmo 3 compared to other open base models of comparable size. During Olmo 3 development, we use RULER (Hsieh et al., 2024) as our development suite; we hold HELMET (Yen et al., 2025) out as an unseen evaluation suite. The table contains base variants of each model; models are sorted by their respective release dates. Qwen 3 8B Base (Yang et al., 2025a) and Xiaomi MiMo 7B (Xiaomi et al., 2025) only support a context length of up to 32,768 tokens. We exclude any base model that does not support at least 32,768 tokens.

中文表注: Olmo 3 与其他规模相近开放基座模型的性能对比。在 Olmo 3 开发期间，我们使用 RULER（Hsieh et al., 2024）作为开发套件；将 HELMET（Yen et al., 2025）留作未见过的评测套件。表中包含每个模型的基座变体；模型按其各自发布日期排序。Qwen 3 8B Base（Yang et al., 2025a）和 Xiaomi MiMo 7B（Xiaomi et al., 2025）最多仅支持 32,768 令牌的上下文长度。我们排除任何不支持至少 32,768 令牌的基座模型。

Reading note: 分 7B 与 32B 两个规模，分别报告 RULER（4K–65K）与 HELMET（8K–65K）得分。Olmo 3 7B 在 RULER 4K 上达 94.89、32B 达 96.10，与顶尖开放模型相当。

3.7 Base Model Results

3.7 基座模型结果


Source: p.35 S113

Original: In Table 13 we outline the results of Olmo 3 Base after the pretraining, midtraining, and long-context extension stages, comparing performance to other open base models. Compared to OLMo 2, the Olmo 3 models demonstrate clear improvements on science, math, and code-based evaluation metrics, which we attribute largely to our emphasis and upsampling of STEM-related data during the pretraining and midtraining stages. On the other hand, because of this emphasis on STEM, we see slight degradation in general knowledge benchmarks.

中文: 在表 13 中，我们概述了 Olmo 3 Base 在预训练、中期训练与长上下文扩展各阶段之后的结果，并与其他开放基座模型对比性能。与 OLMo 2 相比，Olmo 3 模型在科学、数学和代码类评测指标上表现出明显改进，我们将其主要归因于在预训练与中期训练阶段对 STEM 相关数据的重视与上采样。另一方面，由于对 STEM 的重视，我们在通用知识基准上看到了轻微的退化。

Table 13. Olmo 3 各阶段与其他开放基座模型的对比

Placed near: p.35 S113
Source: p.36 C022

Original caption: Results comparing Olmo 3 to open base models across stages of pretraining, midtraining and long-context extension.

中文表注: 在预训练、中期训练与长上下文扩展各阶段上，Olmo 3 与开放基座模型的对比结果。

Reading note: 展示 Olmo 3 Base 在预训练 → 中期训练 → 长上下文扩展三个阶段的逐阶段性能演进。

4. Olmo 3 Think


Source: p.36 S114

Original: We train Olmo 3 Think to reason by first generating extended thought sequences and then producing a final answer (Figure 2). To achieve this, we curate high-quality reasoning data (Dolci Think), harness a three-stage training recipe (SFT, DPO, and RLVR), and introduce OlmoRL approach, which merges our new algorithmic and engineering advances with a strong community platform of research in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Through these data, training, and algorithmic innovations, Olmo 3 Think achieves strong performance in math, coding, reasoning, and general conversation. At the 32B scale, it stands as the best fully-open thinking model, outperforming Qwen 2.5 32B, Gemma 2 and 3 27B, and narrowing the gap to top open-weight systems like Qwen 3 32B while being trained on fewer FLOPs (Table 14).

中文: 我们训练 Olmo 3 Think 进行推理：先生成扩展的思维序列，再给出最终答案（图 2）。为此，我们整理高质量推理数据（Dolci Think），运用三阶段训练配方（SFT、DPO 与 RLVR），并提出 OlmoRL 方法，它将我们新的算法与工程进展同可验证奖励强化学习领域强大的社区研究平台相结合。通过这些数据、训练与算法创新，Olmo 3 Think 在数学、编码、推理与通用对话方面取得了强劲性能。在 32B 规模上，它是最好的全开放思考模型，超越了 Qwen 2.5 32B、Gemma 2 和 3 27B，并在以更少 FLOPs 训练的同时，缩小了与 Qwen 3 32B 等顶尖开放权重系统的差距（表 14）。


Source: p.37 S115

Original: 1. Data: Dolci Think — Building on prior open-source datasets (Guha et al., 2025a; Lambert et al., 2024; PrimeIntellect, 2025) inter alia, we introduce Dolci Think SFT, Dolci Think DPO, and Dolci Think RL, new cutting-edge post-training datasets designed to target a broad range of key capabilities such as math, coding, instruction following, and general conversation. The dataset includes synthetic examples with long thinking traces for supervised finetuning, high-contrast paired data for contrastive learning via preference optimization, and challenging prompts for reinforcement learning across diverse domains. Our data curation pipeline is shown in Figure 15. 2. Three-Stage training recipe — We employ a three-stage post-training process comprising Supervised Finetuning (SFT), Preference Finetuning via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and then Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). We observe consistent gains across all three stages, demonstrating the impact of careful data curation, algorithmic refinement, and infrastructure development. This contrasts with most recent prior work on open thinking models, which typically employs only a subset of these training stages. For example, we find that our RL framework yields greater improvements when applied after contrastive learning with DPO rather than directly following SFT (Figure 19).

中文: 1. 数据：Dolci Think——在先前开源数据集（Guha et al., 2025a; Lambert et al., 2024; PrimeIntellect, 2025）等基础上，我们提出 Dolci Think SFT、Dolci Think DPO 和 Dolci Think RL，这些是新的、前沿的后训练数据集，旨在覆盖广泛的关键能力，如数学、编码、指令遵循与通用对话。该数据集包括用于监督微调的、带长思维轨迹的合成示例；用于通过偏好优化进行对比学习的高对比配对数据；以及用于跨多种领域强化学习的挑战性提示。我们的数据整理流水线见图 15。2. 三阶段训练配方——我们采用三阶段后训练过程，包括监督微调（SFT）、通过直接偏好优化（DPO）的偏好微调，以及随后的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）。我们在所有三个阶段都观察到一致的收益，展示了精心数据整理、算法精炼与基础设施开发的影响。这与最近大多数关于开放思考模型的先前工作形成对比，后者通常只采用这些训练阶段的子集。例如，我们发现，在通过 DPO 做对比学习之后再应用 RL 框架，比直接紧随 SFT 应用能带来更大的改进（图 19）。


Source: p.37 S116

Original: 3. OlmoRL — We present OlmoRL, our RL training approach which builds upon GRPO and extends it with improvements from recent work. Additionally, we expand verifiable reasoning to multiple domains, going beyond the math and code settings typically explored in prior work. OlmoRL enables longer and more stable RL runs across diverse domains and increases the overall efficiency of training cycles (Section §4.4).

中文: 3. OlmoRL——我们提出 OlmoRL，我们的 RL 训练方法，它建立在 GRPO 之上，并用近期工作的改进加以扩展。此外，我们将可验证推理扩展到多个领域，超越了先前工作通常探索的数学与代码设置。OlmoRL 使得跨多种领域的 RL 运行能够更长久、更稳定地进行，并提高了训练循环的整体效率（第 §4.4 节）。

4.1 Main Results for Olmo 3 Think

4.1 Olmo 3 Think 的主要结果

4.1.1 Evaluation Details

4.1.1 评测细节


Source: p.37 S117

Original: We establish a suite of benchmarks to evaluate Olmo 3 post-trained models on math, reasoning, coding, precise instruction following, question answering, knowledge recall, and general chat. We expand upon the evaluation suite of OLMo 2 (OLMo et al., 2024) by adding new, more challenging benchmarks and removing saturated or noisy ones. Table 16 shows our evaluation benchmarks and describes the task configurations and metrics for the Olmo 3 post-training evaluation suite. We establish a standard evaluation configuration between all baseline models, including thinking and instruct models, to simplify comparisons. Namely, we follow Guo et al. (2025); Adler et al. (2024); Yang et al. (2025a) and use a 32K max context length, a sampling temperature of 0.6 and top-p of 0.95. Note, some models likely perform better with a higher inference budget, for instance K2 V2 (Team et al., 2025) use a 128K sequence length. Further details of our evaluation settings are provided in Appendix A.8.

中文: 我们建立了一套基准，在数学、推理、编码、精确指令遵循、问答、知识回忆与通用对话方面评测 Olmo 3 后训练模型。我们在 OLMo 2（OLMo et al., 2024）的评测套件基础上扩展，加入新的、更具挑战性的基准，并移除已饱和或噪声较大的基准。表 16 展示了我们的评测基准，并描述了 Olmo 3 后训练评测套件的任务配置与指标。我们在所有基线模型（包括思考与指令模型）之间建立统一的评测配置，以简化对比。即，我们遵循 Guo et al. (2025); Adler et al. (2024); Yang et al. (2025a)，使用 32K 最大上下文长度、0.6 的采样温度与 0.95 的 top-p。注意，一些模型可能在更高的推理预算下表现更好，例如 K2 V2（Team et al., 2025）使用 128K 序列长度。评测设置的更多细节见附录 A.8。


Source: p.37 S118

Original: Evaluation with reasoning models is both computationally expensive and often high variance. In our development of our recipe on versions of our 7B model—i.e., before the hyperparameter sweeps for final models—we find that evaluation costs between 10 and 20% of our compute budget. When compiling results, we measure the variance of every evaluation in our suite by taking the mean of the standard deviation from 3 runs of 14 models (both baselines and our final models). By taking the variance per model and then the average variance per evaluation, we can bucket the evaluations by their variance. We partition evaluations based on their variance as follows: High variance: GPQA: 1.4798, AlpacaEval 3: 1.2406, IFEval: 0.8835. Stable: ZebraLogic: 0.5638, Omega: 0.5579, AIME 24 (Avg@32): 0.5437, HumanEvalPlus: 0.4615, AgiEval: 0.4339, BigBenchHard: 0.3866. Very stable: LiveCodeBench (Avg@10): 0.2852, MBPPPlus: 0.2749, MATH: 0.2522, MMLU: 0.2219, PopQA: 0.1554.

中文: 用推理模型做评测既计算昂贵，又往往方差很大。在我们对 7B 模型各版本开发配方的过程中（即在对最终模型做超参数扫描之前），我们发现评测占用了我们算力预算的 10% 到 20%。在汇总结果时，我们通过取 14 个模型（包括基线与我们的最终模型）各 3 次运行标准差之均值，来衡量套件中每个评测的方差。通过先按模型取方差、再按评测取平均方差，我们可以按方差给评测分桶。我们按方差将评测划分如下：高方差：GPQA: 1.4798、AlpacaEval 3: 1.2406、IFEval: 0.8835。稳定：ZebraLogic: 0.5638、Omega: 0.5579、AIME 24 (Avg@32): 0.5437、HumanEvalPlus: 0.4615、AgiEval: 0.4339、BigBenchHard: 0.3866。非常稳定：LiveCodeBench (Avg@10): 0.2852、MBPPPlus: 0.2749、MATH: 0.2522、MMLU: 0.2219、PopQA: 0.1554。

4.1.2 主要结果


Source: p.38 S119

Original: Table 14 and Table 15 show the performance of Olmo 3 Think across different training stages and compare it with other baselines of similar scale on our benchmarks. As described before, Olmo 3 Think 32B is the best fully-open model at the 32B scale, outperforming other models including Gemma 2 27B, Gemma 3 27B, and Qwen 2.5 32B-Instruct. It narrows the gap to the best open-weight models at this scale, Qwen 3 and Qwen 3VL, while being trained with 6x fewer tokens. Similarly, Olmo 3 Think-7B outperforms OpenReasoning Nemotron 7B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and OpenThinker-7B, some of the best open-weight thinking models. In addition, it performs similarly to Nemotron-Nano-9B-v2 despite being smaller. At 7B, it lags the Qwen 3 series of models in knowledge tasks. We think that this is mainly due to the fact that Qwen 3 models are trained through distillation from Qwen’s largest model.

中文: 表 14 和表 15 展示了 Olmo 3 Think 在不同训练阶段的表现，并在我们的基准上与其他规模相近的基线对比。如前所述，Olmo 3 Think 32B 是 32B 规模上最好的全开放模型，超越了包括 Gemma 2 27B、Gemma 3 27B 和 Qwen 2.5 32B-Instruct 在内的其他模型。它在以少 6 倍令牌训练的同时，缩小了与该规模最佳开放权重模型 Qwen 3 和 Qwen 3VL 的差距。类似地，Olmo 3 Think-7B 超越了 OpenReasoning Nemotron 7B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 和 OpenThinker-7B 等一些最好的开放权重思考模型。此外，尽管规模更小，它的表现与 Nemotron-Nano-9B-v2 相当。在 7B 上，它在知识任务上落后于 Qwen 3 系列模型。我们认为这主要归因于 Qwen 3 模型是通过从 Qwen 最大模型蒸馏训练而来的。

4.2.1 Dolci Think SFT: Data Curation

4.2.1 Dolci Think SFT：数据整理


Source: p.39 S122

Original: To curate Dolci Think SFT, we compile a large collection of prompts across a diverse set of skills from other open efforts (e.g., Guha et al., 2025a; PrimeIntellect, 2025), substantially filter them, and synthetically generate reasoning traces for their completions. An overview of the Dolci Think SFT data mix is shown in Table 17 and is described below. Step 1: sourcing prompts and generating reasoning traces. Math — We source prompts from the math subsets of OpenThoughts3 (Guha et al., 2025a) and SYNTHETIC-2 (PrimeIntellect, 2025). For OpenThoughts3 prompts, we use all the available math prompts (maintaining the 16X repetition from the original) and the available reasoning traces with complete solutions. For incomplete traces, we generate full reasoning chains and solutions using QwQ-32B, the original model used for the completions, and the same generation settings as OpenThoughts3, except up to 32K tokens instead of the original 16K. We discard any examples that are still incomplete after regenerating. For SYNTHETIC-2, we take completions directly from the verified subsection.

中文: 为整理 Dolci Think SFT，我们从其他开放工作中（例如 Guha et al., 2025a; PrimeIntellect, 2025）汇集了一大批覆盖多种技能的提示词，对其进行大量过滤，并为其补全合成生成思维轨迹。Dolci Think SFT 数据混合的概览见表 17，描述如下。第 1 步：来源提示词与生成思维轨迹。数学——我们从 OpenThoughts3（Guha et al., 2025a）和 SYNTHETIC-2（PrimeIntellect, 2025）的数学子集获取提示词。对于 OpenThoughts3 提示词，我们使用所有可用的数学提示词（保持原始 16 倍重复）以及带完整解答的可用思维轨迹。对于不完整的轨迹，我们使用 QwQ-32B（原本用于补全的模型）和与 OpenThoughts3 相同的生成设置（除最多 32K 令牌而非原始 16K 外）生成完整的推理链与解答。我们丢弃重新生成后仍不完整的任何示例。对于 SYNTHETIC-2，我们直接采用已验证子集中的补全。


Source: p.41 S123

Original: Code — We collect code prompts from different sources and generate completions for them. To create Dolci Think Python Algorithms, we source prompts from AceCoder (Zeng et al., 2025a), the Python subset of The Algorithms (The Algorithms, 2025), Llama Nemotron Post-training (Bercovich et al., 2025), and OpenCodeReasoning (Ahmad et al., 2025), and then we generate up to 16 responses per prompt from QwQ-32B, which we filter for correctness using synthetically generated test cases from GPT-4.1. For OpenThoughts 3 code prompts, we downsample each prompt to at most 16 times and regenerate complete responses for all incomplete examples. We combine Dolci Think Python Algorithms with the code prompts from OpenThoughts3, downsample them to 16 repetitions, and regenerate completions for incomplete ones.

中文: 代码——我们从不同来源收集代码提示词，并为其生成补全。为创建 Dolci Think Python Algorithms，我们从 AceCoder（Zeng et al., 2025a）、The Algorithms 的 Python 子集（The Algorithms, 2025）、Llama Nemotron Post-training（Bercovich et al., 2025）和 OpenCodeReasoning（Ahmad et al., 2025）获取提示词，然后对每个提示词用 QwQ-32B 生成最多 16 个响应，并使用 GPT-4.1 合成生成的测试用例过滤其正确性。对于 OpenThoughts 3 代码提示词，我们将每个提示词下采样到至多 16 次，并为所有不完整的示例重新生成完整响应。我们将 Dolci Think Python Algorithms 与 OpenThoughts3 的代码提示词结合，下采样到 16 次重复，并为不完整者重新生成补全。


Source: p.41 S124

Original: Chat & safety — We source chat prompts from both the Tülu 3 (Lambert et al., 2024) subset of WildChat (Zhao et al., 2024a), as well as WildChat prompts not used during Tülu 3, and the Tülu 3 subset of OpenAssistant (Köpf et al., 2024). For safety, we reuse safety prompts used during Tülu 3. We then generate reasoning traces and completions from DeepSeek R1 (Guo et al., 2025). Precise instruction following — We source precise IF prompts from the overall Tülu 3 mix with additional verifiable constraints added from Pyatkin et al. (2025). We also regenerate Persona IF prompts as in Tülu 3, but with personas sourced from Meyer and Corneil (2025). We then generate responses for each prompt using QwQ-32B, and we verify responses using verifiers associated with each constraint, keeping only the correct responses.

中文: 对话与安全——我们从 Tülu 3（Lambert et al., 2024）的 WildChat（Zhao et al., 2024a）子集、以及 Tülu 3 未使用的 WildChat 提示词、和 Tülu 3 的 OpenAssistant（Köpf et al., 2024）子集中获取对话提示词。对于安全，我们复用 Tülu 3 期间使用的安全提示词。随后我们从 DeepSeek R1（Guo et al., 2025）生成思维轨迹与补全。精确指令遵循——我们从整体 Tülu 3 混合中获取精确 IF 提示词，并加入来自 Pyatkin et al. (2025) 的额外可验证约束。我们还如 Tülu 3 那样重新生成 Persona IF 提示词，但 persona 取自 Meyer and Corneil (2025)。随后我们用 QwQ-32B 为每个提示词生成响应，并使用与每个约束相关联的验证器验证响应，只保留正确响应。


Source: p.41 S125

Original: Science & other — We source science prompts from the OpenThoughts3 science subset. For other data sources, we include the TableGPT (Zha et al., 2023) subset in Tülu 3 for data transformation and Aya (Singh et al., 2024) for chat and basic multilinguality. We regenerate incomplete responses in OpenThoughts3 as we did for the math and code subsets, and we generate responses with reasoning chains for the other datasets using DeepSeek R1.

中文: 科学与其他——我们从 OpenThoughts3 的科学子集获取科学提示词。对于其他数据源，我们纳入 Tülu 3 中的 TableGPT（Zha et al., 2023）子集用于数据变换，纳入 Aya（Singh et al., 2024）用于对话与基础多语言能力。我们如对数学与代码子集那样，重新生成 OpenThoughts3 中不完整的响应，并使用 DeepSeek R1 为其他数据集生成带推理链的响应。


Source: p.41 S126

Original: Step 2: filtering — We perform extensive filtering on the data we have collected and generated. Heuristic filtering — We filter out examples with (1) non-commercial or unclear licenses, (2) incomplete reasoning chains, (3) domain-specific inaccuracies (i.e., verifying the constraint-adherence of instruction-following data or executing test cases against model completions for code), (4) mentions of other model developers and date cutoffs, (5) excessive repetition, and (6) an excessive number of Chinese characters or Chinese political values reflected in reasoning chains. Topic filtering — We classify our dataset by topic using the OpenAI query taxonomy (Chatterji et al., 2025), and find that filtering out and downsampling topics irrelevant to our model (e.g., requests to generate images or excessive basic greetings) from WildChat qualitatively improves model behavior. See Appendix A.7.1 for detailed descriptions and links to filter scripts.

中文: 第 2 步：过滤——我们对已收集与生成的数据进行大量过滤。启发式过滤——我们过滤掉具有以下情况的示例：（1）非商业或不明确的许可；（2）不完整的推理链；（3）领域特定的不准确之处（即验证指令遵循数据的约束遵守情况，或对代码的模型补全执行测试用例）；（4）提及其他模型开发者及日期截断；（5）过度重复；（6）推理链中反映的过多中文字符或中文政治价值观。主题过滤——我们使用 OpenAI 查询分类法（Chatterji et al., 2025）按主题对数据集分类，并发现从 WildChat 中过滤掉并对模型无关的主题（例如生成图像的请求、或过多的基础问候）做下采样，能定性改善模型行为。过滤脚本的详细描述与链接见附录 A.7.1。


Source: p.41 S127

Original: Step 3: data mixing — For data mixing, we follow a methodology similar to that described in the midtraining section (Section §3.5) for parallel data collection, adhering to shared standards for data mixing and conducting multiple rounds of integration testing. More specifically, we conduct careful experiments using a small “base” mix, consisting of 100K examples taken from our extended OpenThought 3 dataset. We found that this base mix was performant enough on key reasoning benchmarks to serve as a strong baseline, while saving substantial amounts of compute versus training on the full mix. We then train individual models on the base mix combined with up to 100K training examples (without upsampling) from each category to observe the impact on our evaluation suite. As shown in Table 18, we generally find that each dataset is helpful on at least one evaluation, and so our final mix includes at least a portion of each dataset we tested.

中文: 第 3 步：数据混合——对于数据混合，我们遵循与中期训练一节（第 §3.5 节）所述的并行数据收集类似的方法，遵守数据混合的共享标准，并进行多轮集成测试。更具体地说，我们使用一个由取自扩展 OpenThought 3 数据集的 100K 示例组成的小型「基础」混合进行仔细实验。我们发现这一基础混合在关键推理基准上表现足够好，可作为强基线，同时相比在完整混合上训练节省了大量算力。随后，我们在基础混合上，结合每个类别最多 100K 训练示例（不做上采样），分别训练模型，以观察对我们评测套件的影响。如表 18 所示，我们大体发现每个数据集至少在一项评测上有帮助，因此我们的最终混合至少包含我们所测试的每个数据集的一部分。


Source: p.41 S128

Original: Step 4: decontamination — We followed the recommended settings from the Tülu 3 Decontamination Procedure and toolkit (Lambert et al., 2024) to filter out the portions of all post-training data (all three stages) that matched the evaluation sets. We used n-gram matching with 8-grams and an overlap threshold of 0.5 (i.e., at least 50% of the n-grams in the test instance match a training instance) for filtering. We developed additional heuristics to mitigate false positives: (1) we ignored matches of task-irrelevant chunks of text, e.g., common generic phrases, with the irrelevance determined per task based on manual inspection; (2) particularly in math datasets, we ignored matches of n-grams where most of the tokens are of length 1 (typically math symbols).

中文: 第 4 步：去污染——我们遵循 Tülu 3 去污染流程与工具包（Lambert et al., 2024）的推荐设置，过滤掉所有后训练数据（三个阶段）中与评测集匹配的部分。我们使用 8-gram 的 n-gram 匹配与 0.5 的重叠阈值（即测试实例中至少 50% 的 n-gram 与某个训练实例匹配）进行过滤。我们开发了额外的启发式规则以减少误报：（1）我们忽略任务无关文本块（例如常见通用短语）的匹配，其无关性按任务通过人工检查确定；（2）尤其在数学数据集中，我们忽略大多数令牌长度为 1（通常是数学符号）的 n-gram 匹配。

4.2.2 Training

4.2.2 训练


Source: p.42 S129

Original: For supervised finetuning, we switch from Open Instruct to OLMo-core, resulting in an 8× increase in training throughput. See Appendix A.6.1 for more information about our training settings and hyperparameters. We train all models for two epochs to avoid overfitting, and perform a learning-rate sweep to select the best candidate checkpoints based on our evaluation suite. We then test each candidate checkpoint with a series of qualitative “vibe-test” questions to inform our final checkpoint selection. Finally, we explore model souping (Wortsman et al., 2022; Morrison et al., 2024), and our final thinking SFT checkpoint is a linearly weighted merge of two checkpoints trained with different learning rates, merged with mergekit (Goddard et al., 2024).

中文: 对于监督微调，我们从 Open Instruct 切换到 OLMo-core，使训练吞吐量提升了 8 倍。关于训练设置与超参数的更多信息见附录 A.6.1。我们训练所有模型两个 epoch 以避免过拟合，并执行学习率扫描，以根据我们的评测套件选择最佳候选检查点。随后我们用一系列定性的「vibe-test」问题测试每个候选检查点，以指导最终检查点的选择。最后，我们探索了模型「汤」（Wortsman et al., 2022; Morrison et al., 2024），最终思考 SFT 检查点是用不同学习率训练的两个检查点的线性加权合并，使用 mergekit（Goddard et al., 2024）合并。

4.3 Preference Tuning with Delta Learning

4.3 Delta 学习偏好微调


Source: p.42 S130

Original: Prior work in general post-training has positioned preference tuning primarily as a means to improve alignment with human values and preferences (Lambert et al., 2024; Lambert, 2025). Hence, most recent efforts in building capability-oriented thinking models (Guha et al., 2025a; Ahmad et al., 2025) have not incorporated preference tuning (one exception is SmolLM3; Bakouch et al. (2025)). We rethink preference tuning as a stage of contrastive learning that drives capability gains beyond what SFT alone can provide. We introduce Dolci Think DPO, a preference dataset containing completion pairs with clear capability deltas. We leverage these relative contrasts to enhance the model’s reasoning capabilities via preference optimization, extending the ideas from Delta Learning (Geng et al., 2025).

中文: 先前通用后训练领域的工作主要将偏好调优定位为改进与人类价值观和偏好对齐的手段（Lambert et al., 2024; Lambert, 2025）。因此，最近大多数构建能力导向思考模型的工作（Guha et al., 2025a; Ahmad et al., 2025）并未纳入偏好调优（一个例外是 SmolLM3；Bakouch et al. (2025)）。我们重新思考偏好调优，将其视为一个对比学习阶段，能带来超越 SFT 单独所能提供的能力收益。我们提出 Dolci Think DPO，一个包含具有清晰能力差距的补全对（completion pairs）的偏好数据集。我们利用这些相对对比，通过偏好优化增强模型的推理能力，扩展了 Delta Learning（Geng et al., 2025）的思想。


Source: p.42 S131

Original: In particular, we find that further supervised finetuning on thinking traces generated by Qwen3 32B (one of the few open-thought models) outright hurts the performance of Olmo 3 Think SFT, indicating that we are approaching saturation on learning from imitation. To extract a useful training signal out of these now-ineffective completions, we apply Delta Learning’s principle by pairing these completions with even worse responses (Geng et al., 2025); minimizing the quality of the rejected completions (thus increasing the quality delta) yields a useful contrastive signal for preference tuning. With these insights in mind, we construct Dolci Think DPO, which we use to improve the model’s performance across a wide range of benchmarks. We use Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) (Rafailov et al., 2024) for training with pairwise data. Details of DPO training are provided in Appendix A.6.2.

中文: 尤其是，我们发现对 Qwen3 32B（少数开放思考模型之一）生成的思维轨迹做进一步的监督微调，反而直接损害了 Olmo 3 Think SFT 的性能，这表明我们正接近从模仿中学习的饱和点。为从这些现已失效的补全中提取有用的训练信号，我们应用 Delta Learning 的原则，将这些补全与更差的响应配对（Geng et al., 2025）；通过最小化被拒绝补全的质量（从而增大质量差距）来产生有用的对比信号用于偏好调优。带着这些洞见，我们构建了 Dolci Think DPO，用它来改进模型在广泛基准上的性能。我们使用直接偏好优化（DPO）（Rafailov et al., 2024）进行成对数据的训练。DPO 训练的细节见附录 A.6.2。


Source: p.42 S132

Original: Delta Learning — The intuition behind delta-learning is that the quality of preference data depends primarily on the quality of the delta between chosen and rejected responses rather than the quality of either response individually. By constructing preference pairs (x, yc, yr) that exhibit capability-relevant contrasts with yc ≻ yr, tuning to prefer yc over yr can improve the model even when supervised finetuning on yc would not help or even actively hurt (Geng et al., 2025; D’Oosterlinck et al., 2025; Kim et al., 2025).

中文: Delta 学习——delta 学习背后的直觉是：偏好数据的质量主要取决于「被选择」与「被拒绝」响应之间差距的质量，而非任一响应单独的质量。通过构造展示出与能力相关对比的偏好对（x, yc, yr），且 yc ≻ yr，即使对 yc 做监督微调无益甚至有害，调优以偏好 yc 而非 yr 仍能改进模型（Geng et al., 2025; D’Oosterlinck et al., 2025; Kim et al., 2025）。

4.3.1 Dolci Think-DPO: Preference Data Creation

4.3.1 Dolci Think-DPO：偏好数据创建


Source: p.43 S133

Original: To construct Dolci Think DPO, we compile a large pool of prompts covering a wide range of datasets and skills (see Table 19) and synthesize chosen and rejected responses to exhibit capability deltas. Following the delta-learning heuristic (Geng et al., 2025), for each prompt x, we simply decode a chosen completion yc from one model (Qwen 3 32B, thinking) and a rejected completion yr from an overall weaker model (Qwen 3 0.6B, thinking) to construct a consistent contrast. Step 1: sourcing prompts and contrastive completions — Olmo 3 Think focuses on reasoning capabilities; we thus construct pairs that exhibit a delta in reasoning quality by pairing model completions from models of differing reasoning capability (Geng et al., 2025; Bakouch et al., 2025; Kim et al., 2023). Our prompt pool is derived from the Dolci Instruct SFT dataset supplemented with the DaringAnteater (Wang et al., 2024b) and UltraFeedback (Cui et al., 2023) subsets from the OLMo 2 7B preference dataset.

中文: 为构建 Dolci Think DPO，我们汇编了一个覆盖广泛数据集与技能的提示词大池（见表 19），并合成「被选择」与「被拒绝」响应以展示能力差距。遵循 delta 学习启发式（Geng et al., 2025），对每个提示词 x，我们简单地从较强模型（Qwen 3 32B，思考模式）解码出一个被选择补全 yc，从整体较弱的模型（Qwen 3 0.6B，思考模式）解码出一个被拒绝补全 yr，从而构造一致的对比。第 1 步：来源提示词与对比补全——Olmo 3 Think 聚焦推理能力；因此，我们通过将来自不同推理能力模型的补全配对，来构造展示推理质量差距的配对（Geng et al., 2025; Bakouch et al., 2025; Kim et al., 2023）。我们的提示词池来自 Dolci Instruct SFT 数据集，并补充以 OLMo 2 7B 偏好数据集中的 DaringAnteater（Wang et al., 2024b）和 UltraFeedback（Cui et al., 2023）子集。


Source: p.43 S134

Original: Step 2: filtering — We apply topic filtering and heuristic model-identity filtering as described from the SFT stage (Section §4.2.1) to all chosen responses. We leave rejected responses unfiltered with the intuition that an incorrect rejected response may elicit a useful contrast. We further decontaminate all prompts against our evaluation suites. Step 3: mixing — Experimentation with long reasoning traces is significantly more expensive than with non-thinking completions. To obtain the final mix of prompts for Dolci Think DPO, we leverage mixing experiments conducted on prompts with non-thinking completions (see Section §5 for details). Specifically, we select the three best-performing prompt distributions from our Olmo 3 Instruct experiments and generate chosen and rejected responses for these prompts using the thinking versions of the Qwen models to elicit a delta in reasoning quality. We choose the empirically best-performing mix during our experiments as our final DPO data pool.

中文: 第 2 步：过滤——我们对所有被选择响应应用如 SFT 阶段（第 §4.2.1 节）所述的主题过滤与启发式模型身份过滤。我们让被拒绝响应保持不过滤，其直觉是：一个错误的被拒绝响应可能引出一个有用的对比。我们还对所有提示词针对评测套件做进一步去污染。第 3 步：混合——用长思维轨迹做实验比用非思考补全显著昂贵。为得到 Dolci Think DPO 提示词的最终混合，我们利用在非思考补全提示词上进行的混合实验（见第 §5 节）。具体而言，我们从 Olmo 3 Instruct 实验中选出三个表现最佳的提示词分布，并使用 Qwen 模型的思考版本为这些提示词生成被选择与被拒绝响应，以引出推理质量的差距。我们选择实验中经验表现最佳的混合作为最终 DPO 数据池。

4.3.2 Training

4.3.2 训练


Source: p.44 S135

Original: We train all models for one epoch following previous work (Lambert et al., 2024), sweeping learning rate and dataset size to identify the best candidate checkpoints based on our evaluation suite. Dataset size is an important hyperparameter, as we observe that early stopping is important for performant preference tuning; please see our data mixing experiments on our Instruct model (Section §5.3.2) for our motivating results. Beyond our evaluation suite, we further inspect each checkpoint via the same “vibe-tests” as in SFT training to qualitatively assess model behavior. See Appendix A.6.2 for full training settings.

中文: 我们遵循先前工作（Lambert et al., 2024），将所有模型训练一个 epoch，并扫描学习率与数据集大小，以根据我们的评测套件确定最佳候选检查点。数据集大小是一个重要的超参数，因为我们观察到早停对有效的偏好调优很重要；请参阅我们在 Instruct 模型上的数据混合实验（第 §5.3.2 节）以了解动机性结果。除评测套件外，我们还通过 SFT 训练中相同的「vibe-tests」检查每个检查点，以定性评估模型行为。完整训练设置见附录 A.6.2。

4.4 Reinforcement Learning with OlmoRL: The Cherry on Top

4.4 OlmoRL 强化学习：锦上添花


Source: p.44 S136

Original: The third stage of post-training is reinforcement learning with a mixture of verifiable and LM-judge rewards across a variety of domains. We introduce OlmoRL, which includes our algorithm and closely intertwined engineering infrastructure to address challenges for reinforcement learning with long reasoning traces, extending RLVR to include a wider variety of verifiable tasks. We also release Dolci-Think-RL—a large-scale and diverse dataset of roughly 100K prompts across four domains: mathematics, coding, instruction following, and general chat—to support robust reinforcement learning on varied reasoning tasks while maintaining general utility. Next, we describe the RL algorithmic details (§4.4.1), the Dolci Think RL dataset (§4.4.2), and finally OlmoRL infrastructure in Open Instruct (§4.4.3).

中文: 后训练的第三阶段是在多种领域上、以可验证奖励与 LM 评判奖励相混合的强化学习。我们提出 OlmoRL，它包含我们的算法与紧密交织的工程基础设施，以应对长思维轨迹强化学习的挑战，将 RLVR 扩展到更多种类的可验证任务。我们还发布 Dolci-Think-RL——一个大规模、多样化、约 100K 提示词的数据集，覆盖数学、编码、指令遵循与通用对话四个领域——以在多样化的推理任务上支持稳健的强化学习，同时保持通用效用。接下来，我们描述 RL 算法细节（§4.4.1）、Dolci Think RL 数据集（§4.4.2），以及 Open Instruct 中的 OlmoRL 基础设施（§4.4.3）。

4.4.1 OlmoRL Algorithmic Details

4.4.1 OlmoRL 算法细节


Source: p.44 S137

Original: Our reinforcement learning stage is powered by OlmoRL, an approach that builds on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) (Shao et al., 2024) and integrates a number of recent algorithmic advances. In particular, we adopt improvements from DAPO (Yu et al., 2025) and Dr GRPO (Liu et al., 2025b), among others (Yao et al., 2025; Piché et al., 2025). At its core, the objective of RLVR is to maximize the expected reward of a model-generated response y given the prompt x, where a verifier checks whether the response y matches the ground-truth answer associated with x. We make the following improvements over vanilla GRPO:

中文: 我们的强化学习阶段由 OlmoRL 驱动，这一方法建立在组相对策略优化（GRPO）（Shao et al., 2024）之上，并整合了若干近期的算法进展。特别是，我们采纳了来自 DAPO（Yu et al., 2025）和 Dr GRPO（Liu et al., 2025b）等的改进（Yao et al., 2025; Piché et al., 2025）。其核心上，RLVR 的目标是最大化模型生成的响应 y 在给定提示词 x 下的期望奖励，其中验证器检查响应 y 是否与 x 关联的真实答案匹配。我们在 vanilla GRPO 之上做了如下改进：


Source: p.45 S138

Original: (i) Zero gradient signal filtering — We remove groups of instances whose rewards are all identical (i.e., a batch with zero standard deviation in their advantage) to avoid training on samples that provide zero gradient, similar to DAPO (Yu et al., 2025). (ii) Active sampling — We maintain a consistent batch size in spite of zero gradient filtering with a novel, more efficient version of dynamic sampling (Yu et al., 2025), see Section §4.4.3 for details. (iii) Token-level loss — We use a token-level loss to normalize the loss by the total number of tokens across the batch (Yu et al., 2025), rather than per-sample to avoid a length bias. (iv) No KL loss — We remove the KL loss as a common practice (GLM-4.5 Team et al., 2025; Yu et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2025b) as it allows less-restricted policy updates, and removing it does not lead to over-optimization or destabilized training. (v) Clip higher — We set the upper-bound clipping term in the loss to a slightly higher value than the lower bound to enable larger updates on tokens, as proposed by Yu et al. (2025). (vi) Truncated importance sampling — To adjust for differences between log probabilities from the inference and training engines, we multiply the loss by the truncated importance sampling ratio, following Yao et al. (2025). (vii) No standard deviation normalization — When calculating advantage, we do not normalize by the standard deviation of the group, following Liu et al. (2025b). This removes a difficulty bias, where questions with low standard deviation in their rewards (e.g., too hard or too easy) have their advantages significantly increased by the normalization term.

中文: （i）零梯度信号过滤——我们移除奖励完全相同（即优势标准差为零的批次）的实例组，以避免在提供零梯度的样本上训练，与 DAPO（Yu et al., 2025）类似。（ii）主动采样——尽管有零梯度过滤，我们仍通过一种新颖的、更高效版本的动态采样（Yu et al., 2025）维持一致的批量大小，细节见第 §4.4.3 节。（iii）令牌级损失——我们使用令牌级损失，按批次中令牌总数对损失做归一化（Yu et al., 2025），而非按样本归一化，以避免长度偏差。（iv）无 KL 损失——我们按通行做法（GLM-4.5 Team et al., 2025; Yu et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2025b）移除了 KL 损失，因为它允许更少受限的策略更新，且移除它不会导致过度优化或训练不稳定。（v）更高截断上界（clip higher）——如 Yu et al. (2025) 所提议，我们将损失中截断项的上界设为略高于下界，以允许对令牌进行更大的更新。（vi）截断重要性采样——为校正推理引擎与训练引擎之间对数概率的差异，我们将损失乘以截断重要性采样比率，遵循 Yao et al. (2025)。（vii）无标准差归一化——在计算优势时，我们不对组的标准差做归一化，遵循 Liu et al. (2025b)。这消除了难度偏差，即奖励标准差低的问题（例如过难或过易）其优势会被归一化项显著放大。


Source: p.45 S139

Original: OlmoRL formulation — Our final objective function includes a token-level loss, truncated importance sampling, clip-higher, and no standard deviation in the advantage calculation:

中文: OlmoRL 公式——我们的最终目标函数包含令牌级损失、截断重要性采样、更高的截断上界，以及优势计算中不做标准差归一化：


Source: p.45 E001 · Eq. (1) · medium confidence

低置信度转写（请以原图为准）/ Low-confidence transcription:

$$
J(\theta)=\frac{1}{\sum_{i=1}^{G}|y_i|}\sum_{i=1}^{G}\sum_{t=1}^{|y_i|}\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\mathrm{vllm}}(y_{i,t}\mid x,y_{i,<t};\theta_{\mathrm{old}})}{\pi(y_{i,t}\mid x,y_{i,<t};\theta_{\mathrm{old}})},\ \rho\right)\ \min\left(r_{i,t}A_{i,t},\ \mathrm{clip}\left(r_{i,t},\ 1-\varepsilon_{\mathrm{low}},\ 1+\varepsilon_{\mathrm{high}}\right)A_{i,t}\right)
$$

中文说明： 该式为 OlmoRL 的训练目标；$r_{i,t}=\dfrac{\pi(y_{i,t}\mid x,y_{i,<t};\theta)}{\pi_{\mathrm{vllm}}(y_{i,t}\mid x,y_{i,<t};\theta_{\mathrm{old}})}$ 为重要性采样比率，$\varepsilon_{\mathrm{low}}$、$\varepsilon_{\mathrm{high}}$ 为截断超参数，$\rho$ 为截断重要性采样上限。式中符号与变量名保持原样，不做翻译。


Source: p.45 S140

Original: where $r_{i,t} = \pi(y_{i,t}|x,y_{i,<t};\theta)/\pi_{vllm}(y_{i,t}|x,y_{i,<t};\theta_{old})$, $\varepsilon_{low}$ and $\varepsilon_{high}$ are the clipping hyperparameters. Here, $y_i \sim \pi_{vllm}(\cdot|x;\theta_{old})$ and $\pi_{vllm}(\cdot|x;\theta_{old})$ are the token probabilities returned from vLLM, $\rho$ is the truncated importance sampling cap value (Yao et al., 2025), and the advantage $A_{i,t}$ for the t-th token in the response $y_i$ is calculated within the group G based on the relative reward of the outputs inside each group:

中文: 其中 $r_{i,t} = \pi(y_{i,t}|x,y_{i,<t};\theta)/\pi_{vllm}(y_{i,t}|x,y_{i,<t};\theta_{old})$，$\varepsilon_{low}$ 与 $\varepsilon_{high}$ 是截断超参数。此处，$y_i \sim \pi_{vllm}(\cdot|x;\theta_{old})$，$\pi_{vllm}(\cdot|x;\theta_{old})$ 是 vLLM 返回的令牌概率，$\rho$ 是截断重要性采样上限值（Yao et al., 2025），而响应 $y_i$ 中第 t 个令牌的优势 $A_{i,t}$ 是在组 G 内基于组内输出的相对奖励计算而来：


Source: p.45 E002 · Eq. (2) · medium confidence

低置信度转写（请以原图为准）/ Low-confidence transcription:

$$
A_{i,t}=r(x,y_i)-\mathrm{mean}\left({r(x,y_i)}_{i=1}^{G}\right)
$$

中文说明： $r(x,y_i)$ 是相应验证器返回的奖励分数；优势即「组内奖励减去组内奖励均值」。符号保持原样。


Source: p.45 S141

Original: $r(x, y_i)$ is the reward score returned by the corresponding verifier. Our hyperparameters for various runs are in Appendix Table 49. Verifiers — We extend verifiable rewards beyond math domains from OLMo 2 to include general domains. For each domain we use a different custom verifier (see Figure 16): Math — We use a rule-based verifier that performs basic normalization and compares with a reference answer with SymPy to determine answer correctness. The verifier returns 1 if the answer is determined the same as the reference answer and 0 otherwise. Code — We use a test-case based verifier that runs a set of test cases over the response. We experiment with (a) using the percentage of passed test cases as the reward and (b) returning 1 when the response passes all test cases and 0 otherwise.

中文: $r(x, y_i)$ 是相应验证器返回的奖励分数。各运行所用的超参数见附录表 49。验证器——我们将可验证奖励从 OLMo 2 的数学领域扩展至通用领域。对每个领域，我们使用不同的定制验证器（见图 16）：数学——我们使用基于规则的验证器，执行基本归一化，并用 SymPy 与参考答案比较以判定答案正确性。若答案被判定为与参考答案相同，验证器返回 1，否则返回 0。代码——我们使用基于测试用例的验证器，对响应运行一组测试用例。我们实验了（a）用通过的测试用例百分比作为奖励，和（b）当响应通过所有测试用例时返回 1、否则返回 0。

Fig. 16. 可验证与不可验证任务的验证器与奖励设计

Placed near: p.45 S141
Source: p.44 C026

Original caption: Verifiers and reward design for verifiable and non-verifiable tasks.

中文图注: 可验证与不可验证任务的验证器与奖励设计。

Reading note: 该图展示四类任务（指令遵循、数学、编码、通用对话）各自的验证方式：可验证任务用单元测试通过率/等价检查器等规则验证器，不可验证任务用 LLM-as-a-judge 打分。


Source: p.46 S142

Original: Instruction-following — We pass the response through a set of functions that check adherence to a series of constraints from the prompt. A reward of 1 is assigned if all constraints are satisfied, and 0 otherwise. Chat—reference — For tasks with a ground-truth response, we pass the response to an LM judge to compare the model’s response against a provided reference answer, and ask the judge to give a score in [0, 1] based on the quality of the response. Chat—open-ended — We pass the response to an LM judge and ask the judge to give a score in [0, 1] based on the quality of the response without any reference answer.

中文: 指令遵循——我们将响应传入一组检查是否遵守提示词中一系列约束的函数。若所有约束均满足，则赋奖励 1，否则为 0。对话—有参考——对于具有真实答案的任务，我们将响应传给 LM 评判器，将模型响应与提供的参考答案比较，并让评判器基于响应质量给出 [0, 1] 区间的分数。对话—开放——我们将响应传给 LM 评判器，让评判器在没有任何参考答案的情况下，基于响应质量给出 [0, 1] 区间的分数。

4.4.2 Dolci-Think-RL: Curating a State-of-the-art RLVR Dataset

4.4.2 Dolci-Think-RL：整理最先进的 RLVR 数据集


Source: p.46 S143

Original: We curate a large-scale and diverse dataset of roughly 100K samples across four domains: mathematics, coding, instruction following, and general chat to support robust RL on varied reasoning tasks while maintaining general utility. Each domain is associated with either a verifiable or non-verifiable reward signal (continuous or binary), ensuring that every instance can be automatically checked for correctness or general quality (see Figure 16). For all domains we take particular care with the provenance and licensing of sources. We provide the size of each dataset subsection after sourcing, filtering, and mixing in Table 20. Step 1: sourcing prompts. In what follows, we will describe our data construction process.

中文: 我们整理了一个大规模、多样化、约 100K 样本的数据集，覆盖数学、编码、指令遵循与通用对话四个领域，以在多样化推理任务上支持稳健的 RL，同时保持通用效用。每个领域关联一个可验证或不可验证的奖励信号（连续或二元），确保每个实例都能被自动检查正确性或通用质量（见图 16）。对所有领域，我们都特别关注来源的出处与许可。表 20 提供了每个数据子集在来源、过滤与混合之后的大小。第 1 步：来源提示词。下面我们描述数据构建过程。


Source: p.46 S144

Original: Math — We combine community-curated math problems, including Open-Reasoner-Zero (Hu et al., 2025), DAPO-Math (Yu et al., 2025), AceReason-Math (Chen et al., 2025), DeepScaler (Luo et al., 2025b), KlearReasoner-MathSub (Su et al., 2025c), and OMEGA (Sun et al., 2025) covering a wide range of mathematical domains including algebra, combinatorics, number theory, and geometry. Coding — To construct RL data for code, we required pairs of (problem, test cases). We curate a diverse set of prompts for coding problems, including AceCoder (Zeng et al., 2025a), Klear-Reasoner Code (Su et al., 2025c), Nemotron Post-training Code (NVIDIA AI, 2025), SYNTHETIC-2 code (PrimeIntellect, 2025), and Open-Code Reasoner (Ahmad et al., 2025). We use the Klear-Reasoner and SYNTHETIC-2 test cases directly. For the other datasets, we run prompts through the following synthetic data pipeline: (1) problem rewriting, (2) solution generation, and (3) test case generation. After generating these triplets (problem, solution, test cases), we executed all model-generated or rewritten test cases against the corresponding solutions and kept examples with solutions that passed more than 80% of test cases while removing failed test cases. The resulting filtered dataset provided high-quality (problem, test cases) pairs suitable for training and experimentation with RL methods for code.

中文: 数学——我们组合社区整理的数学题，包括 Open-Reasoner-Zero（Hu et al., 2025）、DAPO-Math（Yu et al., 2025）、AceReason-Math（Chen et al., 2025）、DeepScaler（Luo et al., 2025b）、KlearReasoner-MathSub（Su et al., 2025c）和 OMEGA（Sun et al., 2025），覆盖代数、组合数学、数论与几何等广泛的数学领域。编码——为构建代码的 RL 数据，我们需要（问题，测试用例）对。我们整理了多样化的编码问题提示词集合，包括 AceCoder（Zeng et al., 2025a）、Klear-Reasoner Code（Su et al., 2025c）、Nemotron Post-training Code（NVIDIA AI, 2025）、SYNTHETIC-2 code（PrimeIntellect, 2025）和 Open-Code Reasoner（Ahmad et al., 2025）。我们直接使用 Klear-Reasoner 和 SYNTHETIC-2 的测试用例。对于其他数据集，我们将提示词经过如下合成数据流水线：（1）问题重写，（2）解答生成，（3）测试用例生成。在生成这些三元组（问题、解答、测试用例）之后，我们将所有模型生成或重写的测试用例针对相应解答执行，并保留解答通过超过 80% 测试用例的示例，同时移除失败的测试用例。由此得到的过滤数据集提供了高质量的（问题，测试用例）对，适合用于代码 RL 方法的训练与实验。


Source: p.47 S145

Original: Instruction-following — We use the prompts from IF-RLVR (Pyatkin et al., 2025) with up to 5 constraints, which are sampled from IFEval (Zhou et al., 2023) and IFBench-Train (Pyatkin et al., 2025). General chat — We sample our general chat instances from three sources: (a) Tülu 3 SFT (Lambert et al., 2024); (b) the new WildChat-4.8M data containing a broad spectrum of user-chatbot interactions on ambiguous requests, code-switching, topic shifts, political debates, and more; and © the Multi-subject-RLVR dataset (Su et al., 2025b), consisting of college-level English questions and objective answers written by domain experts for examination purposes. For WildChat, we only sample from instances that are in English and do not require reasoning (such as math and code). For Tülu 3, we first rewrote samples using GPT-4.1 for better clarity and to extract reference answers from the SFT set. We then generated eight samples per prompt with a Qwen 2.5 7B model finetuned on OpenThoughts 2 and computed the F1 score between the reference answer and each response. We then removed all samples with average F1 score < 0.1 and > 0.8. This removes both noisy and overly difficult samples.

中文: 指令遵循——我们使用来自 IF-RLVR（Pyatkin et al., 2025）的提示词，最多含 5 个约束，采样自 IFEval（Zhou et al., 2023）和 IFBench-Train（Pyatkin et al., 2025）。通用对话——我们从三个来源采样通用对话实例：（a）Tülu 3 SFT（Lambert et al., 2024）；（b）新的 WildChat-4.8M 数据，包含关于模糊请求、语码切换、话题转换、政治辩论等的广泛用户-聊天机器人交互；（c）Multi-subject-RLVR 数据集（Su et al., 2025b），由领域专家为考试目的撰写的大学水平英文问题与客观答案组成。对于 WildChat，我们仅从英文且无需推理（如数学与代码）的实例中采样。对于 Tülu 3，我们先用 GPT-4.1 重写样本以提高清晰度并从 SFT 集合中提取参考答案。随后我们用针对 OpenThoughts 2 微调的 Qwen 2.5 7B 模型为每个提示词生成 8 个样本，并计算参考答案与每个响应之间的 F1 分数。然后移除平均 F1 分数 < 0.1 和 > 0.8 的所有样本。这同时移除了噪声样本与过难样本。


Source: p.47 S146

Original: Step 2: offline difficulty filtering — As stated previously, to improve the sample efficiency of RL for our reasoner model, we generate eight rollouts for each prompt from the initial checkpoint of the model we train (e.g., if starting from the DPO-trained model, we generate from the DPO checkpoint). We then remove all samples that the model easily solves (that is, those with a pass rate greater than 62.5%). We sample with a temperature of 1.0 and top-p of 1.0, matching how we sample during RL training. We used offline filtering for the 7B Olmo 3 Think to filter out RL problems that are too easy for our models’ training. For the 32B, we rely on active sampling, which fills RL batches only on samples with a non-zero GRPO group gradient, and re-using the 7B DPO-filtered data as the starting point for the model due to compute and time constraints.

中文: 第 2 步：离线难度过滤——如前所述，为提高推理模型 RL 的样本效率，我们从所训练模型的初始检查点（例如，若从 DPO 训练模型开始，则从 DPO 检查点生成）为每个提示词生成 8 个 rollout。随后移除模型轻松解出的所有样本（即通过率大于 62.5% 者）。我们以 1.0 的温度与 1.0 的 top-p 采样，与 RL 训练时的采样方式一致。我们对 7B Olmo 3 Think 使用离线过滤，以过滤掉对模型训练而言过易的 RL 问题。对于 32B，我们依赖主动采样，仅以具有非零 GRPO 组梯度的样本填充 RL 批次，并因算力与时间限制，将 7B 的 DPO 过滤数据复用为模型的起点。


Source: p.47 S147

Original: Step 3: data mixing — When developing our data mixture and overall recipe, we found RL experiments were both long and compute-expensive, preventing us from ablating the full space of datasets and algorithmic choices. Instead, we established a pipeline in which: (a) we performed dataset-specific runs on an intermediate SFT checkpoint and observed downstream evaluation trends over the first 500-1000 RL steps; (b) focused on math domain training when testing new algorithmic changes; © periodically ran overall mixture experiments to ensure mixing was stable. When setting up our final run, we then took the most promising datasets, performed offline filtering, and carefully mixed them to ensure higher-quality datasets were upweighted, and roughly equal amounts of data were used for each domain (with slightly more focus on math and instruction following, as training on these domains seemed the most effective in per-dataset runs).

中文: 第 3 步：数据混合——在开发数据混合与整体配方时，我们发现 RL 实验既耗时又算力昂贵，这使我们无法对数据集与算法选择的完整空间做消融。取而代之，我们建立了一条流水线，其中：（a）我们在一个中间 SFT 检查点上执行数据集专属运行，并观察前 500–1000 RL 步的下游评测趋势；（b）在测试新算法改动时聚焦数学领域训练；（c）定期运行整体混合实验以确保混合稳定。在设置最终运行时，我们采用最有前景的数据集，执行离线过滤，并仔细混合它们以确保更高质量的数据集被上调权重，且每个领域使用大致等量的数据（对数学与指令遵循略有侧重，因为在逐数据集运行中，对这些领域训练似乎最有效）。

4.4.3 OlmoRL Infrastructure in Open Instruct

4.4.3 Open Instruct 中的 OlmoRL 基础设施


Source: p.48 S148

Original: We made substantial improvements to our reinforcement learning infrastructure to handle longer sequences and faster overall throughput. In RL with LLMs, the key technical challenge for finetuning models that generate long sequences is managing inference—also called the rollouts. For our final models, we generated rollouts with a maximum size of 32K tokens in length, averaging more than 10K tokens (for the reasoner models). Inference dominated our computational costs, using 8 H100 nodes for training and 20 nodes for inference for the 32B OlmoRL reasoner model. Given the cost of autoregressive inference, our learner spends 75% of the time waiting for data, so in terms of GPU utilization, we use approximately 5x as much for inference as for training. In fact, we use the minimal possible sharding configuration to fit the learner in memory and do not prioritize speed at all, unlike in the supervised learning setting. For the 7B reasoner model, where we have less memory pressure on the learner, the situation was more dramatic, as we used 7 nodes for inference and only 2 for the learner. Given the similarly low utilization of the learner, we used approximately 14x as much compute for inference as for training. We suspect that we have a suboptimal sharding configuration for the 32B learner and expect that we could do better in future work.

中文: 我们对强化学习基础设施做了大量改进，以处理更长序列并提高整体吞吐。在用 LLM 做 RL 时，微调生成长序列的模型所面临的关键技术挑战是管理推理——也称为 rollout。对于我们的最终模型，我们生成的 rollout 最大长度为 32K 令牌，平均超过 10K 令牌（对推理模型而言）。推理主导了我们的算力成本：32B OlmoRL 推理模型使用 8 个 H100 节点训练、20 个节点推理。鉴于自回归推理的成本，我们的学习器 75% 的时间都在等待数据，因此就 GPU 利用率而言，我们用于推理的算力大约是训练的 5 倍。事实上，我们使用尽可能最小的分片配置让学习器装进内存，完全不像监督学习场景那样优先速度。对于 7B 推理模型，由于学习器的内存压力较小，情况更为极端：我们使用 7 个节点推理、仅 2 个节点做学习器。鉴于学习器同样低的利用率，我们用于推理的算力大约是训练的 14 倍。我们怀疑 32B 学习器的分片配置并非最优，并预期未来工作能做得更好。


Source: p.48 S149

Original: Fully asynchronous training — Shown in Figure 17a, we employ an off-policy asynchronous RL setup (Noukhovitch et al., 2024) featuring a centralized learner distributed across multiple nodes via DeepSpeed (Rasley et al., 2020) and a large pool of actors, each running an independent vLLM (Kwon et al., 2023) instance. The learner produces prompts that are queued and dispatched to the actors, which execute the prompts, interact with the environment, and return results through a results queue that the learner uses to update the model parameters. Due to the variance in completion length, a long time delta can emerge between completions in an individual batch of RLVR. The guiding principles to mitigate this issue are to make efficient use of resources (avoiding idling) and to make processes asynchronous.

中文: 全异步训练——如图 17a 所示，我们采用离策略异步 RL 设置（Noukhovitch et al., 2024），其特点是：一个通过 DeepSpeed（Rasley et al., 2020）跨多个节点分布的集中式学习器，以及一个大型 actor 池，每个 actor 运行一个独立的 vLLM（Kwon et al., 2023）实例。学习器产生提示词，它们被排队并派发给 actor；actor 执行提示词、与环境交互，并通过结果队列返回结果，学习器用这些结果更新模型参数。由于补全长度的方差，单个 RLVR 批次中的补全之间可能出现较长的时间差。缓解这一问题的指导原则是高效利用资源（避免空闲）并使进程异步化。


Source: p.48 S150

Original: Continuous batching — We employ continuous batching to constantly enqueue new generations as each one finishes to remove the compute waste for long generations (see Figure 17). This is in contrast to static batching, in which a batch of prompts is split over N actors, and each actor generates the entire batch, returns the generated responses to the learner, and then receives a new batch of data. Static batching is inefficient, as when one generation finishes that “slot” of the batch will remain empty until we get a new batch. The exact wasted compute can be calculated as the maximum sequence length minus the average sequence length divided by the maximum sequence length. With Olmo 3, at a 32K generation length, we see a mean generation length of 14628 and a maximum of 32K, which means that up to 54% of our compute would have been wasted with static batching. See Figure 17 for an illustrated example.

中文: 连续批处理——我们采用连续批处理，在每个生成完成时不断将新生成入队，以消除长生成的算力浪费（见图 17）。这与静态批处理形成对比：静态批处理将一批提示词拆到 N 个 actor 上，每个 actor 生成整个批次，将生成的响应返回给学习器，然后接收新一批数据。静态批处理是低效的，因为当一个生成完成时，该批次的「槽位」会一直空着直到拿到新批次。精确的浪费算力可计算为「最大序列长度减去平均序列长度、再除以最大序列长度」。对 Olmo 3，在 32K 生成长度下，我们看到平均生成长度为 14628、最大为 32K，这意味着若用静态批处理，最多会有 54% 的算力被浪费。图 17 给出了图示示例。

Fig. 17. OlmoRL 基础设施概览

Placed near: p.48 S150
Source: p.49 C027

Original caption: Overview of OlmoRL infrastructure. On the left: distributed reinforcement learning architecture (Figure 17a). On the right: static vs. continuous batching. Static batching (Figure 17b) wastes compute when generations have variable sequence lengths. Pink cells are prefilled tokens, green cells are decoded tokens, with dark green representing EOS. Grey indicates that sequence is not doing anything, so continuous batching (Figure 17c) backfills finished rows immediately, resulting in no wasted compute.

中文图注: OlmoRL 基础设施概览。左：分布式强化学习架构（图 17a）。右：静态批处理 vs 连续批处理。当生成具有可变序列长度时，静态批处理（图 17b）会浪费算力。粉色单元格为预填充令牌，绿色单元格为解码令牌，深绿色代表 EOS。灰色表示该序列未做任何事，因此连续批处理（图 17c）会立即回填已完成的各行，从而不浪费算力。

Reading note: 该图是 OlmoRL 工程的核心示意图：学习器-actor 异步架构（左）与连续批处理（右）共同消除长生成带来的算力浪费。


Source: p.48 S151

Original: Active sampling — To compensate for filtered instances, our fully asynchronous framework enables continuously pulling completions from the actor and resampling prompts into the queue. We actively sample and filter until we reach our desired batch size of non-zero-gradient completions. Previously, Yu et al. (2025) dynamic sampling would oversample and generate three times the number of prompts used in each training batch. This was to reasonably guarantee that the batch had enough completions with non-zero standard deviation. In contrast, our active sampling more efficiently uses the infrastructure. As demonstrated in section 6, we find this significantly stabilizes training and prevents batch size from reducing over the course of training (a common issue with vanilla GRPO).

中文: 主动采样——为补偿被过滤的实例，我们的全异步框架支持持续从 actor 拉取补全并将提示词重新采样入队。我们主动采样并过滤，直到达到期望的非零梯度补全批量大小。此前，Yu et al. (2025) 的动态采样会过采样并生成每个训练批次所用提示词数量的三倍，以合理保证批次中有足够多标准差非零的补全。相比之下，我们的主动采样更高效地利用了基础设施。如第 6 节所示，我们发现这显著稳定了训练，并防止批量大小在训练过程中缩减（vanilla GRPO 的常见问题）。


Source: p.49 S152

Original: Inflight updates — A common goal of RL training for LLMs is to minimize the degree of difference between the actor policy and the learner policy, i.e., to minimize being off-policy (Van Hasselt et al., 2018). This can be achieved by synchronizing the weights after every training step as follows: each actor finishes all of their ongoing generations, dumps the KV cache, and updates its copy of the weights. However, this causes GPUs to be idle and hurts training efficiency. Instead, we follow Piché et al. (2025) to immediately update the weights without pausing the engine, relying on the generation framework to be thread-safe, and continue generating, without invalidating the KV cache. This enables a significant increase in throughput: up to 4x faster with the same resources, without hurting accuracy.

中文: 在途更新（Inflight updates）——LLM RL 训练的一个共同目标是尽量缩小 actor 策略与学习器策略之间的差异，即尽量减小离策略程度（Van Hasselt et al., 2018）。这可以通过在每一步训练后同步权重来实现：每个 actor 完成其所有进行中的生成，转储 KV 缓存，并更新其权重副本。然而，这会导致 GPU 空闲并损害训练效率。相反，我们遵循 Piché et al. (2025)，在不暂停引擎的情况下立即更新权重，依赖生成框架的线程安全性，并在不使 KV 缓存失效的情况下继续生成。这带来了显著的吞吐提升：在相同资源下最多快 4 倍，且不损害准确率。


Source: p.49 S153

Original: Better threading and engineering — These changes are primarily around handling the weight synchronization after each training step to make actors more efficient. Our new setup decouples the actors, allowing each one to start and stop by itself, without waiting for the rest of the actors to finish their syncs as well. Similarly, we make a large number of optimizations that were not machine learning specific, and were centered around efficiently using the CPU. For example, our initial implementation of continuous batching, for instance, was slower than static batching before adding a prefetch thread to our actors that constantly refilled the inference queue to see a throughput improvement. Our final RL run ended up mixing carefully-filtered data from all domains roughly equally and running on top of the DPO checkpoint.

中文: 更好的线程与工程——这些改动主要围绕在每一步训练后处理权重同步、使 actor 更高效。我们的新设置解耦了 actor，允许每个 actor 自行启动与停止，无需等待其余 actor 也完成同步。类似地，我们做了大量并非机器学习特定的优化，其核心是高效使用 CPU。例如，我们连续批处理的最初实现，在给 actor 添加一个持续回填推理队列的预取线程之前，其实比静态批处理还慢，加之后才看到吞吐提升。我们的最终 RL 运行最终以大致相等的比例混合了来自所有领域的、经过仔细过滤的数据，并运行在 DPO 检查点之上。

4.5 Key Findings

4.5 关键发现


Source: p.49 S154

Original: DPO yields gains where SFT on the same data cannot — Continued supervised finetuning directly on the chosen responses from Dolci Think DPO outright hurts the initial SFT model (Table 21), dropping all evaluation tasks. We conjecture that this is because the chosen responses (generated by Qwen3 32B Thinking) are weaker relative to data the model has already seen in Dolci Think SFT, and hence, they are no longer useful targets for imitation. However, by pairing these chosen responses with rejected responses generated by a weaker model, we construct a useful contrast, enabling preference tuning to drive strong gains beyond the initial SFT model (Table 21). Promisingly, these gains are not merely converting pass@k into pass@1 but rather expanding the reasoning frontier of the model (e.g., improved pass@k on AIME evaluations; Figure 20). These findings highlight contrastive learning with preference tuning as a useful stage for improving capabilities even when imitation is saturated.

中文: DPO 在相同数据上 SFT 无法取得收益的地方带来收益——直接在 Dolci Think DPO 的被选择响应上继续监督微调，反而直接损害了初始 SFT 模型（表 21），所有评测任务都下降。我们推测这是因为被选择响应（由 Qwen3 32B Thinking 生成）相对于模型在 Dolci Think SFT 中已见过的数据而言较弱，因而不再是有效的模仿目标。然而，通过将这些被选择响应与较弱模型生成的被拒绝响应配对，我们构造了有用的对比，使偏好调优能带来超越初始 SFT 模型的强劲收益（表 21）。令人鼓舞的是，这些收益并非仅仅把 pass@k 转换为 pass@1，而是扩展了模型的推理前沿（例如，AIME 评测上的 pass@k 改进；图 20）。这些发现凸显了偏好调优的对比学习，即便在模仿已饱和时，仍是改进能力的有用阶段。

Original: Table 21 — The delta between chosen and rejected responses is critical.

Source: p.50 S155

Original: DPO and SFT both benefit from RL, but DPO remains a better starting point — Table 22 shows that running our final RL mix on the DPO model consistently yields better performance than running it on the SFT model. We find three primary differences, highlighted in Figure 19: for evaluations that RL does not improve, the DPO model often performs better and maintains its advantage during RL training (e.g., AlpacaEval). For evaluations explicitly targeted by RL (e.g., Omega), both the DPO and SFT models achieve similar end performance. For evaluations targeted by RL but hard to improve further from DPO (e.g., AIME 2025), the SFT model improves to get close to DPO performance. In no situation does the SFT model improve over the DPO model after RL, and as such we opt to focus on applying RL over our DPO model.

中文: DPO 与 SFT 都能从 RL 中获益，但 DPO 仍是更好的起点——表 22 显示，在 DPO 模型上运行我们的最终 RL 混合，始终比在 SFT 模型上运行获得更好的性能。我们发现三个主要差异，如图 19 所强调：对于 RL 无法改进的评测，DPO 模型通常表现更好，并在 RL 训练期间保持其优势（例如 AlpacaEval）。对于 RL 明确针对的评测（例如 Omega），DPO 与 SFT 模型都达到相似的最终性能。对于 RL 针对但难以从 DPO 进一步改进的评测（例如 AIME 2025），SFT 模型改进后接近 DPO 的性能。没有任何情形下 SFT 模型在 RL 之后优于 DPO 模型，因此我们选择将 RL 应用于 DPO 模型。

Original: Table 22 — Delta learning provides a stronger initialization for subsequent RLVR than SFT alone.

中文表注: 表 22 —— 相比仅用 SFT，Delta 学习为后续 RLVR 提供了更强的初始化。

Source: p.50 S156

Original: Curiously, we find that the SFT model performs similarly when trained either with the data offline-filtered using the SFT or DPO model, suggesting that the additional samples filtered out (i.e., solved) by the DPO model do not provide additional signal for improving the SFT model. Further investigating this, we find that while the DPO model does display lower entropy, it in fact has higher pass@K performance on AIME evaluations, as shown in Figure 20. This suggests that the DPO model remains a strong starting point for RL relative to the SFT model, as prior work (Yue et al., 2025; Shao et al., 2024) suggests RLVR, under certain conditions, helps convert pass@K improvements into pass@1 gains.

中文: 令人好奇的是，我们发现无论用 SFT 还是 DPO 模型离线过滤的数据训练，SFT 模型表现相似，这表明 DPO 模型额外过滤掉（即解出的）样本并未为改进 SFT 模型提供额外信号。进一步调查后，我们发现虽然 DPO 模型确实显示更低的熵，但它实际上在 AIME 评测上具有更高的 pass@K 性能，如图 20 所示。这表明相对于 SFT 模型，DPO 模型仍是 RL 的强起点，因为先前工作（Yue et al., 2025; Shao et al., 2024）表明，RLVR 在特定条件下有助于将 pass@K 的改进转换为 pass@1 的收益。


Source: p.50 S157

Original: Rewards steadily increase across all domains during RL — Figure 18 plots per-verifier reward curves along with average output length. We find that reward steadily increases across all domains, albeit at differing rates (with instruction-following data increasing most steadily, and code reward increasing most slowly). We plot more RL curves in the appendix (Figure 41). Interestingly, we find that sequence lengths first slightly dip and then slowly increase over time. This is likely due to the reasoning SFT and DPO already training the model to produce long reasoning traces of up to the maximum response length of 32K tokens.

中文: 在 RL 期间，所有领域的奖励稳步上升——图 18 绘制了每个验证器的奖励曲线以及平均输出长度。我们发现所有领域的奖励都稳步上升，尽管速率不同（指令遵循数据上升最平稳，代码奖励上升最慢）。我们在附录（图 41）中绘制了更多 RL 曲线。有趣的是，我们发现序列长度先小幅下降、再随时间缓慢上升。这很可能是因为推理 SFT 与 DPO 已经训练模型生成长达 32K 令牌最大响应长度的长思维轨迹。

Source: p.51 S158

Original: Mixing RL data from varied domains can prevent over-optimization — Figure 20 (left) demonstrates that training on specific domains can lead to over-optimization, in which performance on evaluations outside that domain drops, while training on a mix yields steady improvements across different domains. For example, we observe a trade-off when performing OlmoRL on IFEval alone, wherein higher IFEval scores correlate with lower AlpacaEval scores. However, when we perform our final mixed training, we are able to maintain high AlpacaEval scores without compromising IFEval performance, as the LM-judge reward ensures that the model continues to produce well-formed chat responses. Mixing data yields lower train reward, but not lower downstream performance — While Figure 20 demonstrates that our final mixture run achieves downstream performance similar to or greater than RL training runs on single domains, we find that we observe lower train reward across each domain when training on mixed data as opposed to single-domain data, as seen in Figure 21. This suggests that mixing data may in fact reduce the model’s tendency to over-optimize during training, preventing some degree of reward-hacking and thus generalizing better to downstream evaluations. This may explain why RL training on broader data mixtures can outperform domain-specific mixtures (Cheng et al., 2025).

中文: 混合来自多领域的 RL 数据可以防止过度优化——图 20（左）表明，在特定领域上训练可能导致过度优化，即该领域之外的评测性能下降；而在混合上训练则在各领域产生稳步改进。例如，我们观察到仅在 IFEval 上执行 OlmoRL 时存在权衡：更高的 IFEval 分数与更低的 AlpacaEval 分数相关。然而，当我们执行最终混合训练时，我们能够在不牺牲 IFEval 性能的情况下维持高 AlpacaEval 分数，因为 LM 评判奖励确保模型继续产出格式良好的对话响应。混合数据带来更低的训练奖励，但下游性能并不更低——虽然图 20 表明我们的最终混合运行取得了与单领域 RL 训练运行相似或更好的下游性能，但我们发现，与单领域数据相比，在混合数据上训练时每个领域的训练奖励更低，如图 21 所示。这表明混合数据实际上可能降低模型在训练期间过度优化的倾向，在一定程度上防止奖励黑客行为，从而更好地泛化到下游评测。这或许能解释为何在更广泛的数据混合上做 RL 训练能优于领域专属混合（Cheng et al., 2025）。

Fig. 19. 用 DPO 作为 RLVR 起点效果最佳

The importance of starting from DPO or SFT depends on the evaluation, but starting from DPO is overall preferable.

Source: p.51 S159

Original: Continuous batching and inflight updates are crucial to training speed — Using a reasoner SFT or DPO as a starting point stresses RL training to its limits, as the model starts with extremely long average generation lengths. Table 23 demonstrates how using continuous batching and inflight updates is crucial to training speed, allowing us to achieve two times faster training on half as many GPUs, making experimentation and long RL runs more tractable. To carefully benchmark this, we ablate the changes to our RL infrastructure between OLMo 2 and Olmo 3. See Table 23. For each ablation, we ran a benchmark experiment for 2 hours using 2 8x A100 nodes. One node was used for training, and one for inference. Since inference is our bottleneck, we report Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU) and Model Bandwidth Utilization (MBU) based solely on the single node used for inference.

中文: 连续批处理与在途更新对训练速度至关重要——用推理 SFT 或 DPO 作为起点会把 RL 训练压到极限，因为模型一开始就具有极长的平均生成长度。表 23 展示了连续批处理与在途更新对训练速度的关键作用，使我们能在少一半的 GPU 上实现两倍的训练速度，使实验与长 RL 运行更易开展。为仔细基准测试这一点，我们消融了 OLMo 2 与 Olmo 3 之间 RL 基础设施的改动。见表 23。对每个消融，我们使用 2 个 8x A100 节点运行 2 小时的基准实验。一个节点用于训练，一个用于推理。由于推理是我们的瓶颈，我们仅基于用于推理的单个节点报告模型 FLOPs 利用率（MFU）与模型带宽利用率（MBU）。

5. Olmo 3 Instruct


Source: p.53 S161

Original: Recent studies suggest that real-world language model use predominantly centers on general tasks such as advice-seeking and information recall (Chatterji et al., 2025) that may not require extensive reasoning. Everyday chat settings often do not require the inference-time scaling of Olmo 3 Think. Hence, we develop Olmo 3 Instruct, a non-reasoning model designed with these real use cases in mind. Olmo 3 Instruct quickly and helpfully responds to common user queries. This different model type demands different data to support it. We focus on improving the interactivity of the models by introducing multi-turn DPO data and promoting concise responses in our delta-learning preference-tuning pipeline. Additionally, Olmo 3 Instruct is trained for function-calling, for which we release new SFT datasets. Together, our recipe yields Olmo 3 Instruct models that effectively leverage tools and efficiently respond to user queries.

中文: 近期研究表明，现实世界的语言模型使用主要集中于咨询建议与信息回忆等通用任务（Chatterji et al., 2025），这些任务可能不需要大量推理。日常对话场景往往不需要 Olmo 3 Think 那样的推理时扩展。因此，我们开发了 Olmo 3 Instruct，一个考虑到这些真实用例的非推理模型。Olmo 3 Instruct 能快速、有帮助地响应常见用户查询。这一不同的模型类型需要不同的数据来支撑。我们通过引入多轮 DPO 数据、并在 delta 学习偏好调优流水线中鼓励简洁响应，来改进模型的交互性。此外，Olmo 3 Instruct 面向函数调用进行训练，为此我们发布了新的 SFT 数据集。综合起来，我们的配方产出的 Olmo 3 Instruct 模型能有效利用工具、高效响应用户查询。
