5.2 Supervised Finetuning with Dolci Instruct SFT

5.2 Dolci Instruct SFT 监督微调


Source: p.54 S163

Original: We construct Dolci Instruct SFT by building upon our OLMo 2 Instruct mixture, making significant improvements to advance general chat, reasoning, and function-calling capabilities.

中文: 我们在 OLMo 2 Instruct 混合的基础上构建 Dolci Instruct SFT，做了显著改进，以提升通用对话、推理与函数调用能力。

5.2.1 Function-calling Training Data

5.2.1 函数调用训练数据


Source: p.54 S164

Original: Our goals for curating tool-use training data for Olmo 3 Instruct are to provide the model a strong foundation in basic function calling and to expose the model to trajectories demonstrating the effective use of real environments (i.e., MCP servers) to perform tasks. Accordingly, we collect two kinds of trajectories synthesized using LLMs, described below. Trajectories with real interactions — We collect trajectories demonstrating agents’ use of MCP servers to answer queries. All trajectories have a single user turn and multiple agent–environment interactions. We focus on the following domains: Science QA dataset contains two broad classes of queries requiring retrieval and reasoning over scholarly content: 1) paper content-based queries, which focus on information present in the abstract or full text of papers and 2) citation graph-based queries, which are about metadata such as authors, venues, and citations. Trajectories associated with the queries are obtained using an agent based on GPT-4.1-mini equipped with the ASTA Scientific Corpus (ASC) MCP server, which provides structured access to metadata and paper content on Semantic Scholar. Additional details about these datasets are provided in Appendix A.7.2.

中文: 我们为 Olmo 3 Instruct 整理工具使用训练数据的目标是：为模型提供扎实的基础函数调用能力，并让模型接触到展示如何有效使用真实环境（即 MCP 服务器）来执行任务的轨迹。因此，我们收集两类由 LLM 合成的轨迹，描述如下。带真实交互的轨迹——我们收集展示 agent 使用 MCP 服务器回答查询的轨迹。所有轨迹都有一个用户轮次与多次 agent–环境交互。我们聚焦以下领域：Science QA 数据集包含两大类需要对学术内容进行检索与推理的查询：1）基于论文内容的查询，聚焦论文摘要或全文中的信息；2）基于引文图的查询，涉及作者、发表场所与引用等元数据。与查询关联的轨迹通过一个基于 GPT-4.1-mini 的 agent 获得，该 agent 配备 ASTA Scientific Corpus（ASC）MCP 服务器，可对 Semantic Scholar 上的元数据与论文内容进行结构化访问。这些数据集的更多细节见附录 A.7.2。


Source: p.55 S165

Original: Web search QA dataset is adapted from DR Tulu (Shao et al., 2025a). It consists of a multi-stage pipeline that combines benchmark-derived and real-world queries. Queries are drawn from open-access benchmarks: HotpotQA (Yang et al., 2018), TaskCraft (Shi et al., 2025), and WebWalkerQA (silver) (Wu et al., 2025a), as well as from consented, publicly released user prompts from SearchArena (Miroyan et al., 2025) and OpenScholar (Asai et al., 2024). We filter the set of queries using GPT-5 to keep only those that both require search and have long-form, verifiable responses. The trajectories for these queries are obtained from a GPT-5 agent equipped with the Serper API, which provides access to a Google search tool and a tool for fetching webpages given their URLs. Additional details about query filtering and trajectory generation can be found in Appendix A.7.2.

中文: Web search QA 数据集改编自 DR Tulu（Shao et al., 2025a）。它由一个多阶段流水线组成，结合了源自基准的查询与现实世界查询。查询取自开放访问基准：HotpotQA（Yang et al., 2018）、TaskCraft（Shi et al., 2025）和 WebWalkerQA (silver)（Wu et al., 2025a），以及来自 SearchArena（Miroyan et al., 2025）和 OpenScholar（Asai et al., 2024）的、经同意公开的用户提示词。我们使用 GPT-5 过滤查询集合，只保留既需要搜索、又具有长形式可验证响应的查询。这些查询的轨迹来自一个配备 Serper API 的 GPT-5 agent，Serper API 提供 Google 搜索工具及按 URL 抓取网页的工具。查询过滤与轨迹生成的更多细节见附录 A.7.2。


Source: p.55 S166

Original: Trajectories with simulated interactions — While training on trajectories with executable environments is expected to teach the model to effectively deal with real environment outputs and handle unexpected errors, it is difficult to curate such trajectories at scale, thus potentially limiting the model’s generalization to unseen tools at inference time. To fill this gap, we also create a dataset of synthetic trajectories with LLM-simulated environments which are much easier to scale. We call this dataset SimFC. We start with a large pool of tool sets or APIs from existing datasets (e.g., xLAM (Liu et al., 2024c), ToolACE (Liu et al., 2024b)), and from publicly available MCP servers, and prompted LLMs (GPT-4o, GPT-4.1, and GPT-5) to generate entire trajectories including simulated user queries, environment responses, and assistant messages. We design prompts to ensure the dataset contains a variety of interaction patterns including multi-turn, multi-step, and refusals due to inadequate information or tools. Additional details about this dataset and illustrative prompts used for generation can be found in Appendix A.7.2, Figure 42, and Figure 43.

中文: 带模拟交互的轨迹——虽然在带可执行环境的轨迹上训练，预期能教会模型有效处理真实环境输出并应对意外错误，但大规模整理此类轨迹很困难，从而可能限制模型在推理时对未见工具的泛化。为填补这一空白，我们还创建了一个使用 LLM 模拟环境的合成轨迹数据集，它更易扩展。我们将该数据集称为 SimFC。我们从现有数据集（例如 xLAM（Liu et al., 2024c）、ToolACE（Liu et al., 2024b））和公开可用的 MCP 服务器中的大批工具集或 API 出发，并提示 LLM（GPT-4o、GPT-4.1 和 GPT-5）生成完整轨迹，包括模拟的用户查询、环境响应与助手消息。我们设计提示词以确保数据集包含多种交互模式，包括多轮、多步、以及因信息或工具不足而拒绝。该数据集的更多细节及用于生成的示意提示词见附录 A.7.2、图 42 与图 43。


Source: p.56 S167

Original: Balancing function diversity with interaction complexity — As illustrated by the statistics in Table 27, the two types of trajectories have key differences. SimFC has a large number of trajectories with diverse sets of functions. We find that synthesizing trajectories with multiple user turns (multi-turn trajectories) is relatively easier than those with multiple assistant-environment interactions per user request (multi-step trajectories). However, the latter class usually corresponds to more complex tasks. On the other hand, the datasets with real interactions, while smaller in size, are naturally more complex in terms of multi-step interactions.

中文: 平衡函数多样性与交互复杂度——如表 27 的统计所示，两类轨迹有关键差异。SimFC 拥有大量轨迹与多样的函数集。我们发现，合成多个用户轮次的轨迹（多轮轨迹）相对比每个用户请求含多个助手-环境交互的轨迹（多步轨迹）更容易。然而，后者通常对应更复杂的任务。另一方面，带真实交互的数据集虽然规模较小，但在多步交互方面天然更复杂。

Original: Table 27 — Details of function calling datasets. Multi-turn refers to multiple user turns per trajectory and multi-step refers to multiple environment interactions per user request.

中文表注: 表 27 —— 函数调用数据集的细节。多轮指每个轨迹多个用户轮次，多步指每个用户请求多次环境交互。

Source: p.56 S168

Original: Unified data format — Across all tool-use data, we adopt consistent tool definition and tool-calling formats. We find that unifying format to be crucial for stable and high-quality tool-use behavior. Particularly, we use the OpenAPI specification for all tool definitions and represent all function calls as pythonic code blocks. We provide tool specifications in the system prompt, encapsulate tool calls with XML tags within the assistant role, and present environment outputs to the model within a special environment role. We also extend the tokenizer’s vocabulary with dedicated special tokens corresponding to these tags. Unlike Olmo 3 Think, preliminary suggest this approach to be more effective for tool-use training than encoding , , <function_calls>, and </function_calls> as regular text.

中文: 统一数据格式——在所有工具使用数据中，我们采用一致的工具定义与工具调用格式。我们发现统一格式对稳定、高质量的工具使用行为至关重要。特别是，我们对所有工具定义使用 OpenAPI 规范，并将所有函数调用表示为 Python 风格代码块。我们在系统提示词中提供工具规范，在助手角色内用 XML 标签封装工具调用，并在一个特殊的环境角色内向模型呈现环境输出。我们还扩展了分词器的词表，加入与这些标签对应的专用特殊令牌。与 Olmo 3 Think 不同，初步表明该方法比将 、、<function_calls> 和 </function_calls> 编码为普通文本对工具使用训练更有效。


Source: p.56 S169

Original: Evaluating function calling — We evaluate the function calling capabilities of Olmo 3 Instruct in terms of intrinsic function calling and extrinsic task completion accuracies using different benchmarks. We use the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard (BFCLv3) (Patil et al., 2025) to evaluate intrinsic function calling accuracy. This benchmark focuses on models’ ability to choose the relevant functions and the right values for their arguments to accomplish a given task in settings that require one or more interactions with simulated users and environments. We evaluate task completion accuracy of Olmo 3 Instruct in comparison with similar models when they are deployed as agents with access to tools served via Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers. Particularly, we use the Asta Scientific Corpus (ASC) tool (Bragg et al., 2025) that serves eight functions for accessing scientific literature, and the Serper API which provides Google search tool and web browsing functionalities.

中文: 函数调用评测——我们使用不同基准，从内在函数调用准确率与外任务完成准确率两方面评测 Olmo 3 Instruct 的函数调用能力。我们使用 Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard（BFCLv3）（Patil et al., 2025）评测内在函数调用准确率。该基准聚焦于模型在需要与模拟用户和环境进行一次或多次交互的设置下，选择相关函数与正确参数值以完成给定任务的能力。我们将 Olmo 3 Instruct 与规模相近模型部署为可通过 Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器访问工具的 agent，比较其任务完成准确率。具体而言，我们使用 Asta Scientific Corpus（ASC）工具（Bragg et al., 2025），它提供访问科学文献的八个函数，以及提供 Google 搜索工具与网页浏览功能的 Serper API。


Source: p.56 S170

Original: To evaluate models’ usage of the ASC tools, following Bragg et al. (2025), we use a subset of 75 questions from LitQA2 (Skarlinski et al., 2024) for which the associated papers can be found in ASC’s index. We evaluate the models’ usage of search and browsing tools using a subset of SimpleQA (Wei et al., 2024). We use the official Gorilla repository for BFCLv3 evaluations. For LitQA2 and SimpleQA, we implement a basic function-calling agent using OpenAI’s Agent SDK. This agent uses the tools provided by the relevant MCP server, and interacts with the environment by iteratively making function calls and processing the outputs of executing them to solve the given tasks. For LitQA2 and SimpleQA, we also measure model performance when deployed in a No-Tools setting, in which we provide no tools to the agents and they are expected to solve the tasks entirely from the models’ parametric knowledge. We use a zero-shot evaluation for all these benchmarks. We sample from models at temperature 0 and, for LitQA2 and SimpleQA, allow the agents at most 10 turns to finish each task. We run each evaluation three times and report the average accuracy. We release our code for running our MCP-based tool-use evaluations.

中文: 为评测模型对 ASC 工具的使用，遵循 Bragg et al. (2025)，我们使用 LitQA2（Skarlinski et al., 2024）中、其关联论文可在 ASC 索引中找到的 75 个问题的子集。我们使用 SimpleQA（Wei et al., 2024）的一个子集评测模型对搜索与浏览工具的使用。我们使用官方 Gorilla 仓库做 BFCLv3 评测。对于 LitQA2 和 SimpleQA，我们使用 OpenAI 的 Agent SDK 实现一个基础函数调用 agent。该 agent 使用相关 MCP 服务器提供的工具，通过迭代地发起函数调用并处理执行输出来解决给定任务。对于 LitQA2 和 SimpleQA，我们还测量模型在 No-Tools 设置下的表现，即不向 agent 提供任何工具、期望其完全依靠模型的参数化知识解决任务。我们对所有这些基准使用零样本评测。我们以温度 0 采样模型，对于 LitQA2 和 SimpleQA，允许 agent 每个任务最多 10 轮完成。每个评测运行三次并报告平均准确率。我们发布了运行基于 MCP 工具使用评测的代码。

5.2.2 Curating Dolci Instruct SFT

5.2.2 整理 Dolci Instruct SFT


Source: p.57 S171

Original: Step 1. Sourcing Prompts and Completions — Our prompt collection includes all our new function-calling data (Section §5.2.1), new prompts for instruction following (see Section §4.2.1) and science, and more chat prompts from WildChat (Zhao et al., 2024a). For examples that originally contained reasoning traces (such as the OpenThoughts3 science subset described in Section §4.2.1), we remove the reasoning traces and special tokens. We also update completions from older models such as GPT-3.5 and GPT-4 with completions from GPT-4.1. We show a summary of our instruct SFT mix in Table 30. Step 2: Filtering & Mixing — We follow the same filtering and mixing procedure detailed in Section 4.2.1. 5.3 Preference Tuning with Dolci Instruct DPO

5.3 Dolci Instruct DPO 偏好微调


Source: p.57 S173

Original: We create Dolci Instruct DPO by extending the strong base of our delta-learning heuristic preferences (Section §4.3) with further curated preference signals to enhance our model’s behavior in general use settings. We enrich our heuristic data with contrastive pairs from an improved GPT-judge pipeline for general alignment. Additionally, user interaction with LMs commonly requires multi-turn conversational capabilities, so we introduce synthetic multi-turn conversations to our preference data. We also observe that preference-data pipelines often promote overly verbose responses; we introduce counteracting interventions to promote brevity in model responses by mitigating length bias in the preference data.

中文: 我们通过在 delta 学习启发式偏好（第 §4.3 节）的坚实基础之上扩展更多精心整理的偏好信号，来创建 Dolci Instruct DPO，以增强模型在通用使用场景下的行为。我们用来自改进 GPT 评判流水线的对比对来丰富启发式数据，用于通用对齐。此外，用户与 LM 的交互通常需要多轮对话能力，因此我们在偏好数据中引入合成多轮对话。我们还观察到偏好数据流水线常常鼓励过度冗长的响应；我们引入反向干预，通过缓解偏好数据中的长度偏差来鼓励模型响应的简洁性。

5.3.1 Preference Signals

5.3.1 偏好信号


Source: p.58 S174

Original: Dolci Instruct DPO is constructed from a composite of several preference signals to promote model capabilities and general usability: Delta-learning heuristic pairs — Similar to Dolci Think DPO, we construct heuristic contrastive pairs by generating chosen responses with a large model (Qwen3 32B) and rejected responses with a small model (Qwen3 0.6B) following Geng et al. (2025). Note that we turn off thinking mode, as we do not need internal thinking traces. Delta-aware GPT-judged pairs — We additionally generate GPT-judged preference pairs to add a further source of preference signal. Our initial attempts to modernize the UltraFeedback pipeline from OLMo 2 and Tülu 3 by improving the quality of the LLM judge (GPT-4o → GPT-4.1) and updating our data-generator model pool do not yield gains and even hurt model performance relative to the OLMo 2 preference dataset baseline. We speculate that this failure is due to the fact that the majority of our data generators are high-quality, very capable models; hence on average there was minimal meaningful contrast between the resulting chosen and rejected pairs. To mitigate this, we explicitly introduce delta-aware interventions designed to lower the quality of the rejected response. We 1) ensure that responses from weaker models are always present in the response set judged for each prompt, and 2) select the worst response as the rejected completion to maximize the resulting delta. We find these “delta-maximizing” interventions to be critical for the quality of preference pair data.

中文: Dolci Instruct DPO 由多种偏好信号的组合构成，以促进模型能力与通用可用性：Delta 学习启发式配对——与 Dolci Think DPO 类似，我们通过用大模型（Qwen3 32B）生成被选择响应、用小模型（Qwen3 0.6B）生成被拒绝响应来构造启发式对比对，遵循 Geng et al. (2025)。注意我们关闭了思考模式，因为不需要内部思维轨迹。Delta 感知的 GPT 评判配对——我们还生成 GPT 评判的偏好对，以增加另一个偏好信号来源。我们最初尝试通过改进 LLM 评判器质量（GPT-4o → GPT-4.1）并更新数据生成器模型池来现代化 OLMo 2 和 Tülu 3 的 UltraFeedback 流水线，但相对于 OLMo 2 偏好数据集基线并无收益、甚至损害了模型性能。我们推测这一失败是因为我们的大多数数据生成器都是高质量、能力很强的模型；因此平均而言，所得被选择与被拒绝配对之间几乎没有有意义的对比。为缓解这一点，我们显式引入旨在降低被拒绝响应质量的 delta 感知干预。我们 1）确保较弱模型的响应始终出现在每个提示词被评判的响应集合中，2）选择最差响应作为被拒绝补全，以最大化所得差距。我们发现这些「delta 最大化」干预对偏好对数据的质量至关重要。


Source: p.59 S175

Original: Multi-turn preferences — To ensure Olmo 3’s usability in realistic multi-turn conversations, we further add a multi-turn preference dataset with prompts synthetically extended from the Tülu 3-DPO dataset. Preference pairs differed in only the last turn of the conversation to avoid ambiguity in quality ranking between turns of the same conversation. Synthetic conversations are generated with two methods: 1) self-talk extending the original prompt into a multi-turn conversation with LLM-generated follow-up requests and 2) synthetic-context created by generating related, independent questions or paraphrases of the initial prompt to use as previous user turns with associated completions. The combination of these generation methods ensures diversity in generated conversations. Final turns are generated with the delta-learning heuristic (Geng et al., 2025); chosen/rejected completion pairs are generated by either GPT-4o and GPT-3.5 or Qwen 3 32B and Qwen 3 0.6B (both no-thinking) respectively.

中文: 多轮偏好——为确保 Olmo 3 在真实多轮对话中的可用性，我们进一步加入一个多轮偏好数据集，其提示词从 Tülu 3-DPO 数据集合成扩展而来。偏好对仅在对话的最后一轮不同，以避免同一对话不同轮次之间质量排序的歧义。合成对话用两种方法生成：1）self-talk，将原始提示词扩展为带 LLM 生成后续请求的多轮对话；2）synthetic-context，通过生成相关、独立的问题或初始提示词的改写，作为带关联补全的前序用户轮次。这些生成方法的组合确保了生成对话的多样性。最终轮用 delta 学习启发式（Geng et al., 2025）生成；被选择/被拒绝补全对分别由 GPT-4o 与 GPT-3.5、或 Qwen 3 32B 与 Qwen 3 0.6B（均为无思考）生成。


Source: p.59 S176

Original: Controlling length bias — Preference data often has a length bias: the chosen responses are significantly longer than the rejected responses. This comes from sourcing synthetic response pairs where historically more information has been treated as more helpful by both LLM judges and preference heuristics. Namely, LLM judges such as the GPT judge in our pipeline tend to prefer longer responses. Similarly, we empirically observe that preference pairs made with the delta-learning heuristic also exhibit length bias; larger models generate longer responses (Figure 23). Thus, models often learn this length bias in addition to the intended useful quality signal during preference tuning, after which its generation length per prompt increases significantly. While this increased length is empirically useful for reasoning tasks, excessive verbosity can be undesirable for common real-use settings (see an example in Figure 22). We seek to strike a balance by filtering the chat and multi-turn subsets of our preference data to limit the length difference between the chosen and rejected responses to 100 tokens.

中文: 控制长度偏差——偏好数据常有长度偏差：被选择响应显著长于被拒绝响应。这源于合成响应对的来源，其中历史上更多信息被 LLM 评判器与偏好启发式都视为更有帮助。即，我们流水线中的 LLM 评判器（如 GPT 评判器）倾向于偏好更长的响应。类似地，我们经验观察到用 delta 学习启发式生成的偏好对也表现出长度偏差；更大的模型生成更长的响应（图 23）。因此，模型在偏好调优期间除了学到预期的有用质量信号之外，往往还会学到这种长度偏差，之后每个提示词的生成长度会显著增加。虽然这种长度增加对推理任务经验上有用，但过度冗长在常见真实使用场景中可能不受欢迎（示例见图 22）。我们寻求通过过滤偏好数据的对话与多轮子集、将被选择与被拒绝响应之间的长度差限制在 100 令牌以内来达成平衡。

5.3.2 Prompt Mixing

5.3.2 提示词混合


Source: p.59 S177

Original: Our prompt pool for GPT-judged and delta-learning heuristic pairs (see Table 30) is derived from the Dolci Instruct SFT dataset supplemented with the DaringAnteater and UltraFeedback subsets from the OLMo 2 7B preference dataset. Because DPO performance does not monotonically increase with more data (see Figure 23), we optimize the prompt distribution as a ratio within a set data budget and treat dataset size as a hyperparameter when training. To determine our final preference-tuning prompt distribution, we begin with near-uniform random sampling of 100K examples as an empirically strong baseline prompt mix. We then perform ablations of prompt-domain subsets to determine the impact of preference pairs from each domain subset. Additionally, we perform experiments that pair 50K samples of our base mix with 50K samples from a given domain, allowing us to understand the effects of upsampling each prompt domain.

中文: 我们用于 GPT 评判与 delta 学习启发式配对的提示词池（见表 30）来自 Dolci Instruct SFT 数据集，并补充以 OLMo 2 7B 偏好数据集中的 DaringAnteater 和 UltraFeedback 子集。由于 DPO 性能并不随数据量增加而单调上升（见图 23），我们在设定数据预算内将提示词分布作为比例来优化，并在训练时将数据集大小视为超参数。为确定最终偏好调优提示词分布，我们从 100K 示例的近均匀随机采样出发，作为经验上很强的基线提示词混合。随后我们对提示词域子集做消融，以确定每个域子集偏好对的影响。此外，我们还做实验，将基础混合的 50K 样本与给定域的 50K 样本配对，以理解上调每个提示词域的效果。


Source: p.59 S178

Original: Notably, prompt-domain distributions do not consistently align with the contrast exhibited in the response pair and thus in improvements in the corresponding downstream evaluation domains. For example, upsampling code prompts led to the counter-intuitive effect of decreasing code benchmark performance (see Table 51 in the Appendix). For determining our final mix, we create nine candidate mixes based on expert intuition gained from our ablations, comparing these hand-crafted mixes against the uniform sampling baseline. Our final mix is determined empirically; we find that our hand-crafted mixes outperformed random sampling.

中文: 值得注意的是，提示词域分布并不总是与响应对中所展示的对比、以及相应下游评测领域的改进保持一致。例如，上调代码提示词导致了降低代码基准性能的反直觉效果（见附录表 51）。为确定最终混合，我们基于从消融中获得的专家直觉创建了九个候选混合，将这些手工混合与均匀采样基线比较。我们的最终混合是经验确定的；我们发现手工混合优于随机采样。

5.3.3 Training

5.3.3 训练


Source: p.60 S179

Original: We follow the same training setup as Olmo 3 Think and sweep the same hyperparameters, namely learning rate and dataset size. We further sweep different length-control interventions by creating datasets with differing token cutoffs for length filtering. We select the best-performing checkpoint of each length budget and then select the final Olmo 3 Instruct-DPO checkpoint based on qualitative vibe tests and performance-vs-length analysis.

中文: 我们遵循与 Olmo 3 Think 相同的训练设置，并扫描相同的超参数，即学习率与数据集大小。我们还通过创建具有不同令牌截断值的数据集来做长度过滤，扫描不同的长度控制干预。我们为每个长度预算选择表现最佳的检查点，然后基于定性 vibe 测试与「性能-长度」分析选择最终的 Olmo 3 Instruct-DPO 检查点。

5.4 Reinforcement Learning with Dolci Instruct-RL

5.4 Dolci Instruct-RL 强化学习


Source: p.60 S180

Original: For our RL training stage, we modify the pool of prompts from Dolci Think RL (Section §4.4.2) by 1) utilizing less challenging datasets in the math and code domains, and 2) skipping the offline difficulty filtering, as our instruct model focuses more on general instruction following rather than complex reasoning.

中文: 对于 RL 训练阶段，我们修改 Dolci Think RL（第 §4.4.2 节）的提示词池：1）在数学与代码领域使用难度较低的数据集；2）跳过离线难度过滤，因为我们的指令模型更侧重于通用指令遵循而非复杂推理。


Source: p.60 S181

Original: Training — Following our Olmo 3 Think recipe, we train Olmo 3 Instruct on a mixture of general chat, math, and code data. We likewise employ OlmoRL for training, with a maximum response length of 8K tokens for 7B and 16K for 32B. Since our goal for Olmo 3 Instruct is to avoid generating excessively long outputs and preserve general usability, we apply RL on top of two DPO candidates: one that achieved the best average performance, and another with slightly lower performance but better qualitative “vibe test.” We then choose the final RL checkpoint based on final average performance, length analysis, and vibe test. Concretely, we begin by ranking checkpoints by average score; in the case of ties, we place more emphasis on datasets that do not scale with test-time compute (e.g., MATH and AIME performance increase with response length) to avoid biasing our selection towards models with overly long responses. Finally, we apply the vibe test to identify regressions or undesirable behaviors that may fall outside the scope of our evaluation suite.

中文: 训练——遵循我们的 Olmo 3 Think 配方，我们在通用对话、数学与代码数据的混合上训练 Olmo 3 Instruct。我们同样采用 OlmoRL 训练，7B 的最大响应长度为 8K 令牌、32B 为 16K。由于 Olmo 3 Instruct 的目标是避免生成过长的输出并保持通用可用性，我们在两个 DPO 候选之上应用 RL：一个取得了最佳平均性能，另一个性能略低但定性「vibe test」更好。随后我们基于最终平均性能、长度分析与 vibe test 选择最终 RL 检查点。具体而言，我们首先按平均分数给检查点排名；在并列时，我们对不随测试时算力扩展的数据集（例如 MATH 和 AIME 性能随响应长度增长）给予更多权重，以避免将选择偏向响应过长的模型。最后，我们应用 vibe test 识别可能超出评测套件范围的退化或不良行为。

5.5 Key Findings

5.5 关键发现


Source: p.61 S182

Original: Starting from the Olmo 3 Think SFT is helpful — We find that training Olmo 3 Instruct on top of the Olmo 3 Think SFT both increases model performance on benchmarks, as shown in Table 29. Importantly, average model response length is minimally affected by this strategy: Olmo 3 Instruct SFT checkpoints produce succinct answers with no remnants of thinking traces.

中文: 从 Olmo 3 Think SFT 出发是有帮助的——我们发现，在 Olmo 3 Think SFT 之上训练 Olmo 3 Instruct 能提升模型在基准上的性能，如表 29 所示。重要的是，这一策略对模型平均响应长度的影响极小：Olmo 3 Instruct SFT 检查点产生简洁的答案，无任何思维轨迹残留。


Source: p.61 S183

Original: High contrast in preference pairs drives DPO improvements — We observe that a high contrast between completions is critical for achieving improvements during DPO training (Table 32). Using LLM-judge pipelines requires carefully thinking about maximizing the delta between chosen and rejected responses. Our initial attempts to modernize the OLMo 2 preference data pipeline by improving the models used to generate responses failed to yield any improvements beyond the OLMo 2 data baseline (Table 32). This is likely because the models used for synthetic completions were universally too good: the chosen and rejected responses no longer had meaningful contrast. Extending prior findings that high contrast pairs are critical for performance (Geng et al., 2025; D’Oosterlinck et al., 2025), we introduce interventions to explicitly lower the quality of the rejected response and therefore increase the magnitude of the quality delta in the preference pair. These resulting delta-aware GPT pairs significantly outperform the OLMo 2 preference data.

中文: 偏好对中的高对比驱动 DPO 改进——我们观察到补全之间的高对比对在 DPO 训练中取得改进至关重要（表 32）。使用 LLM 评判流水线需要仔细考虑如何最大化被选择与被拒绝响应之间的差距。我们最初尝试通过改进用于生成响应的模型来现代化 OLMo 2 偏好数据流水线，但未能带来超越 OLMo 2 数据基线的任何改进（表 32）。这可能是因为用于合成补全的模型普遍太好了：被选择与被拒绝响应之间不再有有意义的对比。扩展先前关于高对比配对对性能至关重要的发现（Geng et al., 2025; D’Oosterlinck et al., 2025），我们引入干预以显式降低被拒绝响应的质量，从而增大偏好对中质量差距的幅度。由此得到的 delta 感知 GPT 配对显著优于 OLMo 2 偏好数据。


Source: p.61 S184

Original: Combining different preference signals improves overall performance — We combine delta-learning heuristic data with GPT-judged preference pairs to get the “best of both worlds.” Empirically, tuning with either delta-learning or GPT-judged pairs yields a different spread of gains; we find that these gains are complementary. Combining both sources of preference signal outperforms using either alone (Table 32).

中文: 组合不同偏好信号可改进整体性能——我们将 delta 学习启发式数据与 GPT 评判偏好对相结合，以「两全其美」。经验上，仅用 delta 学习或仅用 GPT 评判对调优会产生不同的收益分布；我们发现这些收益是互补的。组合两种偏好信号来源优于单独使用任一种（表 32）。


Source: p.61 S185

Original: The ideal amount of preference data depends on the downstream task — Preference-tuned model performance peaks with different amounts of training for different downstream task domains. We plot preference-tuning performance for example tasks across varying amounts of delta-learning heuristic pairs in Figure 23. Further optimization beyond these optimal points hurts downstream performance, consistent with theoretical results showing that early stopping is important for preference tuning (Azar et al., 2023; Geng et al., 2025). Practically, this informs our training approach: we sweep learning rate and dataset size to control the amount of total optimization, and pick the best-performing setting via our development evaluation set.

中文: 偏好数据的最佳量取决于下游任务——偏好调优模型的性能在不同下游任务域上，随训练量不同而在不同点达到峰值。图 23 绘制了示例任务在 delta 学习启发式对数量变化下的偏好调优性能。在这些最优点之后进一步优化会损害下游性能，与显示早停对偏好调优很重要的理论结果一致（Azar et al., 2023; Geng et al., 2025）。实际上，这指导了我们的训练方法：我们扫描学习率与数据集大小以控制总优化量，并通过开发评测集选择表现最佳的设置。

Fig. 22. 长度控制促进简洁、可用的响应

Placed near: p.60 S185
Source: p.60 C033

Original caption: Length control promotes concise, usable responses. On the left is a response from a development model preference-tuned without length control; on the right, a response to the same prompt from Olmo 3 Instruct-DPO (with length control). Promoting brevity in model responses makes the response easier to read and understand.

中文图注: 长度控制促进简洁、可用的响应。左侧是未做长度控制的开发模型经偏好调优后的响应；右侧是 Olmo 3 Instruct-DPO（有长度控制）对同一提示词的响应。鼓励模型响应简洁使响应更易读、易懂。

Reading note: 该图对比同一提示词下冗长 vs 简洁的响应，直观说明长度控制带来的可用性改进。


Source: p.61 S186

Original: Concise, usable model outputs from preference tuning can boost RL performance — Applying length control during DPO substantially reduces the model’s average generation length, allowing us to trade off some performance for improved conciseness and overall usability. While this reduction in length comes with lower scores on length-sensitive evaluations—particularly math benchmarks such as AIME and MATH—our internal qualitative assessments (“vibe tests”) almost uniformly preferred the shorter, more direct model. We make a conscious decision to prioritize usability. Crucially, despite the lower benchmark performance at the DPO stage, length control ultimately yields a more performant model post RL. At 7B scale, we conjecture that this arises from the RL training context window: with a fixed context window (8K), a shorter model may be “more intelligent per token,” allowing it to leverage the available budget more effectively during optimization. Thus, what initially appeared to be a tradeoff between usability and performance ultimately produced improvements in both. Moreover, we found that RL training progresses more reliably when initialized from the length-controlled DPO policy. Across most benchmarks, performance improves more steadily compared to RL runs starting from a higher-scoring but uncorrected DPO checkpoint, which tends to show earlier signs of instability or degradation. This further supports the role of concise preference-tuned models as advantageous starting points for RL.

中文: 偏好调优产生的简洁、可用输出能提升 RL 性能——在 DPO 期间应用长度控制可显著减少模型的平均生成长度，使我们能以一些性能换取更好的简洁性与整体可用性。虽然这种长度减少伴随对长度敏感评测（尤其是 AIME 和 MATH 等数学基准）上分数的降低，但我们的内部定性评估（「vibe tests」）几乎一致更偏好更短、更直接的模型。我们有意识地决定优先可用性。关键在于，尽管 DPO 阶段基准性能较低，长度控制最终在 RL 之后产出了性能更强的模型。在 7B 规模上，我们推测这源于 RL 训练的上下文窗口：在固定上下文窗口（8K）下，更短的模型可能「每令牌更智能」，使其在优化期间更有效地利用可用预算。因此，最初看似可用性与性能之间的权衡，最终在两方面都产生了改进。此外，我们发现从长度控制的 DPO 策略初始化时，RL 训练进展更可靠。在大多数基准上，相比从得分更高但未经校正的 DPO 检查点出发的 RL 运行（后者往往更早出现不稳定或退化的迹象），性能改进更平稳。这进一步支持了简洁的偏好调优模型作为 RL 有利起点的作用。

Fig. 23. 偏好数据的数据集大小与过滤的影响

Placed near: p.61 S186
Source: p.62 C034

Original caption: Effect of dataset size and filtering for preference data. Ideal preference dataset size depends on the downstream task (left). Both AlpacaEval and ZebraLogic performance increase up to around 75–100K samples, beyond which further data scaling hurts or does not help. In contrast, AIME2024 does not saturate before the point at which AlpacaEval and ZebraLogic begin to see drops in performance. Hence, to strike an ideal balance between all downstream tasks, we sweep dataset size as a hyperparameter during training. Unfiltered preference data exhibits a length bias (right). A significant portion of the data distribution has longer chosen than rejected completions.

中文图注: 偏好数据的数据集大小与过滤的影响。理想偏好数据集大小取决于下游任务（左）。AlpacaEval 与 ZebraLogic 的性能都增加到约 75–100K 样本为止，超过之后继续扩大数据规模会损害或无帮助。相比之下，AIME2024 在 AlpacaEval 与 ZebraLogic 开始出现性能下降之前并未饱和。因此，为在所有下游任务之间达成理想平衡，我们在训练期间将数据集大小作为超参数扫描。未过滤的偏好数据表现出长度偏差（右）。数据分布中有相当一部分的被选择补全长于被拒绝补全。

Reading note: 左图：不同 DPO 数据集大小对 AlpacaEval/AIME24/ZebraLogic 的影响；右图：偏好数据的长度偏差分布（chosen − rejected 令牌长度差）。


Source: p.62 S187

Original: Need for tools — We assess how much of Olmo 3 Instruct’s performance on LitQA2 and SimpleQA can be attributed to tool use by measuring the delta of the model performance on the benchmarks between answering the questions only from parametric memory (“No tools” setting) and doing so using tools. Table 31 shows these deltas in comparison to those from three Qwen models. All models benefit significantly from tool use on SimpleQA. However, Qwen models, unlike Olmo 3 Instruct 7B, mostly seem to rely on parametric knowledge for LitQA2, with two of the models even losing performance when provided with tools.

中文: 对工具的需求——我们通过测量模型在仅靠参数化记忆答题（「No tools」设置）与使用工具答题之间的基准性能差异（delta），来评估 Olmo 3 Instruct 在 LitQA2 与 SimpleQA 上有多大性能可归因于工具使用。表 31 展示了这些 delta 与三个 Qwen 模型的对比。所有模型在 SimpleQA 上都从工具使用中显著受益。然而，与 Olmo 3 Instruct 7B 不同，Qwen 模型在 LitQA2 上似乎主要依赖参数化知识，其中两个模型在提供工具时甚至性能下降。

Original: Table 31 — Comparison of agents’ performance with and without access to tools on LitQA2 and SimpleQA. ASC refers to Asta Scientific Corpus tools and SBT refers to search and browsing tools.

中文表注: 表 31 —— agent 在 LitQA2 与 SimpleQA 上有/无工具访问的性能对比。ASC 指 Asta Scientific Corpus 工具，SBT 指搜索与浏览工具。

6. Olmo 3 RL-Zero


Source: p.63 S188

Original: RL has become a key part of recent LLM pipelines in part due to prominent open models such Deepseek R1-Zero (Guo et al., 2025), which notably leverages RL training on top of a base model to bootstrap complex reasoning behavior (Marjanović et al., 2025), and due to the rapid adoption of closed reasoning models such as OpenAI’s o1-series and Gemini with Thinking. This has made RLVR finetuning from a base model the standard large-scale benchmark for RL algorithms (Liu et al., 2025a; Yu et al., 2025; Luo et al., 2025b). To date, leading open RLVR benchmarks and algorithms train on top of open-weights models that do not reveal their pretraining or midtraining data (Chu et al., 2025; Yang et al., 2025a). This limits the ability for the community to study the role of pretraining data on RLVR performance. It can lead to a myriad of issues with benchmark evaluations being contaminated, e.g., midtraining data containing data from the evaluation set, which makes spurious rewards as effective as true rewards (Shao et al., 2025b; Wu et al., 2025c), or improvements from fixing prompt templates outweighing the improvements from RL (Liu et al., 2025b). We therefore release a fully-open dataset Dolci RL-Zero, an algorithmic RL zero setup for Olmo 3, and open-source OlmoRL code to enable clear benchmarking for the ecosystem. We perform RLVR from Olmo 3 Base over five benchmarking domains to create the Olmo 3 RL-Zero family: math, code, precise instruction following (IF), general chat, and a mix of all listed sub-domains. In all cases, we further decontaminate Dolci RL-Zero from pretraining and midtraining data to guarantee our setup carefully studies the effect of RLVR without data leakage confounding our conclusions.

中文: RL 已成为近期 LLM 流水线的关键部分，部分原因在于 Deepseek R1-Zero（Guo et al., 2025）等杰出开放模型（其在基座模型之上利用 RL 训练引导出复杂推理行为，Marjanović et al., 2025），以及 OpenAI 的 o1 系列、Gemini with Thinking 等闭源推理模型的迅速普及。这使得从基座模型做 RLVR 微调成为 RL 算法的大规模标准基准（Liu et al., 2025a; Yu et al., 2025; Luo et al., 2025b）。迄今为止，领先的开放 RLVR 基准与算法都建立在不公开其预训练或中期训练数据的开放权重模型之上（Chu et al., 2025; Yang et al., 2025a）。这限制了社区研究预训练数据对 RLVR 性能影响的能力。它还可能导致基准评测被污染等一系列问题，例如：中期训练数据包含评测集数据，使虚假奖励与真实奖励同样有效（Shao et al., 2025b; Wu et al., 2025c）；或修复提示模板带来的改进超过 RL 本身的改进（Liu et al., 2025b）。因此，我们发布一个完全开放的数据集 Dolci RL-Zero、一套面向 Olmo 3 的算法化 RL zero 设置，并开源 OlmoRL 代码，为生态提供清晰的基准评测。我们在五个基准领域上从 Olmo 3 Base 执行 RLVR，构建 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 家族：数学、代码、精确指令遵循（IF）、通用对话，以及所有上述子领域的混合。在所有情况下，我们都进一步将 Dolci RL-Zero 与预训练和中期训练数据去污染，以确保我们的设置能够在不被数据泄漏干扰结论的前提下，仔细研究 RLVR 的效果。

6.1 Reinforcement Learning From Base with Dolci RL-Zero

6.1 用 Dolci RL-Zero 从基座做强化学习


Source: p.63 S189

Original: Data — We create Dolci RL-Zero, an effective RL-Zero training dataset. For math, we aggressively filter DAPO Math (Yu et al., 2025), Klear-Reasoner Math (Su et al., 2025c), Open-Reasoner-Zero (Orz) (Hu et al., 2025), and Omega (Sun et al., 2025). We deduplicate DAPO and remove all non-English examples. As Klear-Reasoner, Orz, and Omega are much larger datasets, we further group questions via semantic clustering across Klear-Reasoner, Orz, and Omega, and select one representative question per cluster, in addition to including DAPO. We further decontaminate against both pretraining and evaluation data following subsubsection 4.2.1 and perform offline filtering, removing prompts fully solved in 8 out of 8 sample completions by the final base model. This results in a dataset of 13.3K math prompts. Data for code, instruction-following, and general chat are subsampled from Dolci Think RL (Section §4.4.2).

中文: 数据——我们创建 Dolci RL-Zero，一个有效的 RL-Zero 训练数据集。对于数学，我们积极过滤 DAPO Math（Yu et al., 2025）、Klear-Reasoner Math（Su et al., 2025c）、Open-Reasoner-Zero（Orz）（Hu et al., 2025）和 Omega（Sun et al., 2025）。我们对 DAPO 去重并移除所有非英文示例。由于 Klear-Reasoner、Orz 和 Omega 是更大的数据集，我们在 Klear-Reasoner、Orz 和 Omega 之间通过语义聚类对问题分组，并在纳入 DAPO 之外，每个簇选择一个代表问题。我们进一步按第 4.2.1 小节对预训练与评测数据去污染，并执行离线过滤，移除最终基座模型在 8 个采样补全中 8 个全部解出的提示词。这得到一个 13.3K 数学提示词的数据集。代码、指令遵循与通用对话的数据从 Dolci Think RL（第 §4.4.2 节）下采样。


Source: p.63 S190

Original: Prompt and eval template — Confirming the findings of Liu et al. (2025b), we find that “simple” prompt templates greatly outperform standard post-trained templates (e.g., ) when training from a purely midtrained model, as Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix excluded most special formatting. We develop a simple custom prompt for each domain, using the zero-shot pass@k performance as our metric. We end up with a prompt similar to Yu et al. (2025), shown in Figure 37. We furthermore “clean” all our evaluation prompts to remove special formatting (i.e., \boxed{}) to make evaluation prompts more similar to our training prompts.

中文: 提示词与评测模板——印证 Liu et al. (2025b) 的发现，我们发现当从纯中期训练模型出发训练时，「简单」提示词模板显著优于标准的后训练模板（例如 ），因为 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix 排除了大多数特殊格式。我们为每个领域开发简单的定制提示词，使用零样本 pass@k 性能作为指标。最终得到的提示词与 Yu et al. (2025) 相似，如图 37 所示。我们还「清理」所有评测提示词，移除特殊格式（即 \boxed{}），使评测提示词更接近训练提示词。


Source: p.64 S191

Original: RL algorithm — We follow Section §4.4.1 in all RL details except (i) we train with a response length of 16K tokens to better accommodate long chain-of-thought reasoning in the math and code domains and (ii) we evaluate with a response length of 32K tokens and temperature 1.0 to encourage diversity as we report pass@k. See Table 49 for hyperparameter details.

中文: RL 算法——我们遵循第 §4.4.1 节的所有 RL 细节，除了（i）我们以 16K 令牌的响应长度训练，以更好地容纳数学与代码领域的长思维链推理；（ii）我们以 32K 令牌响应长度与 1.0 温度评测，以鼓励多样性（因为我们报告 pass@k）。超参数细节见表 49。

6.2 Key Findings

6.2 关键发现


Source: p.64 S192

Original: Olmo 3 RL-Zero can strongly improve on reasoning — As shown in Figure 24, our base model can greatly improve on training reward across the different domains when leveraging RL on our datasets. We find that Olmo 3 Base drastically improves in the first couple hundred steps of training and then improves steadily but slowly. To demonstrate out-of-domain improvements, we evaluate our math run on the decontaminated evals AIME 2024 and 2025. We also see a decent improvement in pass@32 results, demonstrating that our run maintains diversity and RLVR pushes the model beyond its initial capabilities. Our initial scores and final scores with the 7B model are, notably, close to DAPO (Yu et al., 2025) which leverages the larger Qwen 2.5 32B and trains for an order of magnitude more steps, see Figure 38 in Appendix A.6.4. This demonstrates how Olmo 3 RL-Zero can be a more efficient alternative to existing RLVR experiments.

中文: Olmo 3 RL-Zero 能显著改进推理——如图 24 所示，当在数据集上利用 RL 时，我们的基座模型能在不同领域上大幅改进训练奖励。我们发现 Olmo 3 Base 在训练前几百步大幅改进，随后稳步但缓慢地改进。为展示域外改进，我们在去污染评测 AIME 2024 和 2025 上评测了数学运行。我们还看到 pass@32 结果有不错的改进，表明我们的运行保持了多样性、RLVR 将模型推向了其初始能力之上。值得注意的是，我们 7B 模型的初始分数与最终分数接近 DAPO（Yu et al., 2025），而后者利用更大的 Qwen 2.5 32B 并训练了多一个数量级的步数，见图 38 与附录 A.6.4。这展示了 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 如何成为现有 RLVR 实验更高效的替代方案。

Fig. 24. Olmo 3 Base 上 RL-Zero 的不同领域运行

We show the main evaluation for the math domain: AIME 2024 and 2025 with pass@1, computed as a bootstrapped average over 32 samples, and pass@32. For all domains, we show reward over training. For Mix, we separate out the individual rewards for each domain.

Reading note: 该图展示各领域训练奖励上升、以及数学领域 AIME pass@1/pass@32 随训练步数的改进。


Source: p.64 S193

Original: Olmo 3 RL-Zero mix can benchmark challenges in multi-objective RL — Most studies have focused exclusively on RLHF (Stiennon et al., 2020) or single-domain RLVR (Yu et al., 2025; Luo et al., 2025a). Our mix of math, code, instruction-following, and general chat is a more challenging RLVR benchmark for models. Figure 24 demonstrates that our general run has improved performance across different domains, but each domain is under-optimized compared to the single-domain setup. Future work can leverage this setup to investigate the interactions between domains in multi-objective RLVR.

中文: Olmo 3 RL-Zero 混合可作为多目标 RL 挑战的基准——大多数研究只专注于 RLHF（Stiennon et al., 2020）或单领域 RLVR（Yu et al., 2025; Luo et al., 2025a）。我们的数学、代码、指令遵循与通用对话混合，是模型更具挑战性的 RLVR 基准。图 24 表明我们的通用运行在不同领域都有改进性能，但每个领域相比单领域设置都优化不足。未来工作可利用这一设置研究多目标 RLVR 中领域间的相互作用。


Source: p.64 S194

Original: Olmo 3 RL-Zero can benchmark reasoning data mixes in midtraining — Midtraining and Olmo 3 RL-Zero offer a chance to ablate specific data sources, unlike the large-scale effort behind Olmo 3 Think. We leverage RL-Zero to evaluate midtraining data mixes for their ability to develop downstream reasoning with RL. For example, we compare two early models in Figure 25. As evidenced by the stagnant response length, the model with insufficient reasoning data does not leverage backtracking, answer verification, and other cognitive skills (Gandhi et al., 2025). Olmo 3 RL-Zero can therefore serve as a testbed for downstream performance of alternative midtraining approaches and improvements over Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix.

中文: Olmo 3 RL-Zero 可作为中期训练推理数据混合的基准——与 Olmo 3 Think 背后的大规模努力不同，中期训练与 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 提供了消融特定数据源的机会。我们利用 RL-Zero 评估中期训练数据混合发展下游 RL 推理的能力。例如，图 25 比较了两个早期模型。如停滞的响应长度所示，推理数据不足的模型没有利用回溯、答案验证及其他认知技能（Gandhi et al., 2025）。因此，Olmo 3 RL-Zero 可作为替代中期训练方法及对 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix 改进的下游性能试验台。

Fig. 25. 两个早期中期训练基座模型在 RL 训练中的响应长度与数学奖励

Placed near: p.64 S194
Source: p.65 C036

Original caption: The response length and math reward over RL training for two early midtrained base models. This demonstrates how base model midtraining can determine whether RL-Zero learns longer, more complex reasoning and increases response length.

中文图注: 两个早期中期训练基座模型在 RL 训练中的响应长度与数学奖励。这展示了基座模型的中期训练如何决定 RL-Zero 能否学会更长、更复杂的推理并增加响应长度。

Reading note: 推理数据充足的模型（Reasoning Mix）响应长度随训练增长、奖励上升；推理数据不足的模型（Insufficient Mix）响应长度停滞。


Source: p.65 S195

Original: Active sampling stabilizes training — Olmo 3 RL-Zero also offers a simpler testbed for ablating RL algorithm and infrastructure decisions. We ablate active sampling, our novel method for continuously resampling prompts after filtering for non-zero advantage (see Section §4.4.3 for details). Running on our math domain, Figure 26 shows that active sampling does indeed maintain a consistently full batch of completions with non-zero advantage. These consistent batch sizes have a stabilizing effect on training, and we see greatly reduced loss variance.

中文: 主动采样稳定训练——Olmo 3 RL-Zero 还为消融 RL 算法与基础设施决策提供了更简单的试验台。我们消融了主动采样，即我们在过滤非零优势后持续重采样提示词的新方法（细节见第 §4.4.3 节）。在数学领域运行，图 26 显示主动采样确实维持了始终满员、优势非零的补全批次。这种一致的批量大小对训练有稳定作用，我们看到损失方差大幅降低。

Fig. 26. 主动采样维持满批次的非零优势样本

Placed near: p.65 S195
Source: p.65 C037

Original caption: Active sampling maintains a full batch of non-zero-advantage samples by continuously pulling prompt–completion pairs from the result queue after filtering. We plot the percentage of the batch with non-zero advantage as well as the train loss for an RL-Zero Math run with and without active sampling.

中文图注: 主动采样通过在过滤后从结果队列持续拉取提示词-补全对，维持满批次的非零优势样本。我们绘制了有/无主动采样的 RL-Zero 数学运行中，批次内非零优势样本的百分比与训练损失。

Reading note: 左图：主动采样使非零优势样本占比保持 ~100%；右图：训练损失方差显著降低。


Source: p.65 S196

Original: Eval decontamination is verified via spurious rewards — Recent RLVR benchmarks have shown substantial improvements from training with spurious rewards that are not correlated with model utility. This can suggest that the RLVR task may have been contaminated, i.e., the model was exposed to evaluation data during pretraining or midtraining. RLVR with a spurious reward can elicit this memorized knowledge, differentiating it from genuine learning of reasoning capabilities (Shao et al., 2025b). To verify that Olmo 3 RL-Zero evaluation is not contaminated, we conduct a negative control experiment by training Olmo 3 Base with spurious rewards. Specifically, we train on Dolci RL-Zero, but instead of rewarding correct answers, we assign random binary rewards to model generations independent of response quality following the protocol in Shao et al. (2025b). If our pretraining or midtraining data contained significant overlap with our evaluation sets, we would expect spurious reward training to elicit these memorized solutions and improve benchmark performance. As shown in Figure 27, training with random rewards does not improve performance on any of our benchmark evaluations. Performance either remains flat with random fluctuations or degrades, which is consistent with the model learning arbitrary patterns unrelated to the task. This negative result is evidence that our data decontamination successfully removed overlaps between our base-model pipeline and RLVR evaluation data.

中文: 通过虚假奖励验证评测去污染——近期 RLVR 基准显示，用与模型效用无关的虚假奖励训练会带来显著改进。这可能表明 RLVR 任务已被污染，即模型在预训练或中期训练期间接触到了评测数据。用虚假奖励做 RLVR 能引出这种被记忆的知识，从而与真正学会推理能力区分开来（Shao et al., 2025b）。为验证 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 的评测未被污染，我们通过用虚假奖励训练 Olmo 3 Base 进行负对照实验。具体而言，我们在 Dolci RL-Zero 上训练，但不是奖励正确答案，而是遵循 Shao et al. (2025b) 的协议，对模型生成分配与响应质量无关的随机二元奖励。若我们的预训练或中期训练数据与评测集有显著重叠，我们会预期虚假奖励训练引出这些被记忆的解答并改进基准性能。如图 27 所示，用随机奖励训练不会在我们任何基准评测上改进性能。性能要么随随机波动保持平稳，要么退化，这与模型学到与任务无关的任意模式一致。这一负结果证明，我们的数据去污染成功移除了基座模型流水线与 RLVR 评测数据之间的重叠。

Fig. 27. 在 Olmo 3 Base 上用随机、无信号奖励做 RL 训练不产生性能收益

Placed near: p.65 S196
Source: p.66 C038

Original caption: RL training on Olmo 3 Base on random, signal-free rewards produces no performance gains, suggesting successful decontamination of training data.

中文图注: 在 Olmo 3 Base 上用随机、无信号奖励做 RL 训练不产生任何性能收益，表明训练数据去污染成功。

Reading note: 该图是负对照实验：各基准（GPQA、ZebraLogic、Minerva Math、GSM8K、Omega 500、AIME、HumanEvalPlus、Alpaca Eval、IFEval）性能均不随训练步数提升，证明评测数据未被污染。

A.2 Base Model Additional Data Details: Pretraining

A.2 基座模型附加数据细节：预训练


Source: p.87 S201

Original: A.2.1 CommonCrawl — The majority of our pretraining corpus comes from CommonCrawl (Common Crawl Foundation). We start with 104 dumps, starting with CC-MAIN-2013-20 and ending with CC-MAIN-2024-51, roughly covering dates from mid-2013 until late 2024. We linearize the WET files provided by Commoncrawl using Resiliparse, yielding an initial pool composed of 252.6B documents. Next we apply a pipeline of heuristic filtering steps to further prune down the dataset to a size amenable for pretraining. Our steps essentially follow those of DCLM (Li et al., 2024a), with a few small differences. We start with URL-based filtering, identifying and removing documents that have URLs containing banned words or subwords from the blacklists used by FineWeb (Penedo et al., 2024) and RefinedWeb (Penedo et al., 2023). This step removes roughly 1% of the data pool. Then we apply the DCLM collection of heuristic filters, roughly targeting and removing: i) very short documents, ii) very long documents, iii) documents with not enough alphanumeric characters, and iv) documents with large amounts of internal repetition.

中文: A.2.1 CommonCrawl——我们的预训练语料大部分来自 CommonCrawl（Common Crawl Foundation）。我们从 104 个转储开始，从 CC-MAIN-2013-20 到 CC-MAIN-2024-51，大致覆盖 2013 年中到 2024 年末的日期。我们使用 Resiliparse 将 Commoncrawl 提供的 WET 文件线性化，得到由 252.6B 文档组成的初始数据池。接下来，我们应用一套启发式过滤流水线，将数据集进一步剪枝到适合预训练的规模。我们的步骤基本遵循 DCLM（Li et al., 2024a），有少量差异。我们从基于 URL 的过滤开始，识别并移除 URL 含 FineWeb（Penedo et al., 2024）与 RefinedWeb（Penedo et al., 2023）所用黑名单中禁用词或子词的文档。这一步移除约 1% 的数据池。随后我们应用 DCLM 的启发式过滤器集合，大致针对并移除：i）过短文档，ii）过长文档，iii）字母数字字符不足的文档，以及 iv）内部重复量大的文档。


Source: p.87 S202

Original: Next, we modify and remove any lines or paragraphs in each document that have i) too many numeric characters or ii) any boilerplate phrases such as “items in cart” or “read more…”, and then we fully remove any documents that have been obliterated by these line-specific removals. We then apply a FastText English language filter, mirroring DCLM and using a threshold of 0.65 to identify documents as containing English text. Finally, we apply a subset of the rules for identifying questionable sentences from MADLAD-400 (Kudugunta et al., 2023). Ablation tests show that only rules 2 and 5 from MADLAD improve dataset quality, targeting sentences that have a large number of capitalized words or contain a “cursed regex”. If the number of sentences in the document is less than 5 or if at least 20% of sentences are questionable, we remove the document from the corpus. Overall, the heuristic steps remove 76% of the total pool, and the English filtering step removes an additional 2.5% of the pool. This leaves a pool of 38.7B documents, attaining a survival rate of 15.1%.

中文: 接下来，我们修改并移除每篇文档中 i）数字字符过多、或 ii）含有「items in cart」「read more…」等样板短语的行或段落，然后彻底移除被这些逐行移除操作「摧毁」的任何文档。随后我们应用 FastText 英语语言过滤器，仿照 DCLM，使用 0.65 的阈值来识别文档是否包含英文文本。最后，我们应用 MADLAD-400（Kudugunta et al., 2023）中识别可疑句子规则的一个子集。消融测试显示，只有 MADLAD 的规则 2 和 5 能改进数据集质量，它们针对大写词过多或含「cursed regex」的句子。若文档中的句子数少于 5，或至少有 20% 的句子可疑，我们就将该文档从语料中移除。总体而言，启发式步骤移除了总数据池的 76%，英语过滤步骤再移除数据池的 2.5%。这剩下 38.7B 文档的数据池，存活率为 15.1%。


Source: p.87 S203

Original: A.2.2 Deduplication — As described in the main paper, we apply a three-stage deduplication pipeline to our dataset, with each stage targeting progressively more nuanced forms of redundancy: (i) global exact deduplication based on document content hashes to remove identical copies, (ii) 32-way sharded MinHash deduplication with exact Jaccard similarity verification to remove near-duplicate documents, and (iii) 56-way sharded fuzzy suffix array deduplication to eliminate repeated boilerplate text. We note that while applying exact deduplication before MinHash deduplication is technically redundant, exact deduplication is substantially more efficient computationally; hence this two-pass approach is much faster overall. For the exact and MinHash deduplication stages, we utilize the Duplodocus tool, and for the suffix array deduplication stage, we employ bsade.

中文: A.2.2 去重——如正文所述，我们对数据集应用三阶段去重流水线，每一阶段针对渐进式更细微的冗余形式：（i）基于文档内容哈希的全局精确去重，以移除相同副本；（ii）32 路分片的 MinHash 去重，配合精确 Jaccard 相似度验证，以移除近似重复文档；（iii）56 路分片的模糊后缀数组去重，以消除重复的样板文本。我们注意到，虽然在 MinHash 去重之前应用精确去重在技术上冗余，但精确去重在计算上高效得多；因此这种两遍方法总体上快得多。对于精确与 MinHash 去重阶段，我们使用 Duplodocus 工具；对于后缀数组去重阶段，我们使用 bsade。


Source: p.88 S204

Original: Exact Deduplication — We perform exact deduplication in two sequential passes. During the heuristic filtration pipeline, we annotate each document with a 128-bit hash computed from the document text. We then apply an initial deduplication step to each of the 104 processed CommonCrawl dumps individually, arbitrarily retaining one copy of each document per dump. This within-dump deduplication removes 24% of the surviving document pool. Following this, we aggregate all documents globally and perform a second exact deduplication pass across the entire corpus, again arbitrarily keeping one copy of each document. This global pass removes an additional 43% of the surviving pool. In total, exact deduplication eliminates 66% of the input documents, reducing the corpus to 12.7 billion documents for subsequent MinHash processing.

中文: 精确去重——我们以两次顺序遍执行精确去重。在启发式过滤流水线期间，我们为每篇文档标注一个由文档文本计算出的 128 位哈希。随后我们对 104 个已处理的 CommonCrawl 转储逐一应用初始去重步骤，每个转储任意保留每篇文档的一个副本。这种转储内去重移除了存活文档池的 24%。之后，我们全局聚合所有文档，并在整个语料上执行第二次精确去重遍，再次任意保留每篇文档的一个副本。这一全局遍再移除存活池的 43%。总计，精确去重消除了输入文档的 66%，将语料减少到 127 亿篇文档，供后续 MinHash 处理。


Source: p.88 S205

Original: MinHash Fuzzy deduplication — We partition the 12.7 billion document corpus resulting from exact deduplication into 32 shards of approximately equal size and perform MinHash deduplication independently on each shard. Our MinHash procedure broadly follows the approach outlined in (Lee et al., 2022). We tokenize documents using the p50k tokenizer and construct sets of 5-gram token sequences. We then apply a MinHash locality-sensitive hashing scheme with 26 bands of size 11, configured to target a Jaccard similarity threshold of 0.80. For any pair of documents that share at least one matching bucket, we treat them as connected by an edge in graph-theoretic terms. We construct a graph from the union of all such edges and identify connected components within this graph. Each document in a connected component is then annotated with a unique identifier for that component. In a second verification phase, we explicitly compute pairwise Jaccard similarities within each MinHash-identified cluster to eliminate false positives. For this verification, we use 3-gram token sequences.

中文: MinHash 模糊去重——我们将精确去重后得到的 127 亿文档语料划分为 32 个大致相等大小的分片，并在每个分片上独立执行 MinHash 去重。我们的 MinHash 过程大致遵循（Lee et al., 2022）所述的方法。我们使用 p50k 分词器对文档分词，并构造 5-gram 令牌序列集合。随后我们应用带 26 个 band（每 band 大小 11）的 MinHash 局部敏感哈希方案，目标 Jaccard 相似度阈值为 0.80。对于共享至少一个匹配桶的任何文档对，我们在图论意义下将它们视为由一条边相连。我们从所有这些边的并集构造一个图，并识别该图中的连通分量。连通分量中的每篇文档随后被标注该分量的唯一标识符。在第二个验证阶段，我们在每个 MinHash 识别出的簇内显式计算成对 Jaccard 相似度，以消除误报。此验证使用 3-gram 令牌序列。


Source: p.88 S206

Original: Our approach varies based on cluster size: for connected components containing 500 or more documents, we apply a more stringent MinHash configuration using 200 bands of size 31; for components with fewer than 500 documents, we perform exhaustive pairwise Jaccard similarity checks and generate final duplicate clusters from these results. After annotating all documents according to their true Jaccard similarity with other documents in the corpus, we retain only the most recent version of each document based on crawl date, removing all earlier duplicates. This complete MinHash deduplication procedure eliminates 24% of the input documents, leaving 9.8 billion documents in the pool.

中文: 我们的方法根据簇大小而变化：对于含 500 篇及以上文档的连通分量，我们应用更严格的 MinHash 配置，使用 200 个 band（每 band 大小 31）；对于少于 500 篇文档的分量，我们执行穷举的成对 Jaccard 相似度检查，并从这些结果生成最终的重复簇。在按每篇文档与语料中其他文档的真实 Jaccard 相似度标注所有文档后，我们按爬取日期仅保留每篇文档的最新版本，移除所有更早的重复项。这一完整的 MinHash 去重过程消除了输入文档的 24%，留下 98 亿篇文档。


Source: p.88 S207

Original: Suffix Array deduplication — In the final deduplication stage, we employ suffix arrays to identify and remove substrings that appear repeatedly throughout the dataset. We partition the 9.8 billion document corpus into 56 shards of roughly equal size and run suffix array deduplication independently on each shard. For each shard, we construct a suffix array and identify every byte sequence of length 500 or greater that appears at least twice in the shard. We then apply a novel “fuzzy suffix array” removal strategy that considers contiguous text spans within each document. Specifically, if a text span is bounded on both sides by 500-byte sequences that appear multiple times in the suffix array, and at least 80% of the span is covered by such repeated sequences, we remove the entire span. For text that does not meet this bookended criterion, we remove all individual occurrences of repeated 500-byte sequences. After these three rounds of deduplication—exact, MinHash, and suffix array—we arrive at a final corpus of 9.7 billion documents.

中文: 后缀数组去重——在最后去重阶段，我们使用后缀数组来识别并移除在整个数据集中反复出现的子串。我们将 98 亿文档语料划分为 56 个大致相等大小的分片，并在每个分片上独立运行后缀数组去重。对每个分片，我们构造后缀数组，并识别分片中出现至少两次、长度为 500 及以上的每个字节序列。随后我们应用一种新颖的「模糊后缀数组」移除策略，它考虑每篇文档内的连续文本片段。具体而言，若某文本片段两侧都被在后缀数组中多次出现的 500 字节序列包围，且至少 80% 的片段被此类重复序列覆盖，我们就移除整个片段。对于不满足此「两侧包围」准则的文本，我们移除所有重复 500 字节序列的单个出现。经过精确、MinHash 与后缀数组这三轮去重，我们得到 97 亿篇文档的最终语料。


Source: p.89 S208

Original: A.2.3 Topic Classification — After strict rounds of deduplication, we partition our data according to topic using the 24 topic categories introduced in WebOrganizer (Wettig et al., 2025). Rather than using the 140M parameter topic classifier used by WebOrganizer, we train a FastText classifier to support cost-effective topic classification at scale. To train this classifier, we use the Llama-labeled training data used to train the original WebOrganizer category as well as an extra 506,746 examples with topics labeled by a combination of gpt-4.1 and o4-mini. The performance of this classifier is outlined in Table 37.

中文: A.2.3 主题分类——经过严格去重后，我们使用 WebOrganizer（Wettig et al., 2025）引入的 24 个主题类别按主题划分数据。我们不用 WebOrganizer 所用的 140M 参数主题分类器，而是训练一个 FastText 分类器，以支持大规模上经济高效的主题分类。为训练该分类器，我们使用训练原始 WebOrganizer 类别所用的 Llama 标注训练数据，以及额外的 506,746 个由 gpt-4.1 与 o4-mini 组合标注主题的示例。该分类器的性能见表 37（整体 F1 约 0.762）。


Source: p.89 S209

Original: A.2.4 CommonCrawl Mixing — We perform a hierarchical mixing procedure on our data. Our procedure Olmix (Chen et al., 2026) generates prescriptions for which percentage of the training mix should come from each topic or source, but offers no guidance on the quality composition within each topic. While prior works, such as DCLM (Li et al., 2024a) use a quality classifier to flatly filter data as high-quality or not, we take a more fine-grained approach and perform selective up and down-sampling within each WebOrganizer topic depending on the quality signal. This section formalizes the search procedure we use to generate these upsampling curves.

中文: A.2.4 CommonCrawl 混合——我们对数据执行分层混合过程。我们的过程 Olmix（Chen et al., 2026）会为训练混合中每个主题或来源应占的百分比生成处方，但不对每个主题内部的质量构成提供指导。虽然 DCLM（Li et al., 2024a）等先前工作使用质量分类器将数据扁平地过滤为「高质量与否」，我们则采取更细粒度的方法，根据质量信号在每个 WebOrganizer 主题内进行选择性的上调与下调采样。本节形式化我们用于生成这些上采样曲线的搜索过程。


Source: p.89 S210

Original: Problem formulation — We discuss this procedure in more general terms: consider a category with X tokens, partitioned into Q strictly ordered quality buckets, where the qth bucket contains Xq tokens. Further assume that Olmix prescribes that Z tokens be taken from this category, and that at no point do we want to upsample any quality bucket more than M times. This equates to a search problem, where we need to take Zq tokens from the qth bucket such that Σq Zq = Z and ∀q, Zq/Xq ≤ M. Parameterizing the solution space — To reduce the dimensionality of this search space, we make a modeling choice, where we search over a family of functions that control the upsampling ratio. Every function in the family is convex and monotonic; the functions are defined on the interval [0, 1]; we are able to control the integral such that ∫₀¹ f(x)dx = Z/X; and we can control the maximum average value of any one bucket. One such family of functions that meets these criteria is the family of truncated power-exponential functions: f_{p,λ}(x) = 0 for x < a, and f_{p,λ}(x) = C(x−a)^p · e^{λ(x−a)} for x ≥ a.

中文: 问题表述——我们用更通用的术语讨论此过程：考虑一个含 X 令牌的类别，划分为 Q 个严格有序的质量桶，其中第 q 桶含 Xq 令牌。进一步假设 Olmix 规定从该类别取 Z 令牌，且我们任何时候都不希望对任何质量桶上采样超过 M 倍。这等价于一个搜索问题：我们需要从第 q 桶取 Zq 令牌，使得 Σq Zq = Z 且 ∀q, Zq/Xq ≤ M。参数化解空间——为降低此搜索空间的维度，我们做一个建模选择：在一个控制上采样比的函数族上搜索。该族中每个函数都是凸且单调的；函数定义在 [0, 1] 区间上；我们能控制积分使 ∫₀¹ f(x)dx = Z/X；且我们能控制任一桶的最大平均值。满足这些准则的此类函数族之一是截断幂指数函数族：f_{p,λ}(x) = 0（当 x < a），f_{p,λ}(x) = C(x−a)^p · e^{λ(x−a)}（当 x ≥ a）。


Source: p.90 S211

Original: Specifically, this becomes a feasibility problem for each topic of the data, where we search over parameters p, λ, C such that the constraints (Token yield is satisfied) ∫₀¹ f_{p,λ}(x)dx = Z/X; (Maximum upsampling ratio is honored) 1/(b) ∫_{1−b}^{1} f_{p,λ}(x)dx ≤ M; (Function is monotonic) λ ≥ 0 are satisfied. Implementation details — For each WebOrganizer topic, we set the maximum upsampling ratio to be M = 7 and also throw away the bottom 40% in terms of quality, a = 0.40. Then we numerically solve for feasible p, λ, C.

中文: 具体而言，这成为数据每个主题的可行性问题，我们在参数 p、λ、C 上搜索，使约束（令牌产出满足）∫₀¹ f_{p,λ}(x)dx = Z/X；（最大上采样比满足）(1/b)∫_{1−b}^{1} f_{p,λ}(x)dx ≤ M；（函数单调）λ ≥ 0 均得到满足。实现细节——对每个 WebOrganizer 主题，我们设最大上采样比 M = 7，并丢弃质量底部 40%（a = 0.40）。随后数值求解可行的 p、λ、C。


Source: p.90 S212

Original: A.2.5 Validating Quality Upsampling and Mixing — We validate our quality upsampling curves and mixing methodology both individually and jointly using small-scale 1B parameter models trained on 100B tokens. Our validation consists of three experiments: Targeted mixing — We first verify that our mixing methodology can successfully optimize for specific prediction targets. Using our swarm optimization procedure, we create mixes optimized for three different objectives: the QA average, Math average, and Code average from OlmoBaseEval. We compare these targeted mixes against both the natural data distribution and the final Olmo 3 mix. Table 38 demonstrates that our swarm optimization successfully adapts the data distribution to match specific capability targets.

中文: A.2.5 验证质量上采样与混合——我们使用在 100B 令牌上训练的 1B 参数小模型，分别及联合地验证质量上采样曲线与混合方法。验证包括三个实验：定向混合——我们首先验证混合方法能成功针对特定预测目标优化。使用集群优化过程，我们创建针对三个不同目标的混合：OlmoBaseEval 的 QA 平均、Math 平均与 Code 平均。我们将这些定向混合与自然数据分布及最终 Olmo 3 混合对比。表 38 表明我们的集群优化成功调整了数据分布以匹配特定能力目标。


Source: p.90 S213

Original: Quality-aware upsampling — Next, we demonstrate that quality-aware upsampling outperforms naive quality-based filtering. To simulate a data-constrained 4.51T token training run, we compare different data selection strategies in Table 39. For the filtering baselines, we select the top percentiles from our vigintile quality buckets. For the upsampling approach, we apply the same methodology but set the target pool size to 100B tokens directly. Our results show that quality-aware upsampling consistently outperforms flat filtering across all repetition factors. Reconciling upsampling and mixing — Finally, we evaluate how to best combine our mixing and upsampling methodologies. We considered several techniques that did not work quite as well as the truncated power-exponential forms described in §A.2.4. Upon considering each of these techniques on small 1B models, we found that the truncated power-exponential family performed the best. Results are contained in Table 40.

中文: 质量感知上采样——接下来，我们展示质量感知上采样优于朴素的基于质量的过滤。为模拟数据受限的 4.51T 令牌训练运行，我们在表 39 中比较不同的数据选择策略。对于过滤基线，我们从 vigintile 质量桶中选择顶部百分位。对于上采样方法，我们应用相同方法，但直接将目标池大小设为 100B 令牌。结果显示，质量感知上采样在所有重复因子下都一致优于平坦过滤。协调上采样与混合——最后，我们评估如何最佳地结合混合与上采样方法。我们考虑了几种不如 §A.2.4 所述截断幂指数形式有效的技术。在 1B 小模型上考虑每种技术后，我们发现截断幂指数族表现最佳。结果见表 40。

A.3 Base Model Additional Data Details: Midtraining

A.3 基座模型附加数据细节：中期训练

Original: This section provides further detail on curation processes for Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix. Additional replication resources, including prompts for synthetic data generation, are available in the Dolma3 GitHub repository. A.3.1 Math Capabilities — Similar to OLMo 2, we take particular care to curate math-specific mixes of data during the midtraining phase of training. In this section we discuss some of the procedures used to generate, as well as validate, the math-specific data sources. It should be noted, that while there has been a flurry of research on high-quality, open-source, STEM-focused datasets, many of these are synthetic data generated using LLama-models, which carry with them a restrictive Llama license. We produce several reproductions of these with more permissive licensing and urge the community to take care in the licensing of the data they release if they wish to see adoption for research or commercial purposes.

中文: 本节进一步详述 Dolma 3 Dolmino Mix 的整理过程。额外的复现资源（包括合成数据生成的提示词）见 Dolma3 GitHub 仓库。A.3.1 数学能力——与 OLMo 2 类似，我们在训练的中期训练阶段特别注意整理数学专用数据混合。本节讨论用于生成与验证数学专用数据源的一些过程。应当指出，虽然出现了大量关于高质量、开源、STEM 聚焦数据集的研究，但其中许多是使用 Llama 模型生成的合成数据，带有受限的 Llama 许可。我们以更宽松的许可生成了这些数据集的多个复刻版，并敦促社区注意所发布数据的许可，若希望看到其被用于研究或商业目的。


Source: p.92 S215

Original: TinyMATH — In OLMo 2, great strides were made in performance on the GSM8K (Cobbe et al., 2021) dataset by generating synthetic math problems seeded from the original GSM training set, and then generating both python code (PoT) and natural language discussions of solutions (MIND). We adopt a similar strategy here, to target the MATH dataset (Hendrycks et al., 2021c). Namely, we adopt the TinyGSM protocol (Liu et al., 2023a) and prompts to generate 100 new problems for each existing MATH problem, and then generate pythonic solutions for each of these new problems. Then we apply the MIND rewrite prompt (Akter et al., 2024), using the two-student and problem-solving variants. This yields the PoT dataset (241M tokens) and the MIND dataset (899M tokens).

中文: TinyMATH——在 OLMo 2 中，通过从原始 GSM 训练集播种生成合成数学题，再生成 Python 代码（PoT）与自然语言解答讨论（MIND），在 GSM8K（Cobbe et al., 2021）数据集上取得了长足进步。我们在此采用类似策略，针对 MATH 数据集（Hendrycks et al., 2021c）。即，我们采用 TinyGSM 协议（Liu et al., 2023a）与提示词，为每个现有 MATH 题生成 100 道新题，再为每道新题生成 Python 解答。随后我们应用 MIND 重写提示词（Akter et al., 2024），使用双学生与问题求解两种变体。这得到 PoT 数据集（241M 令牌）与 MIND 数据集（899M 令牌）。


Source: p.92 S216

Original: CraneMath — SwallowMath (Fujii et al., 2025) is a 2.3 Billion token dataset, generated from rewriting FineMath4+ (Allal et al., 2025). Unfortunately the data was rewritten using a Llama model, which would require that any model trained on this data would need to have “Llama” in the name, according to the Llama Community License. To provide truly open data, we mirror the generation of this dataset, but use Qwen3 32B (Yang et al., 2025a) to rewrite FineMath4+ using the prompt presented in the SwallowMath paper. This yields a 5.62B token dataset we refer to as CraneMath. Compared to the 9.6B tokens contained in FineMATH4+, CraneMath is a distillation into fewer tokens, but not as few as SwallowMath (2.3B).

中文: CraneMath——SwallowMath（Fujii et al., 2025）是一个 23 亿令牌数据集，通过对 FineMath4+（Allal et al., 2025）重写生成。不幸的是，该数据是使用 Llama 模型重写的，根据 Llama 社区许可，任何在此数据上训练的模型都需在名称中含「Llama」。为提供真正开放的数据，我们镜像该数据集的生成，但使用 Qwen3 32B（Yang et al., 2025a）按 SwallowMath 论文中呈现的提示词重写 FineMath4+。这得到一个 5.62B 令牌的数据集，我们称之为 CraneMath。与 FineMath4+ 所含的 9.6B 令牌相比，CraneMath 是蒸馏到更少令牌，但不如 SwallowMath（2.3B）那么少。


Source: p.92 S217

Original: MegaMatt — OctoThinker (Wang et al., 2025) generated a 70B token data pool dubbed Megamath-Web-Pro-Max, intended to be a rewrite of LLM360’s MegaMath data pool (Liu et al., 2023c), with quality mirroring that of the MegaMath-Web-Pro quality. Again, unfortunately, MegaMath-Web-Pro-Max was rewritten using Llama, and an independent recreation needed to be performed for fully-open usage in training. Since our initial ablations showed that the Megamath-Web-Pro-Max pool wasn’t as high of quality as, say, SwallowMath, we didn’t need a recreation of the full 70B pool. Instead, we generated a recreation of just the documents from Megamath-Web-Pro-Max that occurred in CommonCrawl dumps from dump CC-MAIN-2023-23 and later. We ultimately generated 3.9B tokens of data, dubbed MegaMatt.

中文: MegaMatt——OctoThinker（Wang et al., 2025）生成了一个 70B 令牌的数据池，名为 Megamath-Web-Pro-Max，旨在重写 LLM360 的 MegaMath 数据池（Liu et al., 2023c），质量对标 MegaMath-Web-Pro。同样不幸的是，Megamath-Web-Pro-Max 使用 Llama 重写，需执行独立复刻以供训练中的全开放使用。由于我们的初始消融显示 Megamath-Web-Pro-Max 数据池的质量不如 SwallowMath，我们无需复刻全部 70B 数据池。取而代之，我们仅复刻了 Megamath-Web-Pro-Max 中出现在 CC-MAIN-2023-23 及以后 CommonCrawl 转储中的文档。最终我们生成了 3.9B 令牌的数据，名为 MegaMatt。


Source: p.93 S218

Original: Key Findings and Results — We summarize the annealing results for the math datasets in Table 41. Each value reflects the change in the evaluation score relative to the pre-anneal baseline, normalized by the number of training tokens. Presenting the results this way highlights several distinct tiers of math-data quality, stratified by the effect-per-token. Notably, these quality tiers anticorrelate with the number of available tokens: the highest-quality sources are also the smallest. At the top of the quality-spectrum are the tinyMATH variants. Although each contains less than a billion tokens, they deliver the strongest improvement per token. Next in the tier-list of quality are the synthetic rewrites of natural high-quality data: the Crane, SwallowMath and MegaMatt sources which are each rewrites of FineMath4+ and MegaMathWeb-Pro. Finally, the largest data sources, including those of naturally occurring data such as FineMath4+ and MegamathWeb, also yield improvements, but their effect-per-token is noticeably smaller than that of the highly curated synthetic data. Finally we note that the effect of math midtraining on MMLU is generally neutral to negative, but is more strongly negative the more targeted the data pool is to Math evals, suggesting “overcooking”, where increased specialization comes at the expense of broader general-purpose performance.

中文: 关键发现与结果——表 41 总结了数学数据集的退火结果。每个值反映相对退火前基线、按训练令牌数归一化的评测分数变化。以这种方式呈现结果，凸显了数学数据质量的几个不同层级（按每令牌效果分层）。值得注意的是，这些质量层级与可用令牌数呈反相关：最高质量的来源也是最小的。质量谱顶端是 tinyMATH 变体。尽管每个不到十亿令牌，它们带来了最强的每令牌改进。质量层级表中的下一级是自然高质量数据的合成重写：Crane、SwallowMath 和 MegaMatt 来源（各为 FineMath4+ 和 MegaMathWeb-Pro 的重写）。最后，最大的数据源（包括 FineMath4+ 和 MegamathWeb 等自然数据）也带来改进，但其每令牌效果明显小于高度整理的合成数据。最后我们注意到，数学中期训练对 MMLU 的影响总体中性偏负，且数据池越针对 Math 评测，负面影响越强，暗示「过拟合」，即增加的专门化以牺牲更广泛的通用性能为代价。


Source: p.93 S219

Original: A.3.2 Code Capabilities — During pretraining, we relied entirely on stack-edu (Allal et al., 2025) for providing coding data. During midtraining, we focused on improving Python and code-completion capabilities. To this end, we incorporated 10B tokens of FIM-transformed data from the same source as the pretraining code mixture. Inspired by improvements in math metrics by incorporating synthetic data, we also created a fully-open replica of SwallowCode (Fujii et al., 2025), which we call CraneCode. Of the off-the-shelf synthetic code data sources we considered, SwallowCode provided the greatest lift to coding evaluation metrics. Unfortunately, SwallowCode was generated using Llama models and thus had the less-permissive Llama license attached. We created a replica of SwallowCode by starting with just the python files from The Stack v2 Smol (Lozhkov et al., 2024), and applying the compilation and linting filters just as in SwallowCode. Then we applied a two-stage rewriting process, first to generate code data that is more compliant to the python style guides (SGCR), and then to generate optimized code (SCOR); both using the prompts from the original SwallowCode paper and Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct (Qwen et al., 2024).

中文: A.3.2 代码能力——在预训练期间，我们完全依赖 stack-edu（Allal et al., 2025）提供代码数据。在中期训练期间，我们聚焦于改进 Python 与代码补全能力。为此，我们从与预训练代码混合相同的来源纳入了 10B 令牌的 FIM 变换数据。受合成数据改进数学指标的启发，我们还创建了 SwallowCode（Fujii et al., 2025）的全开放复刻版，称之为 CraneCode。在我们考虑的现成合成代码数据源中，SwallowCode 对编码评测指标的提升最大。不幸的是，SwallowCode 使用 Llama 模型生成，因此附有许可较不宽松的 Llama 许可。我们从 The Stack v2 Smol（Lozhkov et al., 2024）中仅取 Python 文件、并如 SwallowCode 那样应用编译与 linting 过滤，创建了 SwallowCode 的复刻版。随后我们应用两阶段重写过程：首先生成更符合 Python 风格指南（SGCR）的代码数据，然后生成优化代码（SCOR）；两者都使用原始 SwallowCode 论文的提示词与 Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct（Qwen et al., 2024）。


Source: p.93 S220

Original: A.3.3 Thinking Capabilities — Recent work demonstrates that structured meta reasoning capabilities present during pre-training and mid-training serve as the foundation for successful reinforcement learning in complex reasoning tasks. Gandhi et al. (2025) showed that models exhibiting verification and backtracking behaviors during base training achieved dramatically superior performance trajectories during mathematical reasoning RL. Therefore, we begin by identifying structured reasoning capabilities that are critical for mathematical problem-solving. We select seven core capabilities that are foundational to mathematical and programming expertise: self-awareness, self-evaluation, goal management, hierarchical organization, backward chaining, backtracking and conceptual reasoning. We then design specific tasks that systematically target these capabilities, as shown in Table 43 and 44.

中文: A.3.3 思考能力——近期工作表明，预训练与中期训练期间呈现的结构化元推理能力，是在复杂推理任务中成功做强化学习的基础。Gandhi et al. (2025) 表明，在基座训练期间表现出验证与回溯行为的模型，在数学推理 RL 中取得了显著更优的性能轨迹。因此，我们从识别对数学问题求解至关重要的结构化推理能力入手。我们选择七项构成数学与编程专长基础的核心能力：自我意识、自我评估、目标管理、层级组织、逆向链、回溯与概念推理。随后我们设计系统针对这些能力的具体任务，如表 43 与 44 所示。


Source: p.94 S221

Original: For data generation, we start with existing math (Luo et al., 2025a; Moshkov et al., 2025) and coding (Li et al., 2023a; Hendrycks et al., 2021a; Ahmad et al., 2025) problems and their corresponding correct answers. Following Pandalla dataset, we automatically augment each problem with detailed annotations covering “problem classification”, “difficulty analysis”, “solution approaches”, “common pitfalls”, and “verification methods”. These rich annotations serve as inputs for our capability-targeted tasks. Using the annotated datasets as foundation, we employ GPT-4.1 and o4-mini to generate training data at scale for each capability-targeted task.

中文: 对于数据生成，我们从现有数学（Luo et al., 2025a; Moshkov et al., 2025）与编码（Li et al., 2023a; Hendrycks et al., 2021a; Ahmad et al., 2025）问题及其对应正确答案出发。遵循 Pandalla 数据集，我们自动为每个问题增补覆盖「问题分类」「难度分析」「求解思路」「常见陷阱」「验证方法」的详细标注。这些丰富标注作为我们针对能力定制的任务的输入。以带标注的数据集为基础，我们使用 GPT-4.1 与 o4-mini 为每个针对能力定制的任务大规模生成训练数据。


Source: p.94 S222

Original: Existing thinking traces — The full list of existing thinking traces is as follows: 1. General reasoning mix is a compilation of three existing datasets: GeneralThought-430K, OpenThoughts-114k (Guha et al., 2025b), and Open-R1-Math-220k. The resulting dataset contains questions, thinking traces, and answers for topics spanning math, code, natural sciences, humanities, social sciences, and puzzles. 2. Gemini reasoning traces (Muennighoff et al., 2025b) contains thinking traces covering domains of math, astronomy, biology, chemistry, computer science, geography, physics, English, law, logic, and more. 3. OpenThoughts2 reasoning traces (Guha et al., 2025b) contains thinking traces in domains of math, science, code, and puzzles. 4. Llama Nemotron reasoning traces (Bercovich et al., 2025) contains thinking trace data for math, code, general reasoning, and instruction following. 5. QwQ reasoning traces consists of the QwQ subset of the OpenMathReasoning dataset (Moshkov et al., 2025).

中文: 现有思维轨迹——现有思维轨迹的完整清单如下：1. General reasoning mix 是三个现有数据集的汇编：GeneralThought-430K、OpenThoughts-114k（Guha et al., 2025b）与 Open-R1-Math-220k。所得数据集包含覆盖数学、代码、自然科学、人文学科、社会科学与谜题主题的问题、思维轨迹与答案。2. Gemini reasoning traces（Muennighoff et al., 2025b）包含覆盖数学、天文、生物、化学、计算机科学、地理、物理、英语、法律、逻辑等领域的思维轨迹。3. OpenThoughts2 reasoning traces（Guha et al., 2025b）包含数学、科学、代码与谜题领域的思维轨迹。4. Llama Nemotron reasoning traces（Bercovich et al., 2025）包含数学、代码、通用推理与指令遵循的思维轨迹数据。5. QwQ reasoning traces 由 OpenMathReasoning 数据集（Moshkov et al., 2025）的 QwQ 子集组成。

Original: Filtering steps included subselecting for permissively-licensed generations, filtering to remove empty and truncated responses, performing checks of verifiable claims and safety, filtering overt LLM self-references, filtering heavily repeated sentences, paragraphs, and phrases, and removing reasoning traces consisting of greater than 5% Chinese characters.

中文: 过滤步骤包括：子选许可宽松的生成；过滤以移除空响应与截断响应；检查可验证声明与安全性；过滤明显的 LLM 自我引用；过滤大量重复的句子、段落与短语；以及移除中文字符占比超过 5% 的思维轨迹。

A.4 Base Model Additional Evaluation Details

A.4 基座模型附加评测细节


Source: p.95 S224

Original: The OlmoBaseEval suite expands on the 11 tasks in the OLMo 2 iteration of OLMES (OLMo et al., 2024; Gu et al., 2024b), to include 43 tasks across new families of capabilities. Here, we enumerate details from Section §3.3. All task suites are publicly available. Expanding OLMES tasks — We expanded our evaluation to target specific capabilities: new QA tasks focusing on science knowledge (SciQ, QASPER, SciRIFF), medical/lab knowledge (ProtocolQA, DBQA, MedMCQA, MedQA), math tasks (GSM Symbolic, Minerva MATH) and coding tasks (DS 1000, BigCodeBench, Deepseek LeetCode, MultiPL-E HumanEval, MultiPL-E MBPP). We use MultiPL-E to evaluate our multilingual code execution, limited to six core programming languages. Additionally, we track fill-in-the-middle (FIM) performance using HumanEval with the three settings from Bavarian et al. (2022): single-line infilling, multi-line infilling and random span infilling.

中文: OlmoBaseEval 套件在 OLMo 2 版 OLMES（OLMo et al., 2024; Gu et al., 2024b）的 11 个任务基础上扩展，纳入跨新能力族的 43 个任务。这里我们列举第 §3.3 节的细节。所有任务套件均公开。扩展 OLMES 任务——我们扩展评测以针对特定能力：聚焦科学知识的新 QA 任务（SciQ、QASPER、SciRIFF）、医学/实验知识（ProtocolQA、DBQA、MedMCQA、MedQA）、数学任务（GSM Symbolic、Minerva MATH）与编码任务（DS 1000、BigCodeBench、Deepseek LeetCode、MultiPL-E HumanEval、MultiPL-E MBPP）。我们使用 MultiPL-E 评测多语言代码执行，限于六种核心编程语言。此外，我们使用 HumanEval 跟踪中间填充（FIM）性能，采用 Bavarian et al. (2022) 的三种设置：单行填充、多行填充与随机片段填充。


Source: p.95 S225

Original: We support code execution in Python, C++, Java, JavaScript, PHP, Rust and Shell using AWS Lambda functions to grade instances in parallel, isolated environments of up to 50K generations simultaneously. In total, our environments graded 17.2 million generated code samples during Olmo 3 development. To ensure reproducibility, we release a lightweight Docker library for code execution without AWS infrastructure. Additionally, OLMo 2 only tracked math and code capabilities after mid-training, as small models exhibit random-chance pass@1 performance on math and code tasks (Wei et al., 2022). Our base easy suite tracks perplexity over human-written math and code solutions (Huang et al., 2024b), which allows us to broaden the scope of capabilities we track during pre-training.

中文: 我们使用 AWS Lambda 函数支持 Python、C++、Java、JavaScript、PHP、Rust 与 Shell 的代码执行，在并行、隔离的环境中同时对多达 50K 个生成打分。总计，在 Olmo 3 开发期间，我们的环境对 1720 万个生成的代码样本打分。为确保可复现性，我们发布了一个轻量级 Docker 库，无需 AWS 基础设施即可做代码执行。此外，OLMo 2 仅在中期训练后跟踪数学与代码能力，因为小模型在数学与代码任务上表现出随机水平的 pass@1（Wei et al., 2022）。我们的 base easy 套件跟踪人工书写数学与代码解答上的困惑度（Huang et al., 2024b），使我们能在预训练期间拓宽所跟踪能力的范围。

A.4.1 基座评测套件


Source: p.96 S226

Original: Using the analysis tools described in the previous section, we construct two evaluation suites for decision making in pre-training: the Base Easy suite for small-scale data decisions and the Base Main suite for in-loop evaluation and mid-training data decisions. Table 46 describes the task configuration and metrics for the Olmo 3 Base Main evaluation suite. Table 45 provides an overview of the Base Easy suite. Base Easy suite — For multiple-choice BPB, we simply use the correct answer as the continuation. For math BPB, we use the provided human-written solutions from Minerva MATH (Lewkowycz et al., 2022). For code BPB, we use the gold “canonical” solution as provided in HumanEval and MBPP. For BPB over non-Python coding tasks, MultiPL-E did not release gold solutions, so we generate silver continuations for 16 languages using o4-mini-medium. Figure 30 shows the scaling behavior of the three base easy task clusters, where we see signal even at very small (190M parameter) model sizes.

中文: 使用上一节所述的分析工具，我们构建两个用于预训练决策的评测套件：Base Easy 套件（用于小规模数据决策）与 Base Main 套件（用于循环评测与中期训练数据决策）。表 46 描述了 Olmo 3 Base Main 评测套件的任务配置与指标。表 45 提供了 Base Easy 套件的概览。Base Easy 套件——对于多项选择 BPB，我们直接用正确答案作为续写。对于数学 BPB，我们使用 Minerva MATH（Lewkowycz et al., 2022）提供的人工书写解答。对于代码 BPB，我们使用 HumanEval 与 MBPP 提供的金标准「canonical」解答。对于非 Python 编码任务的 BPB，MultiPL-E 未发布金标准解答，因此我们使用 o4-mini-medium 为 16 种语言生成银标准续写。图 30 展示了三个 base easy 任务簇的缩放行为，其中我们甚至在极小（190M 参数）模型规模下也能看到信号。
