Original: One important property of the Base eval suite is that a ranking of two small models on the base easy suite agrees with their ranking on the downstream base main suite. We validate this by measuring rank correlation between the easy and main task suites, as pictured in Figure 31. Base Main suite — As a result of the clustering procedure, the base main suite tracks 6 task groups: MCQA STEM, MCQA Non-STEM, Gen, Math, Code, Code FIM. Unlike OLMo 2, we are tracking generative math and code tasks at pre-training. We chose to evaluate pass@k with the largest number of samples such that each task could evaluate on OLMo 2 7B on 1 H100 in under 30 minutes, in order to ensure the eval speed is not bottlenecked by any particular task. For tasks with a large enough n, we set k = 16 to match the GRPO group size, which we observed to act as an empirical upper-bound on the possible improvement from RL training.

中文: Base 评测套件的一个重要性质是：两个小模型在 base easy 套件上的排名，与其在下游 base main 套件上的排名一致。我们通过测量 easy 与 main 任务套件之间的秩相关来验证这一点，如图 31 所示。Base Main 套件——作为聚类过程的结果，base main 套件跟踪 6 个任务组：MCQA STEM、MCQA Non-STEM、Gen、Math、Code、Code FIM。与 OLMo 2 不同，我们在预训练时跟踪生成式数学与代码任务。我们选择以尽可能大的样本数评测 pass@k，使每个任务都能在 1 块 H100 上、30 分钟内完成对 OLMo 2 7B 的评测，以确保评测速度不被任何特定任务所瓶颈。对于 n 足够大的任务，我们设 k = 16 以匹配 GRPO 组大小，我们观察到这充当 RL 训练可能改进的经验上界。


Source: p.96 S228

Original: To decide on the temperature and top-p, we ran a sweep and evaluated 5 models (OLMo 2 7B, 13B; Qwen 2.5 7B, 13B; Qwen 3 8B) to find an adequate configuration setting for high scores on both pass@1 and pass@k. Results are shown in Figure 32, and we select temperature and top-p of 0.6 for all base math and code evaluation.

中文: 为确定 temperature 与 top-p，我们做了一次扫描，评测 5 个模型（OLMo 2 7B、13B；Qwen 2.5 7B、13B；Qwen 3 8B），以寻找在 pass@1 与 pass@k 上都取得高分的合适配置。结果见图 32，我们为所有基座数学与代码评测选择 temperature 0.6 与 top-p 0.6。


Source: p.96 S229

Original: Base Chat suite — During mid-training, we refashion the Chat eval suite (§4.1) for use evaluating base models. To do this, we used a standard, simple chat template (Question: {text}\nAnswer:) across all base models (both Olmo 3 and baseline models) and we included stop tokens to prevent degenerate responses. We also excluded tasks which required an API-based judge (AlpacaEval, SimpleQA) due to cost. In practice, we noticed most of the disagreements between the base main and base chat evaluation suites were due to noise, so we primarily used the base suite for making decisions.

中文: Base Chat 套件——在中期训练期间，我们改造 Chat 评测套件（§4.1）用于评测基座模型。为此，我们对所有基座模型（Olmo 3 与基线模型）使用标准、简单的聊天模板（Question: {text}\nAnswer:），并加入停止令牌以防退化响应。由于成本，我们还排除了需要基于 API 的评判器的任务（AlpacaEval、SimpleQA）。实践中，我们注意到 base main 与 base chat 评测套件之间的大多数分歧源于噪声，因此我们主要使用 base 套件做决策。


Source: p.97 S230

Original: Base Long-Context suite — During the long-context extension phase, we evaluate long-context capability using RULER (Hsieh et al., 2024) as our primary development signal. As a complementary held-out set, we also use HELMET (Yen et al., 2025), noting that the HELMET Recall task directly implements several RULER evaluations (specifically, ruler-niah-mk-2, ruler-niah-mk-3, and ruler-niah-mv). Because we evaluate only base models at this stage, we disable chat templates within HELMET to ensure consistent scoring across models. For HELMET tasks requiring an LLM-as-a-judge, we use its default judge configuration (gpt-4o-2024-05-13). Taken together, RULER guides most model-selection decisions during long-context development, with HELMET providing an additional check on generalization.

中文: Base Long-Context 套件——在长上下文扩展阶段，我们使用 RULER（Hsieh et al., 2024）作为主要开发信号评测长上下文能力。作为补充的留出集，我们还使用 HELMET（Yen et al., 2025），注意到 HELMET 的 Recall 任务直接实现了若干 RULER 评测（具体为 ruler-niah-mk-2、ruler-niah-mk-3 与 ruler-niah-mv）。由于此阶段我们只评测基座模型，我们在 HELMET 内禁用聊天模板以确保跨模型评分一致。对于需要 LLM-as-a-judge 的 HELMET 任务，我们使用其默认评判器配置（gpt-4o-2024-05-13）。综合来看，RULER 指导长上下文开发中的大多数模型选择决策，而 HELMET 提供泛化性的额外检查。


Source: p.98 S231

Original: Base Held-out Suite — We targeted one held-out evaluation task to match each family of capability: MMLU Pro for QA (Wang et al., 2024a), LBPP for code (Matton et al., 2024), Deepmind Math for math (Saxton et al., 2019), and BigBench Hard to measure broad coverage across unseen task types (Suzgun et al., 2022).

中文: Base Held-out 套件——我们为每个能力族对应一个留出评测任务：MMLU Pro 对应 QA（Wang et al., 2024a）、LBPP 对应代码（Matton et al., 2024）、Deepmind Math 对应数学（Saxton et al., 2019）、BigBench Hard 用于衡量对未见任务类型的广泛覆盖（Suzgun et al., 2022）。

A.4.2 New Evaluation Benchmarks

A.4.2 新评测基准


Source: p.98 S232

Original: Basic Skills — We developed a new benchmark, BasicSkills, to measure whether core capabilities are being acquired during pretraining. BasicSkills consists of 6 subtasks: basic arithmetic, string manipulation, simple coding, elementary logical reasoning, basic common sense, and simple pattern recognition. Each task isolates a single skill using a self-contained context that requires no external knowledge or additional information and can be completed through natural text continuation without relying on instruction-following abilities.

中文: Basic Skills——我们开发了一个新基准 BasicSkills，用于衡量预训练期间是否习得核心能力。BasicSkills 由 6 个子任务组成：基础算术、字符串操作、简单编码、基础逻辑推理、基础常识与简单模式识别。每个任务用一个自包含上下文隔离单一技能，无需外部知识或额外信息，可通过自然文本续写完成，不依赖指令遵循能力。


Source: p.98 S233

Original: Gen2MC — One takeaway from OLMo 2 development was a sensitivity to task format. The clustering procedure further confirmed this, finding that generative scores rank models similarly as rank choice (RC) QA tasks, disagreeing with ranking of single-token multiple choice (MC) QA tasks (see Figure 5). In particular, the short-form generative QA tasks (GenQA in Table 46) evaluate by comparing a generated answer to a bank of plausible answers, but these answer banks are often not complete, leading to false negatives. To address this, we introduce the Gen2MC benchmarks, which were constructed by taking the original question/answer pairs and generating incorrect multiple-choice distractor answers using a strong LLM. We create Gen2MC tasks for DROP, Jeopardy, NaturalQs, SQuAD, CoQA using GPT-4o for generating distractors, and fall-back to GPT-4.1 in cases where output parsing failed.

中文: Gen2MC——OLMo 2 开发的一个收获是对任务格式的敏感性。聚类过程进一步印证了这一点，发现生成式分数与 rank choice（RC）QA 任务对模型的排序相似，而与单令牌多项选择（MC）QA 任务的排序不一致（见图 5）。尤其是，短形式生成式 QA 任务（表 46 中的 GenQA）通过将生成答案与一个候选答案库比较来评测，但这些答案库往往不完整，导致假阴性。为解决这一点，我们引入 Gen2MC 基准，它通过取原始问题/答案对、并用强 LLM 生成错误的多项选择干扰项来构建。我们为 DROP、Jeopardy、NaturalQs、SQuAD、CoQA 创建 Gen2MC 任务，使用 GPT-4o 生成干扰项，在输出解析失败时回退到 GPT-4.1。


Source: p.98 S234

Original: Masked perplexity — We want our model to perform well on the diversity of requests from real user chat data; however, we don’t want to overfit to the “style” of chat outputs. To avoid this, we use a simple token masking strategy, inspired by work in loss masking (Mindermann et al., 2022): 1. Fine-tune a 1B model on a tiny subset of the dataset (~5%) with a small learning rate. The key idea is that we “warm up” to the format of the target set without learning a lot of new knowledge. 2. Compute the token losses of the base model and the fine-tuned model on every sequence in the dataset and compute the difference: log p_SFT(y|x) − log p_base(y|x). 3. Mask tokens where the difference is greater than some threshold (found by inspection). 4. Also mask the user responses and tool calls (we don’t want to model these for data selection). Use the loss at all the non-masked tokens positions for perplexity evaluations. In practice, we use OLMo 2 1B and the trained OLMo 2 1B SFT to compute the loss difference on target tokens. We use UltraChat and WildChat (Ding et al., 2023; Zhao et al., 2024a) as our masked perplexity sets.

中文: 掩码困惑度——我们希望模型在真实用户聊天数据的多样化请求上表现良好；然而，我们不想对聊天输出的「风格」过拟合。为避免这一点，我们使用一种受损失掩码工作（Mindermann et al., 2022）启发的简单令牌掩码策略：1. 在数据集的一个极小子集（约 5%）上，以较小学习率微调一个 1B 模型。核心思想是在不学大量新知识的情况下「热身」到目标集的格式。2. 在数据集中每个序列上计算基座模型与微调模型的令牌损失，并计算差值：log p_SFT(y|x) − log p_base(y|x)。3. 掩码差值大于某阈值（经检查确定）的令牌。4. 同时掩码用户响应与工具调用（数据选择时不想建模这些）。用所有非掩码令牌位置的损失做困惑度评测。实践中，我们使用 OLMo 2 1B 与训练好的 OLMo 2 1B SFT 计算目标令牌上的损失差值。我们使用 UltraChat 与 WildChat（Ding et al., 2023; Zhao et al., 2024a）作为掩码困惑度集合。

A.5 Base Model Additional Decontamination Details

A.5 基座模型附加去污染细节


Source: p.101 S235

Original: Important: this section is adapted from the documentation of the decon package; for up to date information, please consult the official documentation. Evals provide measurable outcomes for model capabilities. We hope that these are meaningful measurements. When evals leak into training data we run the risk of overfitting on evals. A.5.1 Definitions and Preliminaries — Training data and evals both consist of variable length token sequences. Contamination is a sufficient presence of a given eval sequence e in a given training sequence t. We characterize the problem as an approximate substring search for e in t for all e ∈ E, t ∈ T. Our goal is to partition the set T × E into the set of contaminated documents, denoted as C, and the set of pure documents, denoted as P. Our goal is to call whether any training sequence t is derived directly from an eval sequence e.

中文: 重要：本节改编自 decon 包的文档；最新信息请查阅官方文档。评测为模型能力提供可测量的结果。我们希望这些是有意义的测量。当评测泄漏进训练数据时，我们就有在评测上过拟合的风险。A.5.1 定义与预备——训练数据与评测都由变长令牌序列组成。污染是指给定评测序列 e 充分存在于给定训练序列 t 中。我们将此问题刻画为对所有 e ∈ E、t ∈ T 的、在 t 中近似搜索 e 的子串。我们的目标是将集合 T × E 划分为污染文档集 C 与纯净文档集 P。我们的目标是判断任何训练序列 t 是否直接衍生自评测序列 e。


Source: p.102 S236

Original: A.5.3 Eval Normalization — decon normalizes all eval instances into question (Q), answer (A), and passage (P) components. An eval instance can be described as having a Q, QA, QP, or QAP composition. Question — All eval instances to be decontaminated contain a question, and it serves as the primary vessel for information to describe the task. decon uses the question field for initial identification of contamination clusters. Questions with substantial information content and a strong match are sufficient to call contamination. Answer — While the answer of an eval is important for measuring whether a model has learned a specific task, in the context of decontamination the answer primarily serves to provide supporting evidence of contamination. Passage — The passage, often derived from reference documents, is not a strong indicator of contamination, but in conjunction with a substantial question and answer match, further supports a contamination call.

中文: A.5.3 评测归一化——decon 将所有评测实例归一化为问题（Q）、答案（A）与篇章（P）三个成分。一个评测实例可被描述为具有 Q、QA、QP 或 QAP 组合。问题——所有要去污染的评测实例都包含问题，它是描述任务的主要信息载体。decon 使用问题字段初步识别污染簇。信息量大且强匹配的问题足以判定污染。答案——虽然评测的答案对衡量模型是否学会特定任务很重要，但在去污染语境下，答案主要提供污染的支持性证据。篇章——篇章常衍生自参考文档，不是污染的强指标，但与大量的问题与答案匹配结合时，会进一步支持污染判定。


Source: p.102 S237

Original: A.5.4 Decon Implementation — We can now describe a computationally tractable definition of contamination. Cluster discovery — We start by defining a contamination cluster as a substring of a training document and a set of candidate evals which have at least 1 matching ngram. We discover clusters by sequentially sampling training document ngrams and checking for a hit in an inverted index which resolves ngrams to eval document ids. Upon an initial hit we expand left and right from the initial hit index until we observe a certain number misses, representing inserts, deletions, or edits. The initial hit produces a set of matching document ids, which we call the active set. We use the intersection between the active set and the step set to identify which documents in the active set hit for a given step. Once a specific document reaches 11 misses, it is removed from the active set. We repeat this process until the active set is empty or we reach the training document boundaries.

中文: A.5.4 Decon 实现——我们现在可以描述一个计算上可行的污染定义。簇发现——我们首先将污染簇定义为一个训练文档的子串与一组至少含 1 个匹配 ngram 的候选评测。我们通过顺序采样训练文档的 ngram、并在将 ngram 解析到评测文档 id 的倒排索引中检查命中来发现簇。在初始命中后，我们从初始命中索引向左、右扩展，直到观察到一定数量的未命中（代表插入、删除或编辑）。初始命中产生一组匹配文档 id，我们称之为活跃集（active set）。我们使用活跃集与步集（step set）的交集来确定活跃集中哪些文档在给定步命中。一旦某个文档达到 11 次未命中，就将其移出活跃集。我们重复此过程，直到活跃集为空或到达训练文档边界。


Source: p.103 S238

Original: IDF-weighted overlap — Contamination scoring uses inverse document frequency (IDF) weighting: O = (Σ_{x∈Ut∩Ue} idf(x)) / (Σ_{y∈Ue} idf(y)), where Ut is the set of unique n-grams in the training document segment and Ue is the set of unique n-grams in the evaluation document. Cluster match length decay — Less informative short texts require stronger matches. O′ = O × 1 (if L ≤ L_start), O × (1 − 0.2 · (L−L_start)/(L_end−L_start)) (if L_start < L < L_end), O × 0.8 (if L ≥ L_end). By default L_start is 20 and L_end is 50.

中文: IDF 加权重叠——污染评分使用逆文档频率（IDF）加权：O = (Σ_{x∈Ut∩Ue} idf(x)) / (Σ_{y∈Ue} idf(y))，其中 Ut 是训练文档片段中的唯一 n-gram 集合，Ue 是评测文档中的唯一 n-gram 集合。簇匹配长度衰减——信息量较少的短文本需要更强的匹配。O′ = O × 1（若 L ≤ L_start）；O′ = O × (1 − 0.2 · (L−L_start)/(L_end−L_start))（若 L_start < L < L_end）；O′ = O × 0.8（若 L ≥ L_end）。默认 L_start 为 20、L_end 为 50。


Source: p.104 S239

Original: A.5.5 Scoring System — Scores combine question, answer, and passage overlaps with adaptive weights based on the length of components: QAP (all components): 0.7 question, 0.2 answer, 0.1 passage; QA (no passage): 0.75 question, 0.25 answer; QP (no answer): 0.85 question, 0.15 passage; Q (question only): 1.0 question. Length penalty — We penalize short matches based on the length of Q+A+P by scaling down scores for shorter texts, making the contamination threshold effectively harder to reach. Perfect scores (1.0) are never penalized. Confidence adjusted weight — The question component is the core of a contaminated prompt and carries the most weight. Because longer sequences with more informative content provide stronger contamination evidence, we adjust component weights based on confidence factors derived from length by reducing the question weight and redistributing it to the answer or passage.

中文: A.5.5 评分系统——分数结合问题、答案与篇章重叠，并按成分长度做自适应加权：QAP（全部成分）：0.7 问题、0.2 答案、0.1 篇章；QA（无篇章）：0.75 问题、0.25 答案；QP（无答案）：0.85 问题、0.15 篇章；Q（仅问题）：1.0 问题。长度惩罚——我们按 Q+A+P 的长度惩罚短匹配，对较短文本下调分数，使污染阈值实际上更难达到。满分（1.0）永不惩罚。置信度调整权重——问题成分是被污染提示词的核心，权重最大。由于更长、信息量更多的序列提供更强的污染证据，我们按从长度导出的置信度因子调整成分权重：降低问题权重，并将其重分配到答案或篇章。

A.6 Post-Training Additional Training Details

A.6 后训练附加训练细节

A.6.1 Supervised Finetuning Details

A.6.1 监督微调细节


Source: p.105 S240

Original: Using OLMo-core infrastructure for SFT Training — Relative to pretraining, this involves a substantially smaller batch size, different data packing, and masking. This leads to an 8x faster training speed than open-instruct, dramatically improving our iteration speed. We use between 1 and 8 8xH100 nodes, or 1 to 4 8xB200 nodes to train our 7B reasoner and instruct models. We use 32 8xH100 nodes to train our 32B thinking model. As a consequence of using olmo-core, our batch size is now measured in tokens instead of instances, and we train with document packing instead of padding. We train all of our 7B SFT models with a batch size of 1M tokens and 32B SFT models with a batch size of 4M tokens, for two epochs, with packing, and a 32,768 sequence length. Our hyperparameter settings are also summarized in Table 47.

中文: 使用 OLMo-core 基础设施做 SFT 训练——相对预训练，这涉及小得多的批量大小、不同的数据打包与掩码。这带来比 open-instruct 快 8 倍的训练速度，显著提高了我们的迭代速度。我们使用 1 到 8 个 8xH100 节点、或 1 到 4 个 8xB200 节点训练 7B 推理与指令模型。我们使用 32 个 8xH100 节点训练 32B 思考模型。使用 olmo-core 的一个结果是，我们的批量大小现在以令牌而非实例计，并且我们用文档打包而非填充训练。我们用 1M 令牌的批量大小训练所有 7B SFT 模型、用 4M 令牌训练 32B SFT 模型，两个 epoch、带打包、32,768 序列长度。超参数设置见表 47。

A.6.2 Preference Tuning Details

A.6.2 偏好调优细节


Source: p.105 S241

Original: Training Settings — Given a preference dataset D = {(x, yc, yr)} of prompts x and corresponding chosen and rejected responses yc ≻ yr, we optimize the model policy πθ on a length-normalized DPO loss (Lambert et al., 2024):

中文: 训练设置——给定一个由提示词 x 及对应被选择与被拒绝响应 yc ≻ yr 组成的偏好数据集 D = {(x, yc, yr)}，我们在长度归一化的 DPO 损失（Lambert et al., 2024）上优化模型策略 πθ：


Source: p.105 E003 · Eq. (DPO loss) · medium confidence

低置信度转写（请以原图为准）/ Low-confidence transcription:

$$
\mathcal{L}{\mathrm{DPO}}(\theta)=\mathbb{E}{(x,y_c,y_r)\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\log\sigma\left(\frac{\beta}{|y_c|}\log\frac{\pi_\theta(y_c\mid x)}{\pi_{\mathrm{ref}}(y_c\mid x)}-\frac{\beta}{|y_r|}\log\frac{\pi_\theta(y_r\mid x)}{\pi_{\mathrm{ref}}(y_r\mid x)}\right)\right]
$$

中文说明： 式中 $\pi_{\mathrm{ref}}$ 为初始参考策略，$\beta$ 是正则化学习、在参考策略与训练策略之间施加隐式 KL 散度惩罚的超参数。符号保持原样。


Source: p.105 S242

Original: where πref is the initial reference policy and β is a hyperparameter that regularizes learning via an implicit Kullback–Leibler (KL) divergence penalty between the reference policy and the training policy. We sweep learning rate and preference dataset size, as we observe that performance increases up until some task-dependent optimal optimization point beyond which further tuning hurts (Figure 23). All other hyperparameters are kept fixed. See Table 48 for exact hyperparameters. We train our 7B models using 2–4 8xH100 nodes, and our 32B models with 8–16 8xH100 nodes.

中文: 其中 πref 是初始参考策略，β 是一个超参数，通过参考策略与训练策略之间的隐式 Kullback–Leibler（KL）散度惩罚来正则化学习。我们扫描学习率与偏好数据集大小，因为我们观察到性能增长到某个依赖任务的最优优化点为止，超过之后继续调优会损害性能（图 23）。所有其他超参数保持不变。精确超参数见表 48。我们用 2–4 个 8xH100 节点训练 7B 模型，用 8–16 个 8xH100 节点训练 32B 模型。

中文表注: 表 48 —— Olmo 3 Think DPO 与 Olmo 3 Instruct DPO 的训练超参数。

超参数 7B Thinking DPO 32B Thinking DPO 7B Instruct DPO
Num. Preference Pairs 150K 200K 260K
Num. Epochs 1 1 1

A.6.4 RL-Zero Details

A.6.4 RL-Zero 细节


Source: p.106 S244

Original: We detail the prompt used for math in Figure 37. Prompts of other domains are quite similar. We also compare Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B to one of the more common benchmarks in RLVR, DAPO (Yu et al., 2025) in Figure 38. Olmo 3 RL-Zero achieves reasonable performance faster and is also much more compute efficient, making it better for experimentation. Finally, we compare Olmo RL-Zero 3.1 to the initially released RL-Zero 3.0 in Figure 39 and see a sizable improvement. There were some minor fixes to loss calculation but the major improvement comes from 1. setting completion length to 16k instead of 12k and 2. not masking truncated sequences, one of the components of DAPO (Yu et al., 2025).

中文: 图 37 详述了用于数学的提示词。其他领域的提示词非常相似。我们还在图 38 中将 Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B 与 RLVR 中较常见的基准之一 DAPO（Yu et al., 2025）对比。Olmo 3 RL-Zero 更快达到合理性能，且算力效率高得多，更适合实验。最后，我们在图 39 中将 Olmo RL-Zero 3.1 与最初发布的 RL-Zero 3.0 对比，看到显著改进。损失计算有一些小修复，但主要改进来自：1. 将补全长度从 12k 设为 16k；2. 不掩码截断序列（DAPO（Yu et al., 2025）的组成部分之一）。


Source: p.106 S245

Original: Despite initial results suggesting this masking improved the speed of the trainer (by having fewer completions to train on), we ultimately found that variations in batch size caused by some examples masked out to reduce stability. And without training on overlong negative sequences, completion lengths were higher, on average. We therefore found that any efficiency gains in training speed from masking were outweighed by slowdowns from generating longer sequence lengths.

中文: 尽管最初结果表明这种掩码（因需训练的补全更少）提高了训练器速度，但我们最终发现，某些示例被掩掉导致的批量大小变化降低了稳定性。且若不训练过长的负序列，补全长度平均更高。因此我们发现，掩码带来的训练速度效率收益，被生成更长序列长度带来的减速所抵消。

Original caption: Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B vs DAPO (Yu et al., 2025) which leverages Qwen 2.5 32B. We compare the two benchmarks in terms of increase in model performance over training steps as well as GPU hours.

中文图注: Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B 与利用 Qwen 2.5 32B 的 DAPO（Yu et al., 2025）的对比。我们从「性能随训练步数提升」与「GPU 时数」两方面比较两个基准。

Reading note: 左图按梯度步数、右图按 GPU 时数；Olmo 3 RL-Zero 7B 以更少算力更快达到相近的 AIME pass@1。

Fig. 39. Olmo 3 RL-Zero 与 Olmo 3.1 RL-Zero 的对比

中文图注: Olmo 3 RL-Zero 与 Olmo 3.1 RL-Zero 的对比。我们在 AIME 2024 和 2025 的 pass@1 上，将新基线与之前发布的 Olmo 3 RL-Zero Math 对比。新设置起初改进较慢，但训练更久后表现更优，最终在约 50% 的更高分数处趋于平稳。

A.7 Post-Training Additional Data Details

A.7 后训练附加数据细节

A.7.1 Filtering for Dolci Think-SFT

A.7.1 Dolci Think-SFT 的过滤


Source: p.107 S246

Original: In this section we detail the filtering methods created primarily for training Olmo 3 Think, which was also used for mid-training and Olmo 3 Instruct data. Each phase of filtering would remove 0-1% of data across most available or generated reasoning traces. 1. Source filtering — We perform some filtering to remove non-compliant licenses or data that will not be useful. For OpenThoughts2, we removed ShareGPT prompts due to questionable provenance (as done in Tulu 3). For LlamaNemotron Post-Training we filter to only reasoning samples from DeepSeek and Qwen that have not been touched by Llama models. 2. Format filtering — We remove truncated answers (i.e. if they have and no ) and empty outputs.

中文: 本节详述主要为训练 Olmo 3 Think 而创建的过滤方法，这些方法也用于中期训练与 Olmo 3 Instruct 数据。每个过滤阶段会在大多数可用或生成的思维轨迹中移除 0–1% 的数据。1. 来源过滤——我们做了一些过滤以移除非合规许可或无用的数据。对于 OpenThoughts2，我们因来源存疑移除了 ShareGPT 提示词（如 Tulu 3 所做）。对于 LlamaNemotron Post-Training，我们只保留未被 Llama 模型处理的 DeepSeek 与 Qwen 推理样本。2. 格式过滤——我们移除截断答案（即有 而无 ）与空输出。


Source: p.107 S247

Original: 3. Domain specific accuracy filtering — We check accuracy for many domains, such as precise instruction following, code, or math. Additionally, for chat domains we use included metadata in some datasets such as Wildchat to remove responses or prompts tagged as unsafe. 4. General content filters — Here we remove mention of date cutoffs to try and avoid hallucinations of model characteristics and any mention in the user prompt or completion that indicates the date is to or from any model. 5. Repetition filtering — Many open-weights reasoning models have tendencies to perform extreme repetitions, even in thinking traces that result in a correct answer. We filter this roughly by searching for heavily repeated (10x+) sentences, paragraphs, or (50x+) phrases. 6. Chinese language filtering — In order to encourage Olmo 3 Think to stay in its intended language of English, we remove any post-training responses with 5% or higher prevalence of Chinese characters.

中文: 3. 领域特定准确率过滤——我们对许多领域检查准确率，如精确指令遵循、代码或数学。此外，对于对话领域，我们使用某些数据集（如 Wildchat）中自带的元数据移除被标记为不安全的响应或提示词。4. 通用内容过滤——这里我们移除日期截断的提及，以避免对模型特征的幻觉、以及用户提示词或补全中表明日期来自或指向任何模型的任何提及。5. 重复过滤——许多开放权重推理模型有极端重复的倾向，即便在得到正确答案的思维轨迹中也是如此。我们通过搜索大量重复（10 次以上）的句子、段落或（50 次以上）的短语来粗略过滤。6. 中文语言过滤——为鼓励 Olmo 3 Think 保持其预期的英语语言，我们移除中文字符占比 5% 及以上的任何后训练响应。

A.7.2 Tool-use data

A.7.2 工具使用数据


Source: p.108 S248

Original: Additional details about the Science QA dataset — Citation graph-based queries are produced by prompting GPT-5 in a few-shot setup to create query templates (e.g., “What are the top-three most cited papers by {AUTHOR} on {TOPIC}?”) which are subsequently instantiated with real paper entities. Content-based questions are generated by a GPT-5-based agent equipped with the ASC server, which retrieves relevant papers and formulates grounded questions that can be answered using retrieved text. For both types of queries, to obtain corresponding tool-use trajectories we employ a GPT-4.1-mini agent with access to the same ASC server. All tool call outputs are derived from actual environment responses rather than synthetic completions.

中文: Science QA 数据集的更多细节——基于引文图的查询通过以 few-shot 方式提示 GPT-5 创建查询模板（例如「{AUTHOR} 在 {TOPIC} 上被引用最多的三篇论文是什么？」）来生成，这些模板随后用真实论文实体实例化。基于内容的问题由一个配备 ASC 服务器的 GPT-5 agent 生成，它检索相关论文并制定能用检索文本回答的、有据可依的问题。对于两类查询，为获得相应工具使用轨迹，我们使用一个可访问同一 ASC 服务器的 GPT-4.1-mini agent。所有工具调用输出都来自真实环境响应，而非合成补全。


Source: p.109 S249

Original: Additional details about the Web Search QA dataset — Given the varied quality of real-world queries, GPT-5 is employed to rate each query drawn from existing open-access benchmarks on a five-point scale assessing (i) whether it calls for comprehensive long-form responses, (ii) factual verifiability, and (iii) the degree of search required. Only queries scoring 4 or 5 on these criteria are retained. We then use an agent equipped with web search and browsing via the Serper API, and scientific snippet retrieval via ASC to generate tool-use trajectories for these queries. This agent is instructed with tool specifications and step-by-step search instructions, resulting in detailed trajectories containing both tool calls and environment outputs.

中文: Web Search QA 数据集的更多细节——鉴于现实世界查询质量参差不齐，使用 GPT-5 在五分量表上对取自现有开放访问基准的每个查询打分，评估（i）是否要求全面的长形式响应、（ii）事实可验证性、（iii）所需搜索程度。仅保留这些准则上得 4 或 5 分的查询。随后我们使用一个配备 Serper API 网页搜索与浏览、以及 ASC 科学片段检索的 agent，为这些查询生成工具使用轨迹。该 agent 被给予工具规范与逐步搜索说明，从而生成既含工具调用、也含环境输出的详细轨迹。


Source: p.109 S250

Original: Additional details about simulated interaction trajectories — We run various post-hoc checks on synthesized datasets to verify whether the generated trajectories adhere to the prompts, and filter the dataset to create SimFC. We filter out trajectories where the function calls include functions not part of the presented APIs. Our data-synthesis prompts explicitly target multi-turn, multi-step, parallel function calls (i.e., multiple calls per assistant turn) and refusals, and we filter out the trajectories that do not conform to such requirements specified in the prompts.

中文: 模拟交互轨迹的更多细节——我们对合成数据集运行各种事后检查，以验证生成轨迹是否符合提示词，并过滤数据集创建 SimFC。我们过滤掉函数调用包含不属于所呈现 API 的函数的轨迹。我们的数据合成提示词明确针对多轮、多步、并行函数调用（即每个助手轮次多次调用）与拒绝，并过滤掉不符合提示词中这些要求的轨迹。

中文图注: 生成带模拟环境反馈的多轮函数调用交互的示意提示词（提示词为可读性已截断）。

A.7.3 Coding Data Synthesis Pipeline

A.7.3 代码数据合成流水线


Source: p.109 S251

Original: To construct reinforcement learning (RL) data for code, we required pairs of (problem, test cases). We curate a diverse set of prompts for coding problems, including AceCoder, Klear-Reasoner Code, Nemotron Post-training Code, SYNTHETIC-2 code, Open-Code Reasoner. We use the klear-reasoner and SYNTHETIC-2 test cases directly. For the other datasets, we run prompts through the following synthetic data pipeline: Problem rewriting — Given a coding problem, we first prompted GPT-4.1 to rewrite the description so that it either (a) included a function signature, or (b) explicitly specified that the solution should read from and write to standard input/output (stdio). Solution generation — GPT-4.1 was then prompted to provide a corresponding solution. Test case generation — GPT-4.1 was further prompted to generate test cases in the appropriate format (function-based or stdio-based).

中文: 为构建代码的 RL 数据，我们需要（问题，测试用例）对。我们整理多样化的编码问题提示词集合，包括 AceCoder、Klear-Reasoner Code、Nemotron Post-training Code、SYNTHETIC-2 code、Open-Code Reasoner。我们直接使用 klear-reasoner 与 SYNTHETIC-2 的测试用例。对于其他数据集，我们将提示词经过如下合成数据流水线：问题重写——给定一个编码问题，我们先提示 GPT-4.1 重写描述，使其（a）包含函数签名，或（b）明确说明解答应从标准输入读、向标准输出写（stdio）。解答生成——随后提示 GPT-4.1 提供相应解答。测试用例生成——再提示 GPT-4.1 以合适格式（基于函数或基于 stdio）生成测试用例。

Fig. 40. 非可验证任务的 LLM 评判器提示词

Placed near: p.108 S252
Source: p.108 C057

Original caption: LLM judge prompt for non-verifiable tasks.

中文图注: 非可验证任务的 LLM 评判器提示词。

Reading note: 该图是 Olmo 3 用于非可验证任务（LM-as-a-judge 奖励）的评判提示词模板，要求以 JSON 格式输出 REASONING 与 SCORE（1–10）。

A.7.4 Dolci Instruct DPO Details

A.7.4 Dolci Instruct DPO 细节


Source: p.111 S252

Original: Model pool for LLM-judged pairs — To create the GPT-judged subset of Dolci Instruct DPO, we generate completions on our prompt pool with the following models: gpt-oss-20B, gpt-oss-120B, GPT-4.1, Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct, OLMo 2-1B/7B/13B/32B-Instruct, Phi4-Mini-Instruct, Gemma3-4B/12B/27B-it, Qwen3-Coder-30B-3A (no reasoning), Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B/8B/14B/32B (no reasoning), Qwen3-30B-3A (no reasoning), QwQ-32b, Yi-9B, and Yi-34B. For each prompt, we sample four model completions and judge them via a GPT-4.1 judge with the UltraFeedback judge prompts. To enforce a meaningful delta between chosen and rejected responses, we enforce our judge pipeline to sample responses from exactly two of the following smaller and/or previous generation models which show lower overall performance: OLMo 2-1B-Instruct, OLMo 2-7B-Instruct, Yi-9B, Yi-34B, Phi4-Mini-Instruct, Qwen3-0.6B (no reasoning), Qwen3-1.7B (no reasoning). We binarize into preference pairs by selecting the worst response out of the four to be rejected, and the best as chosen.

中文: LLM 评判配对的模型池——为创建 Dolci Instruct DPO 的 GPT 评判子集，我们用以下模型在提示词池上生成补全：gpt-oss-20B、gpt-oss-120B、GPT-4.1、Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct、OLMo 2-1B/7B/13B/32B-Instruct、Phi4-Mini-Instruct、Gemma3-4B/12B/27B-it、Qwen3-Coder-30B-3A（无推理）、Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B/8B/14B/32B（无推理）、Qwen3-30B-3A（无推理）、QwQ-32b、Yi-9B 与 Yi-34B。对每个提示词，我们采样四个模型补全，并用带 UltraFeedback 评判提示词的 GPT-4.1 评判器评判。为强制被选择与被拒绝响应之间有意义的差距，我们强制评判流水线恰好从以下表现较低的较小/上一代模型中的两个采样响应：OLMo 2-1B-Instruct、OLMo 2-7B-Instruct、Yi-9B、Yi-34B、Phi4-Mini-Instruct、Qwen3-0.6B（无推理）、Qwen3-1.7B（无推理）。我们通过选择四个中最差响应为被拒绝、最佳响应为被选择，二值化为偏好对。

A.8 Post-Training Additional Evaluation Details

A.8 后训练附加评测细节

A.8.1 General Evaluation Settings

A.8.1 通用评测设置


Source: p.114 S253

Original: For post-training, we focus exclusively on generative evaluations, in which we generate completions until a max length is reached or eos token is generated (as opposed to multiple-choice-based evaluations used in pretraining), better matching real-world downstream usage. Following DeepSeek R1 report (Guo et al., 2025) and Nvidia Nemotron (Adler et al., 2024) we use a sampling temperature of 0.6 and top-p of 0.95. We strip thinking traces from the answer text when generated. We account for the variance this induces in smaller benchmarks (e.g. AIME, which is made up of 30 questions) by taking multiple samples and reporting the overall average performance. For QA tasks (e.g. BBH, MMLU), we create a unified set of “Olmo 3” regexes for answer extraction, covering a wide variety of potential answer templates. We additionally update AlpacaEval 2 Length Controlled (LC) (Dubois et al., 2024) to use GPT-4.1 as a judge instead of the original GPT-4-Turbo both to increase the reliability of the evaluation and to save ~90% of inference costs. Importantly, our evaluation settings are unified across thinker and instruct models, simplifying our evaluation development process.

中文: 对于后训练，我们只关注生成式评测，即生成补全直到达到最大长度或生成 eos 令牌（而非预训练中使用的多项选择评测），更好地匹配现实世界下游使用。遵循 DeepSeek R1 报告（Guo et al., 2025）与 Nvidia Nemotron（Adler et al., 2024），我们使用 0.6 的采样温度与 0.95 的 top-p。生成时我们从答案文本中剥离思维轨迹。我们对较小基准（如由 30 道题组成的 AIME）上由此引入的方差，通过多次采样并报告总体平均性能来应对。对于 QA 任务（如 BBH、MMLU），我们创建一组统一的「Olmo 3」正则表达式用于答案提取，覆盖各种潜在答案模板。我们还将 AlpacaEval 2 Length Controlled（LC）（Dubois et al., 2024）更新为用 GPT-4.1 而非原始 GPT-4-Turbo 做评判器，既提高了评测可靠性，又节省约 90% 的推理成本。重要的是，我们的评测设置在思考与指令模型之间统一，简化了评测开发过程。


Source: p.114 S254

Original: Is AlpacaEval useful? — Certainly! AlpacaEval, and similar evaluations, such as ChatBotArena, MT-Bench, Arena-Hard, etc. are established as crucial benchmarks for the AI industry. It’s not a broken evaluation, it’s a trade-off. It’s well established that most people enjoy reading language model completions that have a bit of flair to them. Pro: Ease-of-reading and flair; Con: Over-optimized style. There aren’t many better options! There are just so few evaluations that test a model’s ability to chat with the users reliably. Pro: Common adoption; Con: Low diversity in chat evaluations. In summary, we need evaluations like this to make sure the model is behaving as expected.

中文: AlpacaEval 有用吗？——当然！AlpacaEval 及类似评测（如 ChatBotArena、MT-Bench、Arena-Hard 等）已被确立为 AI 行业的关键基准。它不是坏掉的评测，而是一种权衡。众所周知，大多数人喜欢阅读略带风格的语言模型补全。优点：易读、有风格；缺点：风格被过度优化。没有太多更好的选择！测试模型能否可靠与用户聊天的评测实在太少了。优点：普遍采用；缺点：对话评测多样性低。总之，我们需要这类评测来确保模型行为符合预期。

A.8.2 Safety Evaluations Overview

A.8.2 安全评测概览


Source: p.114 S255

Original: The safety evaluations that were tested upon during training runs and whose average was reported earlier were the same set from OLMo 2 (OLMo et al., 2024) and Tülu 3 (Lambert et al., 2024). In addition to the development safety evaluations, we also evaluate our models on four new safety evaluations, chosen due to their prevalence in recent LLM safety evaluations. Development safety evaluations — We include HarmBench (Mazeika et al., 2024), DoAnythingNow (DAN; Shen et al., 2024), XSTest (Röttger et al., 2023), WildGuard-Test (Han et al., 2024), WildJailbreak-Test (Jiang et al., 2024), and TrustLLM-JailbreakTrigger (Huang et al., 2024a). Unseen safety evaluations — We further evaluated on four held-out safety benchmarks: Toxigen (Hartvigsen et al., 2022), StrongReject (Souly et al., 2024), Weapons of Mass Destruction Proxy (WMDP; Li et al., 2024b), and Bias Benchmark for QA (BBQ; Parrish et al., 2022).

中文: 训练期间测试并此前报告平均值的开发安全评测，与 OLMo 2（OLMo et al., 2024）和 Tülu 3（Lambert et al., 2024）所用集合相同。除开发安全评测外，我们还在四个新安全评测上评测模型，这些评测因在近期 LLM 安全评测中的普遍性而入选。开发安全评测——我们纳入 HarmBench（Mazeika et al., 2024）、DoAnythingNow（DAN; Shen et al., 2024）、XSTest（Röttger et al., 2023）、WildGuard-Test（Han et al., 2024）、WildJailbreak-Test（Jiang et al., 2024）与 TrustLLM-JailbreakTrigger（Huang et al., 2024a）。未见安全评测——我们还在四个留出安全基准上评测：Toxigen（Hartvigsen et al., 2022）、StrongReject（Souly et al., 2024）、大规模杀伤性武器代理（WMDP; Li et al., 2024b）与 QA 偏差基准（BBQ; Parrish et al., 2022）。


Source: p.115 S256

Original: Averaging and reported metrics — Safety and accuracy scores are aggregated according to benchmark protocol, with all reported metrics normalized such that higher values are better (1 indicates perfect safety performance). Specifically, we report the average of: refusal accuracy, i.e., inverted ASR (Attack Success Rate), for DoAnythingNow, Harmbench, Wildguard, TrustLLM-JailbreakTrigger, Toxigen, and StrongReject; accuracy for XSTest and BBQ; the average of inverted ASR for Wildjailbreak (harmful) and ASR for Wildjailbreak (benign); and inverted accuracy (i.e., error rate) for WMDP. For the safety benchmarks, models were evaluated with a top-p of 0.95 and sampling temperature of 0.7.

中文: 平均与报告指标——安全与准确率分数按基准协议聚合，所有报告指标都归一化为越高越好（1 表示完美安全性能）。具体而言，我们报告以下平均值：DoAnythingNow、Harmbench、Wildguard、TrustLLM-JailbreakTrigger、Toxigen 与 StrongReject 的拒绝准确率（即反转 ASR——攻击成功率）；XSTest 与 BBQ 的准确率；Wildjailbreak（有害）反转 ASR 与 Wildjailbreak（良性）ASR 的平均；以及 WMDP 的反转准确率（即错误率）。对安全基准，模型以 0.95 的 top-p 与 0.7 的采样温度评测。

Table 55. Olmo 3.1 32B Instruct 安全基准结果与对比

Placed near: p.117 S256
Source: p.117 C054

Original caption: Olmo 3.1 32B Instruct and comparisons on the safety benchmarks. All numbers are the mean of three runs.

中文表注: Olmo 3.1 32B Instruct 在安全基准上的结果与对比。所有数字均为三次运行的平均值。


Source: p.115 S257

Original: We explain all of the evaluations in more detail below: HarmBench (Mazeika et al., 2024) evaluates models’ refusal to comply with a diverse suite of harmful prompts, distributed across both functional and semantic categories. The benchmark contains 320 harmful prompts, covering functional behaviors including “standard” harms, prompts testing for copyright violations, and contextual prompts. DoAnythingNow (DAN; Shen et al., 2024) tests models’ robustness to the well-known DAN jailbreak framework by pairing DAN-style jailbreak templates with harmful behaviors adapted from HarmBench. XSTest (Röttger et al., 2023) measures models’ over-refusal tendencies, i.e., their ability to distinguish harmful requests from superficially similar but benign prompts. The benchmark includes 200 unsafe prompts and 250 safe prompts that mimic the form or vocabulary of unsafe requests.

中文: 下面我们更详细地解释所有评测：HarmBench（Mazeika et al., 2024）评测模型对一组多样化有害提示词的拒绝遵守情况，分布跨功能与语义两类。该基准含 320 个有害提示词，覆盖功能行为，包括「标准」危害、测试版权侵犯的提示词与上下文提示词。DoAnythingNow（DAN; Shen et al., 2024）通过将 DAN 式越狱模板与改编自 HarmBench 的有害行为配对，测试模型对知名 DAN 越狱框架的鲁棒性。XSTest（Röttger et al., 2023）衡量模型的过度拒绝倾向，即区分有害请求与表面相似但良性提示词的能力。该基准含 200 个不安全提示词与 250 个模仿不安全请求形式或词汇的安全提示词。


Source: p.116 S258

Original: WildGuard-Test (Han et al., 2024) provides a comprehensive evaluation of prompt harm, response harm, and response refusal across a set of 1,725 items. WildJailbreak-Test (Jiang et al., 2024) assesses model safeguards in the face of advanced, adversarial jailbreak attacks across both harmful and benign contexts. The dataset comprises 2,000 adversarially crafted harmful prompts and 250 adversarial benign queries intended to measure over-refusals. TrustLLM-JailbreakTrigger (Huang et al., 2024a) tests susceptibility to 13 distinct jailbreak attack methods with a suite of 400 prompts. Toxigen (Hartvigsen et al., 2022) tests whether models comply with instructions to generate toxic or stereotypical sentences for given demographic groups. StrongReject (Souly et al., 2024) assesses models’ susceptibility to advanced jailbreak prompts intended to elicit forbidden content, using one of 37 jailbreak techniques.

中文: WildGuard-Test（Han et al., 2024）在 1725 个条目上对提示词危害、响应危害与响应拒绝提供全面评测。WildJailbreak-Test（Jiang et al., 2024）在有害与良性两种语境下评估模型面对高级、对抗性越狱攻击的防护。该数据集含 2000 个对抗性构造的有害提示词与 250 个用于衡量过度拒绝的对抗性良性查询。TrustLLM-JailbreakTrigger（Huang et al., 2024a）用 400 个提示词的套件测试对 13 种不同越狱攻击方法的易感性。Toxigen（Hartvigsen et al., 2022）测试模型是否遵守为给定人口群体生成有毒或刻板句子的指令。StrongReject（Souly et al., 2024）评估模型对旨在引出违禁内容的高级越狱提示词的易感性，使用 37 种越狱技术之一。


Source: p.117 S259

Original: Weapons of Mass Destruction Proxy (WMDP) (Li et al., 2024b) evaluates whether models can provide answers to dual-use knowledge questions in the domains of biology, chemistry, and cybersecurity. Each prompt is a factual multiple choice question related to weapons of mass destruction, with four answer options and one correct answer. Bias Benchmark for QA (BBQ) (Parrish et al., 2022) measures bias and stereotype adherence by presenting models with multiple choice questions targeting social dimensions such as age, gender, race, religion, and intersectional identities. Model responses are evaluated for accuracy (proportion of correct answers) and for bias, using a regex-based string parser.

中文: 大规模杀伤性武器代理（WMDP）（Li et al., 2024b）评估模型能否回答生物、化学与网络安全领域的双用途知识问题。每个提示词都是与大规模杀伤性武器相关的事实性多项选择题，有四个答案选项与一个正确答案。QA 偏差基准（BBQ）（Parrish et al., 2022）通过向模型呈现针对年龄、性别、种族、宗教与交叉身份等社会维度的多项选择题，衡量偏差与刻板印象遵循。模型响应使用基于正则的字符串解析器，评测准确率（正确答案比例）与偏差。

阅读提示 / Critical Reading Notes

以下为本文的「论证骨架」导读，帮助快速把握全文结构；不能替代上方逐段中英对照。

  • 体裁：这是技术报告 / 模型卡（research technical report），IMRaD 结构 + 大规模附录。与 Nature 类研究论文不同，它的「贡献」不是单一发现，而是一整套可复现的训练配方（数据 + 代码 + 检查点全开放）。
  • 核心论点：一个 SOTA 语言模型的「模型流程」（model flow）——而不仅是最终权重——应当透明可访问。Olmo 3 用「全开放」来支撑这一主张：发布 Dolma 3（预训练/中期/长上下文数据）、Dolci（后训练数据）、OlmoRL 代码、全部中间检查点。
  • 贡献递进（正文 §1–§6）：
    1. 基座（§3）：Olmo 3 Base 靠 Dolma 3 数据课程（预训练 5.9T → 中期 100B → 长上下文 50/100B）成为最强全开放基座；关键创新是 OlmoBaseEval 评测套件、约束数据混合、质量感知上采样。
    2. 思考模型（§4）：Olmo 3 Think 用 SFT→DPO→RLVR 三阶段 + OlmoRL（GRPO 改进，见 E001/E002）成为最强全开放思考模型；核心发现是「DPO 的 delta 学习对比信号优于继续 SFT」。
    3. 指令模型（§5）：Olmo 3 Instruct 用多轮 DPO + 长度控制实现简洁、可用的函数调用能力。
    4. RL-Zero（§6）：从基座直接 RLVR 的基准，验证去污染（负对照实验，图 27）。
  • 空白/差距信号词（本文的「However / limitation」）：§2.4 承认后训练理论不成熟需反复实验；§4.1.2 承认 7B 在知识任务上落后 Qwen 3（归因于蒸馏）；§4.4.3 承认 32B learner 分片配置非最优。
  • 图表速查：图 2（全流程总览）、图 15（后训练数据流水线）、图 16（验证器设计）、图 17（OlmoRL 架构）、图 24（RL-Zero 各域）；表 1/14/15（Think 结果）、表 2/3（Base 结果）、表 25/26（Instruct 结果）。
  • 公式：全文仅 2 个编号公式（E001 OlmoRL 目标、E002 优势函数）+ 1 个 DPO 损失（E003）；技术深度集中在算法而非数学。

局限与可信度说明（配合 translation_notes.md 阅读）：

  • 本文为 arXiv 预印本（v2），未经同行评审。
  • 宽表（基准结果、安全评测）以「图像裁剪 + 关键数字」呈现；精确数值请对照 PDF 原表。
  • 部分图裁剪为近似（多图同页时裁剪框可能含邻图）；已在 translation_notes.md 标注。

    20. 1.20. 中文: 大规模杀伤性武器代理（WMDP）（Li et al., 2024b）评估模型能否回答生物、化学与网络安全领域的双用途知识问题。每个提示词都是与大规模杀伤性武器相关的事实性多项选择题，有四个答案选项与一个正确答案。QA 偏差基准（BBQ）（Parrish et al., 2022）通过向模型呈现针对年龄、性别、种族、宗教与交叉身份等社会维度的多项选择题，衡量偏差与刻板印象遵循。模型响应使用基于正则的字符串解析器，评测准确率（正确答案比例）与偏差。
    21. 1.21. 阅读提示 / Critical Reading Notes
